Neighbouring registrations in the same file are explicit about it (UpdateBuilderFactory `.SingleInstance()` at line 120, CursorService `.SingleInstance()` at CoreModule.cs:41), so the omission reads as an oversight rather than a decision. Driver 2.28.0 (Directory.Packages.props:47) does share the underlying cluster via ClusterRegistry keyed on settings, and the ClusterConfigurator lambda at line 110 captures nothing so Roslyn caches it — which is the only reason this is not already a connection-pool explosion. Separately, SecurityAuditRepository writes to the SecurityAuditLog collection (line 51-52) and queries it by (UserId, TimestampUtc) at lines 60-63, 85-89, 110-114, 135-139; docker/mongo-init.js declares no index on that collection or on LoginAttempts, and nothing ever prunes either.

Последствие: The MongoClient registration is one closure-capture away from becoming a new cluster and a new connection pool per resolution — a latent, hard-to-diagnose production failure with no test guarding it. The audit collections grow without bound, are scanned linearly on every profile-security page load, and retain IP addresses and user agents indefinitely with no retention policy.

Исправление: Add `.SingleInstance()` to the DmMongoClient registration — the driver is thread-safe and designed to be a singleton. Add `{UserId:1, TimestampUtc:-1}` indexes plus a TTL index on TimestampUtc for both SecurityAuditLog and LoginAttempts, with the retention window chosen deliberately rather than by omission.

### [НИЗКАЯ] PG-12 — Two dead verbatim copies of Mongo-touching background services, and two dead-code defects in the indexing repository

**Статус: Исправлено.** Шесть мутирующих вызовов OpenSearch перестали глотать ответ, отклоненная запись больше не выглядит успешной; неработоспособная перегрузка без вызовов удалена. Половина находки про мертвые копии фоновых служб была закрыта ранее (00f65939)

src/DM.Web.API/Shared/BackgroundServices/SessionCleanupService.cs and src/DM.Web.API/HostedServices/SessionCleanupService.cs differ only by their namespace declaration (diff -u shows a single changed line); the same holds for Warmup/WarmupService.cs vs HostedServices/WarmupService.cs. Startup.cs:32 imports DM.Web.API.HostedServices and registers the unqualified names at lines 156-158, so the Shared/BackgroundServices and Warmup copies are unreachable. Separately, IndexingRepository.cs:35-40 `UpdateByParent(Guid, IEnumerable<Guid>)` emits `ctx._source.authorizedUsers = [3fa85f64-5717-...]` — GUIDs interpolated into a Painless script without quotes, which is not valid Painless; the overload has no callers (only the UserRole overload is used, TopicChangedIndexer.cs:45). None of the OpenSearch write calls in that file check the response's IsValid, unlike the read path which does (SearchEngineRepository.cs:81-86).

Последствие: Two forked copies of the session-expiry logic that touches Mongo means the next fix has a 50% chance of landing in the dead one. The unquoted-GUID script would fail at runtime the day someone wires up per-user access reindexing. Silent OpenSearch write failures are indistinguishable from success — CompositeIndexer.cs:35 returns ProcessResult.Success regardless, so a rejected bulk index is invisible.

Исправление: Delete Shared/BackgroundServices/SessionCleanupService.cs and Warmup/WarmupService.cs. Delete the unused UpdateByParent(Guid, IEnumerable<Guid>) overload, or quote the GUIDs and use a parameterized script. Check `.IsValid` on IndexManyAsync/DeleteAsync/DeleteByQueryAsync and throw so the retry middleware (SearchConsumerRetryMiddleware.cs:18) can actually see the failure.

### [НИЗКАЯ] PG-13 — NullReferenceException in UnreadCountersRepository.FlushAsync when no counter document exists

**Статус: Исправлено.** Guard на отсутствующий счетчик вместо NRE (81198dbc)

UnreadCountersRepository.cs:178-192: `var counter = await Collection.Find(...).FirstOrDefaultAsync();` then, with no null check, `ParentId = counter.ParentId` inside the replacement document. FirstOrDefaultAsync returns null when no counter row exists for that (EntityId, EntryType) pair — which is the normal state for any entity created before the counter was introduced, or one whose counters were removed by DeleteAsync:87-93 (which sets IsRemoved=true, a state this Find does not exclude, so the null case is narrower than it looks but still reachable for entities that never had counters).

Последствие: Marking an entity as read throws a 500 instead of being a no-op. The failure is per-entity and silent until a user hits the specific topic or room.

Исправление: Guard the null (`if (counter == null) return;` or fall back to `entityId` as ParentId, matching the CreateAsync(entityId, entryType) convention at line 56).


---

# Дизайн HTTP API — C-

Route/verb/status discipline and auth coverage across 374 endpoints are genuinely good, but the surface is undermined by two wiring bugs that silently break production behaviour (all per-endpoint rate limits inert; a duplicate route that 500s a shipped moderator screen), an error model that is documented one way, advertised a second way in ~905 OpenAPI attributes, and emitted a third way at runtime, plus PATCH semantics that silently destroy user data.

## Что сделано хорошо

- DELETE semantics are genuinely disciplined: 77 of 78 DELETE actions declare 204 and return NoContent(), and the single deviation (DELETE /v1/polls/{id}/vote -> 200 + Envelope<Poll>, PollController.cs:146) is explicitly recorded with a rationale in docs/conventions/API_DESIGN.md:82-83. Very few codebases keep a deviation ledger that actually matches the code.
- Auth coverage on mutating endpoints is strong: of 222 POST/PUT/PATCH/DELETE actions only 11 carry no [AuthenticationRequired]/[RequireRole], and 8 of those are legitimately anonymous flows (login, register, activation, activation resend, recovery, password reset, ticket intake, mirror transfer accept). The two attributes themselves are correctly built — RequireRoleAttribute.cs:32-59 distinguishes 401 (unauthenticated) from 403 (insufficient role) instead of collapsing both, and both run as IAuthorizationFilter so auth failures are not masked by model-binding errors, with the reasoning written down at AuthenticationRequiredAttribute.cs:19-22.
- Collection response shape is near-uniform: 73 GET actions declare ListEnvelope<T>, 4 declare CursorEnvelope<T>, and the only bare-collection outlier is the dev-only GET /v1/moderation/users (ModerationController.cs:97). Pagination is also bounded by construction — PagingQuery.cs:23 caps take at [Range(1,100)] and [ApiController] auto-rejects, so there is no unbounded-page DoS vector on any list endpoint.
- The documented "semantic shortcut" exceptions in API_DESIGN.md:155-158 all actually exist and match: GET /v1/users/by-role/{role} (UserController.cs:47), /v1/users/{username}/best-publication (PublicationController.cs:81), /best-topic (TopicController.cs:118), /bans/active (BanController.cs:68). The doc is describing the real system, not an aspiration.
- X-Dm-Audience as a rendering-negotiation request header (Shared/BbRendering/BbConverter.cs:98-102) is the right shape for a server-renders-BBCode constraint — it keeps audience out of the URL space so it does not fragment routes, and BbAudienceSwaggerFilter.cs:20 injects it into the OpenAPI operation parameters so it is discoverable rather than tribal knowledge.
- The rate-limit *policies* themselves are well designed even though the wiring is broken: per-authenticated-user partition keys with IP fallback (Startup.cs:259-261), a sliding window specifically for the expensive tsvector search path with the cost rationale written down (Startup.cs:269-284), and OnRejected emits Retry-After (Startup.cs:305-317). This is design work someone actually thought about.
- [ProducesResponseType] density is high — 1286 attributes across 374 actions (avg 3.4) — and status-code coverage per action (401/403/404/400 where applicable) is consistent. The problem is the declared *types*, not missing declarations.

## Подтвержденные находки

### [ВЫСОКАЯ] API-01 — UseRateLimiter() is registered before UseRouting(), making every [EnableRateLimiting] attribute a no-op

**Статус: Исправлено.** UseRateLimiter после UseRouting (304e618a)

**Доказательство.** src/DM.Web.API/Startup.cs:431-432 orders `.UseRateLimiter()` then `.UseRouting()`. RateLimitingMiddleware resolves the per-endpoint policy from `context.GetEndpoint()?.Metadata`, which is only populated by UseRouting — so at that point the endpoint is null and only `options.GlobalLimiter` (Startup.cs:210-218, 100 req/min per IP) ever runs. All 18 [EnableRateLimiting] attributes are therefore dead: AuthenticationController.cs:25, CredentialsController.cs:28, RecoveryController.cs:28, RegistrationController.cs:29, DeactivationController.cs:23 ("auth", 5/min), AvailabilityController.cs:47/:71 ("email-check" 10/min, "username-check" 20/min), UploadController.cs:127 ("uploads" 10/min), ChatController.cs:172 and MessageSearchController.cs:64 ("sliding" 30/min). Corroborating proof that the metadata is never read: six controllers reference `[EnableRateLimiting("default")]` (BlacklistController.

**Последствие.** Every documented per-endpoint limit in API_DESIGN.md:167-172 is fiction. POST /v1/account/login, /v1/account/recovery, /v1/account/register and /v1/account/email-change are gated at 100 req/min per IP instead of 5 — a 20x wider credential-stuffing and account-enumeration window. POST /v1/uploads accepts 100 image-processing requests/min per IP instead of 10 (a CPU/S3 cost amplifier, and the anti-flood rationale written at Startup.cs:253-256 does not hold). GET /v1/search runs 100 heavy tsvector scans/min against the primary OLTP database instead of 30. Additionally, the moment someone correctly moves UseRateLimiter after UseRouting, the six "default" controllers begin throwing 500 on every request — so the naive fix detonates six user-facing feature areas (own profile, preferences, blacklist, invitations, subscriptions).

**Исправление.** Move `.UseRateLimiter()` to after `.UseRouting()` in Startup.cs (between line 432 and 433), and in the same change either register a "default" policy in both the enabled and disabled branches of AddRateLimiter, or delete the six `[EnableRateLimiting("default")]` attributes. Add one integration test that asserts a 429 on the 6th POST to /v1/account/login — there is currently zero test coverage for rate limiting anywhere under tests/.

**Проверка оппонентом.** I tried to refute this and could not — it survives, and I proved the mechanism empirically rather than by argument.

FACTS VERIFIED IN THE REPO
- D:\Code Projects\dm3\src\DM.Web.API\Startup.cs:431-432 does order `.UseRateLimiter()` then `.UseRouting()`, exactly as claimed.
- D:\Code Projects\dm3\src\DM.Web.API\Program.cs:36-39 uses `Host.CreateDefaultBuilder(...).ConfigureWebHostDefaults(w => w.UseDefault<Startup>())` — the classic generic-host/Startup model. This kills the most promising refutation: `WebApplication` (minimal hosting) auto-inserts routing at the head of the pipeline, which would have made the ordering harmless. That auto-insertion does not exist here, so the written order is the actual order.

### [ВЫСОКАЯ] API-02 — Two controllers register the identical route GET /v1/moderation/users/{x}/profile — guaranteed AmbiguousMatchException on an endpoint the client calls

**Статус: Исправлено.** Дублирующий контроллер удален, было 500 стало 401 (304e618a)

**Доказательство.** ModerationProfileController.cs:24 declares [Route("v1/moderation/users")] with [HttpGet("{login}/profile")] at line 52; Moderation/Profiles/ProfileController.cs:25 declares the same [Route("v1/moderation/users")] with [HttpGet("{username}/profile")] at line 53. Different parameter names produce identical route templates and identical precedence, so ASP.NET Core endpoint routing throws AmbiguousMatchException. The same collision exists for PATCH: ProfileController.cs:75 ([HttpPatch("{username}/profile")] under v1/moderation/users) versus ModerationController.cs:124 ([HttpPatch("users/{login}/profile")] under [Route("v1/moderation")] at line 28) — the latter is even named ModerateUserProfileLegacy. A scripted sweep of all 374 actions found exactly these two duplicate groups and no others. The frontend actively calls the broken GET: src/DM.Web.Client/src/shared/api/moderationApi.ts:199 issu

**Последствие.** The moderator profile screen returns 500 on every load. The exception is caught by ErrorHandlingMiddleware (Startup.cs:423) and its message is placed in the ProblemDetails title (see API-06), so the client also gets the internal route-conflict text. Beyond the outage, the two controllers return incompatible bodies for the same URL — Envelope<ModerationProfileDto> (ModerationProfileController.cs:54) versus a bare ModeratedProfile (ProfileController.cs:55) — so there is no single answer to "what does this endpoint return".

**Исправление.** Delete ModerationProfileController and IModerationProfileApiService (ProfileController is the one the client is typed against), and delete ModerateUserProfileLegacy from ModerationController.cs:124-131 in favour of ProfileController.cs:75. Then add a startup smoke assertion that enumerates EndpointDataSource and fails on duplicate (method, RoutePattern.RawText) pairs — this class of bug is invisible until a request arrives, and the [Route("v1")] / [Route("v1/moderation")] bare-prefix style (API-08) makes it easy to reintroduce.

**Проверка оппонентом.** The route collision is real and I reproduced the runtime failure rather than inferring it. Cited code holds verbatim: ModerationProfileController.cs:24 [Route("v1/moderation/users")] + :52 [HttpGet("{login}/profile")] and ProfileController.cs:25 [Route("v1/moderation/users")] + :53 [HttpGet("{username}/profile")] produce identical templates with identical inbound precedence; the PATCH pair is also real (ModerationController.cs:28 [Route("v1/moderation")] + :124 [HttpPatch("users/{login}/profile")] vs ProfileController.cs:75). I closed every refutation avenue: DM.Web.API.csproj has no Compile Remove, so both files build; a grep of src/DM.Web.API for IApplicationModelConvention / IControllerModelConvention / ControllerFeatureProvider / ApplicationPart returns nothing but MapControllers() at Startup.cs:438, so no convention rewrites or filters either controller; no EndpointDataSource dedup 

### [ВЫСОКАЯ] API-03 — Webhook endpoint fails open when its secret is unconfigured, and carries the secret in the URL path

**Статус: Частично.** Fail-open закрыт; секрет по-прежнему в пути URL (24a43d5e)

**Доказательство.** WebhookController.cs:57 exposes an unauthenticated `[HttpPost("{type}/{secret}")]`. Line 73: `if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(expectedSecret) && secret != expectedSecret) return 403;` — when BotConfiguration.TelegramWebhookSecret/DiscordWebhookSecret is empty the guard is skipped entirely, with the comment at line 72 "In dev mode (no secret configured), accept any request". Nothing in Startup.cs makes that a Development-only condition: BotConfiguration is bound at Startup.cs:84 with no `.Validate(...).ValidateOnStart()`, unlike ConnectionStrings/IntegrationSettings/RabbitMqConfiguration which do get fail-fast validation at Startup.cs:91-103. The payload reaches TelegramWebhookHandler.cs:58 `_botLinkService.VerifyAndLink(code, BotType, chatIdStr)`, which binds an attacker-supplied chatId to a DM account. Line 93 `catch { return Ok(); }` swallows every processing failure. The comparison at line 

**Последствие.** A missing or misspelled env var in production silently converts an account-linking endpoint into an unauthenticated one, reachable by anyone who knows the path. Combined with API-01 (per-endpoint rate limiting inert; this controller has no [EnableRateLimiting] at all) an attacker can brute-force link codes at the global 100/min per IP with zero authentication and no failure signal, since line 93 returns 200 on every error. Separately, putting the secret in the path means it is written verbatim into every reverse-proxy access log, Serilog request log and APM trace — rotating it requires log purging, not just a config change.

**Исправление.** Fail closed: if the configured secret is null/empty, return 403 unconditionally. Add BotConfiguration to the ValidateOnStart block at Startup.cs:91-103 so the container refuses to boot without it. Move the secret out of the path into a header (X-Telegram-Bot-Api-Secret-Token is Telegram's own mechanism; Discord signs with Ed25519) and compare with CryptographicOperations.FixedTimeEquals. Keep the 200-on-error behaviour but log at Error, not Warning.

**Проверка оппонентом.** Every cited line checks out and no compensating control exists. WebhookController.cs:57 is an anonymous state-changing POST — there is no global fallback authorization policy in Startup.cs (no AddAuthorization/FallbackPolicy anywhere in the file), and CsrfProtectionMiddleware.cs:65-69 deliberately passes through requests with no Origin/Referer (curl), so nothing upstream blocks it. WebhookController.cs:73 really is fail-open (`!string.IsNullOrEmpty(expectedSecret) && ...`), and it is deliberate-and-documented as such in BotConfiguration.cs:42-43,49-50 ("If not set, any secret is accepted (dev mode)") with no IHostEnvironment check anywhere. Startup.cs:84 binds BotConfiguration with plain .Configure while Startup.cs:91-103 gives ValidateOnStart only to ConnectionStrings/IntegrationSettings/RabbitMq. The finding actually understates one point: appsettings.json:59-62 and appsettings.templat

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] API-04 — Three live error models plus a fourth documented-but-dead one; the OpenAPI contract is wrong on ~905 declared error responses

**Статус: Частично.** Мертвая четвертая модель ошибок удалена, документация приведена к реальности, две выбивающиеся формы унифицированы (7640b6b6). Остаются прямые возвраты GeneralError из четырех контроллеров и ~905 объявлений в OpenAPI

**Доказательство.** docs/conventions/API_DESIGN.md:48,58 specifies `ErrorEnvelope` as THE error format, shaped `{ "errors": [{code, message}] }`. Shared/Dto/ErrorEnvelope.cs:10 exists but is never instantiated anywhere in src/ — and its Errors property is a single ApiError, not an array, so it does not even match its own doc. Shared/Dto/ApiError.cs:54-87 (ErrorCodes) is likewise zero-referenced. Meanwhile controllers declare `GeneralError` 798 times and `BadRequestError` 107 times in [ProducesResponseType], but those DTOs are constructed in exactly four places repo-wide (RegistrationController.cs:68,102; TicketIntakeController.cs:57; BanController.cs:76). What actually goes on the wire: ErrorHandlingMiddleware.cs:77-81 always writes ProblemDetails/ValidationProblemDetails as application/problem+json; RequireRoleAttribute.cs:34-43 and :49-58 and AuthenticationRequiredAttribute.cs:31-40 write an anonymous `{t

**Последствие.** There is no error contract. Every one of the ~798 declared 401/403/404 responses is factually wrong (client receives `{type,title,status}`, spec promises `{message}`), and every one of the ~107 declared 400s is wrong (client receives ValidationProblemDetails `{errors:{field:[...]}}`, spec promises `{message,invalidProperties}`). Any generated client, contract test or third-party integration built from the spec breaks on first error. The doc actively misleads a new developer toward a class that does not run. The client already pays for this with union-typed error handling.

**Исправление.** Pick ProblemDetails — it is what already ships, it is RFC 9457, and the middleware and content-type are already correct. Then: delete ErrorEnvelope.cs, ApiError.cs, GeneralError.cs and BadRequestError.cs; replace the four manual BadRequest/NotFound(new ...Error(...)) call sites with HttpException throws so they route through the middleware; replace the anonymous objects in RequireRoleAttribute/AuthenticationRequiredAttribute/the 429 handler with problemDetailsFactory.CreateProblemDetails so traceId is present everywhere; global-replace typeof(GeneralError)->typeof(ProblemDetails) and typeof(BadRequestError)->typeof(ValidationProblemDetails) in the 905 attributes; rewrite API_DESIGN.md:48-58 to describe ProblemDetails.

**Проверка оппонентом.** Could not refute — every cited line, path and count verified exactly. ErrorEnvelope.cs:29 declares `public ApiError Errors` (a single object) while API_DESIGN.md:57-58 documents an array, so the class contradicts its own spec; grep across src/ returns zero references to ErrorEnvelope, ApiError or ErrorCodes outside their own definition files. Counts are exact, not approximate: typeof(GeneralError)=798 (272x404, 258x401, 201x403, 42x409, 19x429, 4x410), typeof(BadRequestError)=107, against exactly four construction sites (RegistrationController.cs:68,102; TicketIntakeController.cs:57; BanController.cs:76). ErrorHandlingMiddleware.cs:77-81 unconditionally writes ProblemDetails as application/problem+json, and ExceptionProblemDetailsFactoryExtensions.cs:15 passes the exception message as CreateProblemDetails' third positional arg, which is `title` — so the wire body is {type,title,status,tr

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] API-06 — Unhandled exceptions return the raw exception message as the ProblemDetails title

**Статус: Исправлено.** Постоянный заголовок 500 + удалена мертвая ветка 501, 2 теста red-green (2107db2b)

**Доказательство.** Middleware/ExceptionProblemDetailsFactoryExtensions.cs:62-65: `factory.CreateProblemDetails(httpContext, StatusCodes.Status500InternalServerError, exception.Message, detail: $"Server error... token: {correlationId}")`. The third positional parameter of ProblemDetailsFactory.CreateProblemDetails is `title`, so `exception.Message` is serialised straight to the client. ErrorHandlingMiddleware.cs:71-74 routes every unrecognised exception through this path. The same pattern appears for HttpException at line 15 and NotImplementedException at line 57-60 — the latter surfacing internal "not implemented" text as a 501 title.

**Последствие.** Npgsql/EF exception messages (which embed table, column and constraint names, and can embed connection detail), MinIO/S3 errors, and file paths from IO exceptions are returned verbatim to unauthenticated callers. The correlationId in `detail` shows the author intended the message to stay server-side — the leak is an argument-position slip, not a decision. It also makes API-02 self-documenting for an attacker: the AmbiguousMatchException text names both conflicting controllers.

**Исправление.** Change line 64 to pass a constant title ("Internal Server Error") and keep the correlation token in `detail`; the real message is already logged at LogCritical (ErrorHandlingMiddleware.cs:72). Audit the HttpException path at line 15 too — domain-authored messages are intentional there, but the same call shape means any framework exception wrapped as HttpException leaks.

**Проверка оппонентом.** MECHANISM CONFIRMED — I could not refute it on any of the usual grounds.

1. The code says what the finding claims. src/DM.Web.API/Middleware/ExceptionProblemDetailsFactoryExtensions.cs:62-65 is verbatim `factory.CreateProblemDetails(httpContext, StatusCodes.Status500InternalServerError, exception.Message, detail: $"Server error. Address the administration ... token: {correlationId}")`. `ProblemDetailsFactory.CreateProblemDetails(HttpContext, int? statusCode, string? title, string? type, string? detail, string? instance)` — the third positional argument is `title`, so `exception.Message` lands in `ProblemDetails.Title`. Same shape at :56-60 for NotImplementedException (501) and :13-15 for HttpException.

2. No guard downstream. There is no custom ProblemDetailsFactory anywhere in the repo — `grep -rn "ProblemDetailsFactory"` over src/ returns only the two middleware files and nothing in 

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] API-07 — PATCH endpoints take the full response DTO as the request body, so partial updates silently reset omitted fields

**Статус: Исправлено.** Проверка нашла больше, чем находка: из 14 названных мест реально теряют данные шесть, и одно из них живое. Закрыто разделением модели чтения и модели записи там, где они разошлись по смыслу. (1) Настройки блек-листа: шесть non-nullable флагов сворачивались в один enum целиком, поэтому запрос с одним флагом снимал остальные пять - введен UpdateBlacklistSettingsRequest, складывается на текущее значение. (2) Настройки отображения: PATCH с одной темой сбрасывал все пять размеров страниц в 10, потому что Paging конструировался по умолчанию - UpdatePreferencesRequest с nullable-полями и слияние с текущими. (3) Схема атрибутов: инициализатор Specifications = [] означал для репозитория "удалить все спецификации", а non-nullable Type подставлял Public, то есть приватная схема публиковалась - UpdateAttributeSchemaRequest. (4) Пост: MetagameText записывался всегда, поэтому правка игрового текста стирала внеигровой, а пропуск игрового текста давал NRE - теперь null означает "не менять", пустая строка "очистить". (5) Персонаж: MapFrom возвращал non-nullable bool, из-за чего PATCH без блока privacy разжаловал NPC в игрового персонажа. (6) Игра: ClearClosedUtc выводился из ClosedUtc == null, а API это поле вообще не присылает - любая правка закрытой игры стирала дату закрытия; теперь флаг ставит только переход статуса. (7) Тема форума: живая поломка - клиент шлет ровно {isAttached} или {isClosed}, инициализатор Title = string.Empty превращал это в "поставить пустой заголовок" и валидатор отвечал 400, то есть закрепление и закрытие темы из интерфейса не работали. Введен UpdateTopicRequest, добавлен интеграционный тест на частичный PATCH. Попутно исправлено отображение BoardTitle из Board.Id: сравнение с названием доски никогда не совпадало, и любая правка с блоком доски выглядела как перенос. Мертвый легаси-блокнот игры (GET отдавал null, PATCH кидал InvalidOperationException, оба объявлены в OpenAPI рабочими) удален целиком

**Доказательство.** 14 PATCH/PUT actions bind the read model as the write model: PreferencesController.cs:65 ([FromBody] Preferences), BlacklistController.cs:77 (BlacklistSettings), GameController.cs:265 (GameDetails) and :312 (GameNotes), PostController.cs:100 (Post), TopicController.cs:232 (Topic), RoomController.cs:184 (RoomAccess), AttributeSchemaController.cs:92 (AttributeSchema), CharacterController.cs:133 (CharacterDetails), and the four comment controllers ([FromBody] Comment at BlogCommentController.cs:175, PublicationCommentController.cs:162, TopicCommentController.cs:152, GameCommentController.cs:131). Concrete data loss: PreferencesController.cs:57-59 documents "All fields are optional - only provided fields will be updated", but Preferences (PreferencesDtos.cs:16-27) has non-nullable `Theme` and a `Paging` that default-constructs with all five page sizes = 10 (PreferencesDtos.cs:43-63), and Pre

**Последствие.** A user who changes only their theme silently loses their pagination preferences — exactly the bug the endpoint's own remarks promise cannot happen. The same shape means read-only fields (id, createdUtc, author, computed counters) appear as writable in the OpenAPI request schema for all 14 endpoints, and the server must silently discard them, which is indistinguishable from accepting them. Every one of these is a latent "my settings reset themselves" support ticket.

**Исправление.** Introduce dedicated Update*Request DTOs whose scalar fields are Optional<T> (or at minimum nullable) for these 14 endpoints, and have the API services apply only set members. Start with PreferencesController/PreferencesApiService — it is the one demonstrably losing data today. Room.cs is already the reference implementation.

**Проверка оппонентом.** The core mechanism holds, verified end-to-end, but the breadth and impact are overstated so the severity drops to medium.\n\nWHAT SURVIVES: PreferencesDtos.cs:21,26 — Theme is a non-nullable enum (Theme.Light = 0, Enums/Theme.cs:14) and Paging is property-initialized to new() with all five values = 10 (:43-63), so neither can bind as \"absent\". PreferencesMappingProfile.cs:31 maps Paging -> PagingSettings verbatim with no guard on the write direction (the >0 guards at :25-29 are read-direction only). UserService.cs:231-240 unconditionally wraps all six values in Optional<T>.WithValue, and UserRepository.cs:371-382 emits a Mongo $set for every Optional carrying a value. So PATCH {\"theme\":\"Dark\"} really does write PostsPerPage..EntitiesPerPage = 10, and symmetrically a paging-only PATCH resets Theme to Light. The remarks at PreferencesController.cs:55 (\"only provided fields will be u

- **API-05** — GET /v1/forum marks per-user data `public, max-age=60` while the identical GET /v1/boards marks it `private` — cross-user leak through the server response cache. Причина отклонения: The quoted lines are accurate (ForumController.cs:60 `public, max-age=60` vs BoardController.cs:66-69 `[ResponseCache(Location=Client)]`, both returning `_boardApiService.GetBoards()`; Startup.cs:123/420 wire response caching; no Vary anywhere), but the claimed impact — "the first user to hit GET /v1/forum has their unread counters served to every other user" — cannot occur, because an authenticat

## Средние и низкие

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] API-08 — OpenAPI declares bare DTOs for endpoints that actually return Envelope<T>, and 31% of single-resource responses are un-enveloped against the doc's own rule

**Статус: Исправлено.** Находка недооценила последствия: расхождение атрибута с фактическим типом уже протекло в рантайм тремя живыми багами, и ни один тест форму ответа не проверял. Пять списочных эндпоинтов объявляли ListEnvelope, а клали на провод голый массив - из-за этого страница уведомлений и все списки подписок рендерились пустыми всегда (клиент читал .resources у массива). Пятнадцать одиночных ресурсов объявляли голый DTO, а возвращали Envelope - из-за этого правка записи блокнота стирала строку списка (клиент присваивал конверт в элемент). Серверная сторона: пять тел обернуты в ListEnvelope, пятнадцать атрибутов приведены к Envelope. Клиент: типы updateNote в blogApi/gameApi и трех методов notepadApi приведены к Envelope, три страницы разворачивают .resource. Двойной ProducesResponseType на одном 200 у GetGames снят - две схемы на один статус в OpenAPI невыразимы, ref-проекция описана в remarks. Добавлены 4 интеграционных теста на форму ответа. Оставшиеся честно-голые эндпоинты не тронуты: они попадают под legacy-оговорку API_DESIGN.md, приведение всех к конверту - отдельная задача с правкой клиента

API_DESIGN.md:61-62 states Envelope<T> is the standard for all single resources and calls bare ones "legacy". 89 of 286 declared 200/201 responses are bare. Worse, a subset are outright false: BlogNotepadController.cs:86 and :104 declare `typeof(NotepadEntryResponse)` while IBlogNotepadApiService.cs:21 and :31 return Envelope<NotepadEntryResponse>; the same mismatch repeats at GameNotepadController.cs:86,:104 and CharacterController.cs:205,:224 against IGameNotepadApiService.cs:56,:61. BlogCommentController.cs:115,:143,:170 declare `typeof(Comment)` while IBlogCommentApiService.cs:38,:45,:53 all return Envelope<Comment>. Also GameCommentController.cs:89, Moderation/Profiles/ProfileController.cs:75, NotepadController.cs:48. The inconsistency is not a legacy tail — it splits the same DTO across sibling features: TopicCommentController declares Envelope<Comment> at :101,:126,:147 while BlogCommentController declares bare Comment for the identical type. TagController is bare for single resources (:57,:75,:97,:172,:191,:214) but ListEnvelope for collections (ITagApiService.cs:16,:41). GameController.cs:51-52 declares two different types for the same 200 status (ListEnvelope<Game> and ListEnvelope<GameRef>, switched by `?projection=ref` at line 60) — Swashbuckle keeps one and drops the other.

Последствие: A client generated from the spec unwraps `.resource` on notepad and blog-comment endpoints that the spec says are bare, and fails to unwrap on the game-list ref projection whose schema was silently dropped. There is no mechanical way to tell which of the 89 bare declarations are true and which are lies without reading each service interface. For a consumer, "is there an envelope" has to be answered per endpoint by trial.

Исправление: Two passes. (1) Correctness: fix the ~10 [ProducesResponseType] attributes that contradict their service's return type — these are contract bugs, not style. (2) Consistency: pick enveloped-everywhere and migrate the genuinely bare ones (Tags, Preferences, Subscriptions, Chats, Uploads, ProfileNotes, Blacklists, Endorsements, Invitations), or amend API_DESIGN.md:61-62 to say envelopes are for collections only. For GameController.GetGames, either give the ref projection its own route or return a single schema with optional fields — two 200 declarations on one action cannot be expressed in OpenAPI.

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] API-09 — Public-id/GUID duality is re-implemented inline in 31 places and costs a full aggregate load per request

**Статус: Открыто.** 

GameController.cs:134-136, :167-169, :226-228, :267-269, :289-291, :314-316 each repeat `var gameId = Guid.TryParse(id, out var guid) ? guid : (await _gameApiService.GetByPublicId(id)).Resource.Id;` — six copies in one file. GameApiService.cs:59-63 shows what that costs: a full `_gameService.GetByPublicIdAsync` plus an AutoMapper projection to the complete Game DTO (which per GameController.cs:40-42 carries players/readers arrays), all to extract one Guid that is then discarded. The pattern recurs 31 times across 19 controllers; BlogNotepadController.cs:37-38 at least factored it into a private ResolveBlogId helper, GameController did not. Compounding this, there is a third accepted id representation nobody documents: ReadableGuidBinderProvider.cs:19 installs ReadableGuidBinder globally (Startup.cs:350) for every scalar Guid/Guid? route and query parameter, accepting Base64 URL-safe GUIDs — while OpenAPI reports all of them as plain `format: uuid`. (ReadableGuidBinder.cs:25 also contains a dead branch: `bindingContext.ModelType == typeof(Optional<>)` can never be true for a closed generic, and the provider only ever returns this binder for Guid/Guid?.)

Последствие: DELETE /v1/games/{publicId} performs a read of the entire game aggregate before deleting it; PATCH /v1/games/{publicId}/notes does the same before touching a text field. That is an avoidable query plus mapping on every alias-addressed request on the hottest resource in the product. The duplication also means the alias-resolution *policy* is 31 independent decisions — GameController.cs:199-203 documents that PostGamePremoderation must deliberately NOT use the read-gated resolver, a subtlety that is one copy-paste away from being lost. And the API silently accepts three id encodings per route with only one documented.

Исправление: Add a route constraint plus a model binder (e.g. `{id:gameref}` binding to a GameRef struct) that resolves public-id -> Guid once, ungated, in the binding layer — the ReadableGuidBinderProvider infrastructure at Shared/Binding/ is already the right place. Delete the 31 inline resolutions. Document the accepted id formats in API_DESIGN.md and delete the dead Optional<> branch at ReadableGuidBinder.cs:25-38.

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] API-10 — "v1" is a string literal repeated in 79 route attributes, not a versioning strategy

**Статус: Открыто.** 

Every controller hardcodes its version into the route prefix ([Route("v1/games")] GameController.cs:19, [Route("v1/account")] AuthenticationController.cs:22, and so on across 79 files). There is no Asp.Versioning package in src/DM.Web.API/DM.Web.API.csproj (lines 30-32 show only Swashbuckle) and no [ApiVersion] attribute anywhere in src/. SwaggerExtensions.cs:41 hardcodes `Version = "v1"` into each of the 9 per-group OpenApiInfo blocks. There is no deprecation mechanism, no version negotiation, no sunset header, and no way to run v1 and v2 of one endpoint side by side. Compounding it, Startup.cs:411-416 serves Swagger only in Development, so the contract is never published from a running production system — and it is 9 separate documents (one per ApiExplorerSettings group), never one unified spec.

Последствие: The prefix buys nothing it appears to promise. A genuine breaking change requires either editing 79 route attributes or forking every controller. Because there is no per-operation version metadata, there is also no way to deprecate a single endpoint — the granularity is the whole surface. Consumers cannot fetch a machine-readable contract from prod at all, and the nine-document split means no single artifact describes the API.

Исправление: Either accept that the API is single-version and say so explicitly in API_DESIGN.md (removing the implication that v1 is a versioning scheme), or adopt Asp.Versioning.Mvc.ApiExplorer: replace the literal prefixes with [Route("v{version:apiVersion}/games")] + [ApiVersion("1.0")], which gives per-operation deprecation and a generated version-aware spec for free. Independently, emit the OpenAPI document as a build artifact so the contract exists outside a Development container.

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] API-11 — Location header on 201 points at the parent collection rather than the created resource in 15 endpoints

**Статус: Исправлено.** Проверка нашла в находке одно ложное срабатывание и три недосчитанных места. GameController:115 не дефект - GetGameDetails это элементный ресурс созданной игры, и тело ответа объявлено ровно той же формой. Недосчитаны AwardCatalogController:67 и :128 и UserAwardController:58. Итого правок 17: два Location переведены на существующие элементные маршруты (подписка на пользователя -> GET .../subscribers/me, создание бана -> GET .../bans/active), пятнадцать отдают 201 без Location, потому что у подресурса нет собственного адреса и заводить его только ради заголовка незачем. Правило записано в API_DESIGN.md - его там не было вовсе

RFC 9110 requires the 201 Location to identify the created resource. 15 CreatedAtRoute calls name a collection route instead: BlogInvitationController.cs:77,:99 -> GetBlogInvitations; GameInvitationController.cs:79,:101,:123 -> GetGameInvitations; BlogReaderController.cs:82 -> GetBlogReaders; GameReaderController.cs:79 -> GetGameReaders; BlogBlacklistController.cs:84 -> GetBlogBlacklist; GameBlacklistController.cs:85 -> GetBlacklist; UserSubscriberController.cs:64 -> GetUserSubscribers; BoardController.cs:156 -> GetBoardModerators; BanController.cs:151 -> GetUserBans; WarningController.cs:104 -> GetUserWarnings; BlacklistController.cs:99 -> GetMyBlacklist; AchievementCatalogController.cs:85 -> AchievementController.GetAchievementTypes. GameController.cs:115 is a near miss in the other direction — it points POST /v1/games at GetGameDetails (/v1/games/{id}/details) rather than GetGame (/v1/games/{id}).

Последствие: A client that follows Location after creating an invitation or a ban gets the whole collection back, not the new item — so the header cannot be used for the one thing it exists for. For most of these the created item genuinely has no canonical single-item URL (there is no GET /v1/blogs/{id}/invitations/{invitationId}), which means the 201 is being used for resources the API never made individually addressable.

Исправление: For each: either add the missing item-level GET and point Location at it, or stop pretending — return 200 with the created body and no Location, which is honest for a sub-resource with no canonical URL. Do not leave a Location that resolves to a collection. Fix GameController.cs:115 to nameof(GetGame).

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] API-12 — Dev-only endpoints use ASPNETCORE_ENVIRONMENT as their only security boundary, including an unauthenticated role-escalation path

**Статус: Открыто.** 

ModerationController.cs:63-78 exposes POST /v1/moderation/users/me/role/{role} guarded solely by a runtime `if (!_environment.IsDevelopment()) return NotFound();` at line 71 — any authenticated user can assign themselves UserRole.Admin. ModerationController.cs:96-109 (GET /v1/moderation/users, dumps every user and role) and :152-165 / :189-202 (POST /v1/moderation/seed, /seed/comprehensive) carry no [AuthenticationRequired] or [RequireRole] at all — only the same environment check. These are not compiled out; the code ships in the production binary. The seed endpoints also write substantial data (ModerationApiService.cs is 4000+ lines of seeding) and return 200 rather than 201/202.

Последствие: The security boundary is one environment variable. A misconfigured container, a copied compose file, or a staging box promoted to serve real traffic turns POST /v1/moderation/users/me/role/4 into unauthenticated-adjacent full admin, and POST /v1/moderation/seed/comprehensive into an unauthenticated write of thousands of rows against the production database. Because the guard is a runtime branch, no build or deploy step can detect it.

Исправление: Wrap the four dev-only actions in `#if DEBUG` so they physically do not exist in Release binaries, or move them to a separate controller in a project referenced only by the dev compose profile. As a defence in depth, add [RequireRole(UserRole.Admin)] to all four — there is no reason the seed and user-dump endpoints are anonymous even in development.

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] API-13 — Four overlapping pagination models, one of them dead; two are in simultaneous production use with different field sets

**Статус: Исправлено.** Мертвое удалено: CursorPagingInfo (ноль ссылок во всем репозитории), семейство SearchResult/GameSummary/PostSummary/SearchQuery, ValidationRequiredAttribute (не применен ни к одному действию), конструктор Paging(bool,bool) и свойства HasMoreBefore/HasMoreAfter, которые никогда не заполнялись и вырезались из JSON, вместе с их зеркалом в TS-модели. Живые модели оставлены обе: PagingInfo с алиасами Current/Size/Pages/Number читается фронтом в двух десятках мест, CursorPaging ведет личные сообщения, глобальный чат и поиск. Уточнение к находке: утверждение проверяющего, что ValidationRequiredAttribute - единственный источник ValidationException, опровергнуто прямым замером (58 вызовов ValidateAndThrow в доменных сервисах), поэтому ветка middleware сохранена

Shared/Dto/PagingInfo.cs:14-70 (PagingInfo) is the primary model and ships `{skip,take,total}` plus four derived aliases — Current (:54), Size (:59), Pages (:64) and Number (:69, explicitly commented "alias for Current, for compatibility"). Shared/Dto/Paging.cs:8-63 is a second, differently-shaped offset model `{pages,current,size,number,total,hasMoreBefore,hasMoreAfter}` that is live in four comment services (BlogCommentApiService.cs:76, PublicationCommentApiService.cs:58, ForumCommentApiService.cs:75, GameCommentApiService.cs:76) via DiscussionResponse.cs:34. Shared/Dto/CursorEnvelope.cs:32 (CursorPaging) is used by MessagingApiService.cs:90 and MessageSearchApiService.cs:43. Shared/Dto/PagingInfo.cs:78-122 (CursorPagingInfo) is a fourth model with zero references anywhere. API_DESIGN.md:55 documents only `{skip,take,total}`. Related dead code in the same layer: ValidationRequiredAttribute.cs:13 is declared but applied to no action.

Последствие: Two live offset shapes means a client cannot write one pagination component — list endpoints hand back skip/take/total-plus-aliases, discussion endpoints hand back pages/current/size/number without skip or take. The four derived aliases on PagingInfo are pure redundancy that every consumer must decide whether to trust. Dead types (CursorPagingInfo, ValidationRequiredAttribute, and the ErrorEnvelope family from API-04) send a false signal about what the intended design is.

Исправление: Delete CursorPagingInfo and ValidationRequiredAttribute. Migrate DiscussionResponse to PagingInfo and delete Shared/Dto/Paging.cs. Drop Current/Size/Pages/Number from PagingInfo (skip/take/total is sufficient and is what API_DESIGN.md:55 documents) once the frontend no longer reads them.

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] API-14 — Moderator-facing catalog writes accept and persist unvalidated input while advertising a 400 they cannot produce

**Статус: Исправлено.** Каталог достижений и наград получил валидацию, которой не было ни на входе, ни в домене: семь валидаторов FluentValidation по образцу остальных 64 (код, заголовок, порог, ярус 1-4, порядок, номер и год конкурса), подключены первой строкой методов сервиса. Закрыты и объявленные, но недостижимые коды: создание типа достижения с неизвестной категорией давало 500 от нарушения внешнего ключа вместо 400/404; удаление несуществующего типа отвечало 204 вместо 404; деактивация несуществующего типа награды и серии конкурса падала в 500 через FirstAsync вместо 404; создание дубля серии (ContestType, Number) давало 500 от уникального индекса вместо 409. Найдено сверх находки и НЕ закрыто: 13 валидаторов написаны, но не вызываются ниоткуда (CreateTag, UpdateTag, CreateTagGroup, UpdateTagGroup, CreateBan, CreateModeratedProfileNote, UpdateModeratedProfileNote, CreateUserProfileNote, UpdatePoll, OperateBlogBlacklistLink, OperateUserBlacklistLink, CreateChatRoom, UpdateChatRoom) - сервисы их просто не инжектят. То есть из 64 валидаторов реально работает 51, а фронт ссылается на некоторые как на действующие. Подключение изменит наблюдаемое поведение (появятся 400 там, где сейчас проходит) - за владельцем

AchievementCatalogController.cs:74-88 (POST /v1/moderation/achievement-types) declares [ProducesResponseType(typeof(BadRequestError), 400)] at line 76 and documents "400 Unknown category or invalid data" at line 71. The request DTO AchievementDtos.cs:71-83 has no [Required], no [MaxLength], no [Range] — Code and Title default to string.Empty, Threshold and AchievementCategoryId to their zero values, and Tier is documented "(1-4)" at line 79 with no constraint. No AbstractValidator<CreateAchievementType> exists (a repo-wide sweep of the 64 validator types finds Achievement/Award types absent entirely). AchievementService.cs:53-62 only checks code uniqueness before delegating straight to the repository. The same gap covers UpdateAchievementCategoryRequest, UpdateAchievementTypeRequest, CreateAwardTypeRequest, UpdateAwardTypeRequest, CreateContestSeriesRequest, UpdateContestSeriesRequest and GrantUserAwardRequest. Separately AchievementService.cs:73-74 DeleteTypeAsync performs no existence check, so the declared 404 at AchievementCatalogController.cs:122 cannot fire either.

Последствие: POST {} creates an achievement tier with empty code and title, threshold 0 (immediately satisfied by every user) and a Guid.Empty category FK — which surfaces as a 500 from the database rather than the advertised 400. DELETE of a nonexistent id returns 204 instead of 404. The declared error codes are decorative on these endpoints. Note the contrast with the rest of the codebase, which does this correctly: 64 AbstractValidator types cover the domain DTOs behind games, blogs, topics, posts, comments, characters, bans and tickets — the achievement/award catalog is the hole, not the norm.

Исправление: Add AbstractValidator<CreateAchievementType>/<UpdateAchievementType>/<UpdateAchievementCategory> and the four award-catalog equivalents alongside the existing 64, matching the pattern already used by CreateGame/UpdateGame. Add an existence check to AchievementService.DeleteTypeAsync so the declared 404 is real.

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] API-15 — The same logical resource is exposed under three unrelated URL prefixes, and sub-resource nesting follows three incompatible strategies

**Статус: Открыто.** 

WarningController.cs:24 declares [Route("v1")] and then scatters one resource across three prefixes: GET v1/users/{username}/warnings (:49), GET v1/moderation/warnings (:66), POST v1/warnings (:94), DELETE v1/warnings/{id} (:119). Reviews get two opposite treatments: GameReviewController.cs:18 fully nests ([Route("v1/games/{id:guid}/reviews")], item at v1/games/{id}/reviews/{reviewId}), while PostReviewController.cs:22 declares [Route("v1/reviews")] but escapes out of it for every real operation (~/v1/posts/{postId}/reviews at :106,:141,:170), leaving only the odd GET v1/reviews/posts (:63) on the declared base. UserEndorsementController.cs:19 nests the collection under the user but roots the item (/v1/endorsements/{id} at :145,:170,:201). Four controllers declare a meaningless [Route("v1")] base (PublicationController.cs:18, AchievementController.cs:22, AwardController.cs:20, WarningController.cs:24). Route escapes use three syntaxes for the same thing: 24 `~/v1/...` and 4 `/v1/...` alongside relative templates. Two controllers share [Route("v1/search")] across different feature folders (General/Search/SearchController.cs:22 and the top-level Features/Search/MessageSearchController.cs:22, the only controller outside the nine-group folder layout). GameController offers two different mechanisms for the same "how much data" question in one file — ?projection=ref on the list (:60) and a /details sub-path on the item (:238).

Последствие: There is no rule a consumer can infer for where a resource lives: warnings are simultaneously a user sub-resource, a moderation sub-resource and a root resource. The [Route("v1")] bases mean a class attribute no longer tells you which URLs a controller owns — which is precisely how the API-02 collision survived review. Uniform-interface predictability, which API_DESIGN.md:24-27 lists as principles 2 and 3, does not hold.

Исправление: Adopt one sub-resource rule and write it into API_DESIGN.md: collection nested under parent, item at the root (the Publications/Endorsements pattern), and migrate GameReviews to it. Give WarningController a single home (/v1/warnings with ?username= and ?status= filters, per the doc's own one-endpoint-many-scenarios rule at API_DESIGN.md:91) and drop the moderation/user duplicates. Replace the four [Route("v1")] bases with real resource prefixes and eliminate the ~/ escapes. Move Features/Search under Features/General. Pick either ?projection= or /details for game detail depth, not both.

### [НИЗКАЯ] API-16 — No conditional-request support anywhere: zero ETag/Last-Modified across 152 GET endpoints, and cache headers applied through two mechanisms on only 5 of them

**Статус: Открыто.** 

A repo-wide grep for ETag, If-None-Match and Last-Modified finds no occurrence in src/. Of 152 GET actions only five carry any cache directive, split across two mechanisms: the [ResponseCache] attribute at BoardController.cs:69, GameController.cs:74 and PostController.cs:149, versus manual header assignment at BlogController.cs:49, ForumController.cs:60 and GameController.cs:57. The remaining ~147 GETs emit no cache policy at all, so hot reads (topic lists, post feeds, game lists, chat history) revalidate in full every time.

Последствие: Every poll of a board, topic or game list transfers the full payload even when nothing changed. For a forum-shaped product where clients poll for unread counts, that is the dominant bandwidth and serialisation cost, and it is entirely avoidable for the endpoints whose content is genuinely stable. The two-mechanism split also means the caching policy of an endpoint cannot be read off its attributes alone — GameController uses both styles within one file (:57 manual, :74 attribute).

Исправление: Add an ETag action filter that hashes the serialised body and short-circuits to 304 on If-None-Match — one filter covers all 152 GETs with no per-endpoint work, and pairs correctly with the Vary fix from API-05. Standardise on [ResponseCache] and delete the manual Response.Headers.CacheControl assignments (or vice versa), but not both.


---

# Безопасность — D-

The security primitives are genuinely well built (Argon2id at OWASP-2025 parameters, AES-256-GCM with key versioning, a fail-closed intention-based authorization model with real object-level checks, correct PII gating), but the assembled system has four independently exploitable critical defects reachable in the default deployment path — a stored XSS confirmed by execution on every content surface, a publicly-committed session-encryption key combined with session tokens that never bind userId to sessionId, `ASPNETCORE_ENVIRONMENT=Development` as the docker-compose default (which exposes an unauthenticated seed endpoint and a self-promote-to-Admin endpoint), and endpoint rate limiting that never executes because `UseRateLimiter()` is registered before `UseRouting()`.

## Что сделано хорошо

- Password hashing is correct and current, not cargo-culted: `src/DM.Domain.Account/Features/Security/HashProvider.cs:13-42` uses Argon2id at m=19456 KiB / t=2 / p=1 (OWASP 2025 primary recommendation) with a 32-byte output, and `SecurityManager.cs:36` compares with `CryptographicOperations.FixedTimeEquals`. No legacy hash fallback path exists to weaken it.
- The token crypto is AEAD done properly: `src/DM.Domain.Account/Features/Security/AesGcmSymmetricCryptoService.cs:63-97` uses AES-256-GCM with a fresh 12-byte nonce per message, a 16-byte tag, a 1-byte key-version prefix and a `PreviousKeys` map — i.e. key rotation was designed in from the start rather than bolted on, and the constructor hard-fails on a missing or wrong-length key (`:31-41`).
- The authorization core fails closed. `src/DM.Infrastructure.Core/Authorization/IntentionManager.cs:39` and `:58` log an error and return `false` when no resolver matches the (intention, target) pair. This is the single most important default in an authorization framework and it is right.
- Object-level authorization is actually applied, not just declared. I scanned all 139 `*Service.cs` files in `src/DM.Domain.*` for Create/Update/Delete methods lacking any ownership or intention check; every genuine hit turned out to be gated either at the controller (`[RequireRole]`) or one call level up. Spot-verified: `UserNotepadService.cs:78-172` (per-entry `ThrowIfNotAuthorized`), `BlogBlacklistService.cs:59-62` (`ThrowIfForbidden(BlogIntention.Edit, blog)`), `BlogInvitationService.cs:148-152` (invitation must belong to the caller) and `:201-202` (cancel is owner-gated), `UploadApiService.cs:121-124,143-146` (owner-or-Moderator on read and delete). I found no IDOR in the domain layer.
- PII exposure is explicitly role-scoped rather than accidentally wide: `ModeratedProfileApiService.cs:78-92` returns email/IP/login-history only when `callerRole >= Admin` and nulls them otherwise, and the raw login-history endpoint carries `[RequireRole(UserRole.Admin)]` (`UserController.cs:87-93`). The `Permissions` block (`:122-129`) mirrors the same predicate so the UI cannot disagree with the server.
- Session lifecycle handling is correct on the parts that are usually wrong: login always mints a new session via `_sessionFactory.Create` (`AuthenticationService.cs:127`) so there is no session fixation; a token-based password reset calls `LogoutAll` and an authenticated change calls `LogoutElsewhere` (`PasswordChangeService.cs:124-131`); `TerminateSession` refuses to touch another user's session (`AuthenticationService.cs:274-284`).
- The image upload path is thorough: magic-byte sniffing instead of trusting `Content-Type`, a 64 MPix decompression-bomb guard (`ImageProcessingService.cs:35`), EXIF strip, extension normalized from the *validated* content type rather than the filename, and `SanitizeFileName` stripping everything outside `[\p{L}\p{N}_\-.]` (`UploadApiService.cs:445-457`). Idempotency keys are namespaced per user so cross-user replay is impossible (`:171`).
- The HIBP integration is correct k-anonymity (5-char SHA-1 prefix, suffix matched client-side, `Add-Padding` request header set in `Startup.cs:145`) and fails open with distinct log levels per failure mode (`HibpPasswordChecker.cs:56-91`). It is applied on both registration and every password change including the reset path (`PasswordChangeService.cs:112`).
- The BBCode privacy model is a real architectural invariant, not a UI convention: filtering of `[private]`/`[mod]` happens server-side before JSON serialization, with a per-surface tag set (`BbParserProvider.cs:186-199`) so a privacy tag cannot even be emitted from a surface where it is not allowed. `DirectMessage` deliberately drops `[mod]` so users cannot impersonate moderators in private chat (`:141-146`) — that is the kind of detail that only comes from thinking about the threat.

## Подтвержденные находки

### [КРИТИЧНО] SEC-01 — Stored XSS in the server-side BBCode renderer: link text is emitted unescaped on every content surface

**Статус: Исправлено.** Экранирование текста ссылки + 4 теста (304e618a)

**Доказательство.** `src/DM.Infrastructure.Core/Parsing/BbParserWrapper.cs:387` — in the `[link]` placeholder restoration: `if (safeUrl == "#") return text ?? "";`. When `SanitizeUrl` rejects the URL, the *display text* captured from `[link=TEXT]` is returned into the HTML stream verbatim, skipping the `HtmlEncode` on the success branch at `:390`. The capture group is `([^\]]+)` (`:143`), so any markup without a `]` qualifies. I built a harness against the real `BbParserProvider` and the shipped BBCodeParser 1.0.0 and executed it: input `[link=<img src=x onerror="fetch('//evil/'+document.cookie)">]zzz[/link]` produced output `<img src=x onerror="fetch('//evil/'+document.cookie)">` for Comment, GamePost, Profile, DirectMessage, GlobalChatMessage, SafePost and AuthorEdit — all seven parsers. That HTML is bound with `v-html` at `src/DM.Web.Client/src/shared/ui/Content/ContentText.vue:46`, `features/comment/ui/

**Последствие.** Any user can store script in a forum comment, blog post, game post, profile bio or DM that executes in every viewer's browser on the app origin. HttpOnly does not help — the payload acts as the victim inside their own session, so an admin viewing the thread is a full site takeover (see SEC-03: a self-promote-to-Admin endpoint is one same-origin POST away). CSP does not help either: `SecurityHeadersMiddleware.cs:67` ships `script-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'`, and it is applied to API JSON responses, not to the SPA origin (see SEC-10).

**Исправление.** At `BbParserWrapper.cs:387` return `HttpUtility.HtmlEncode(text ?? "")`, matching `:390`. Then audit every other `return match.Value` / raw-string path in `ToHtmlCore` under the rule "nothing reaches the HTML stream without an explicit encode", and add a table-driven test asserting that `<`, `>`, `"` never survive any BBCode input on any surface.

**Проверка оппонентом.** Survives adversarial verification on every checkable point, including empirical execution. (1) Code matches the citation exactly: BbParserWrapper.cs:387 is `if (safeUrl == \"#\") return text ?? \"\";` — raw text into the HTML stream — versus `HttpUtility.HtmlEncode` on the success branch at :390; the capture group at :143 is `\\[link=([^\\]]+)\\]`. (2) The bypass mechanism is real and structural: Parse() extracts [link] into _linkList at :223-230 BEFORE `_inner.Parse(processed)` at :251, so the display text never passes through BbParser.SecuritySubstitutions. (3) I executed a temporary xUnit probe against the real BbParserProvider. Input `[link=<img src=x onerror=\"alert(1)\">]zzz[/link]` emitted `<img src=x onerror=\"alert(1)\">` verbatim for Comment, GamePost, Profile, DirectMessage and GlobalChatMessage — and also for all five GetSafeForSurface (EmbedSafe) parsers, which the finding o

### [КРИТИЧНО] SEC-02 — Production session key is the placeholder committed to git, and session tokens do not bind userId to sessionId

**Статус: Исправлено.** Обе половины закрыты. Ключ шифрования удален из отслеживаемого appsettings, дефолта в репозитории у него нет, ValidateOnStart валит старт при отсутствии или неверной длине, compose требует переменную через :?, dm.ps1 генерирует локальный ключ. Привязка userId к sessionId: поиск сессии идет по _id владельца, сигнатуру нельзя вызвать без userId, 2 теста. Ротация не событие - боевой базы нет (f9a3e8e2, ca37e954)

**Доказательство.** Two defects that compose. (a) `src/DM.Web.API/appsettings.json` is tracked in git (`git ls-files` confirms it, despite `.gitignore`'s `**/appsettings.json` rule and the template's own `"_security_note": "NEVER commit appsettings.json to git"`), and it contains `"CryptoConfiguration": { "KeyBase64": "QUJDREVGR0hJSktMTU5PUFFSU1RVVldYWVphYmNkZWY=" }` — base64 of the literal string `ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdef`. `docker/app.Dockerfile:37,46` publishes it into the runtime image; `WebHostBuilderExtensions.cs:29,37` makes it a live config source overridable only by `DM_CryptoConfiguration__KeyBase64`, which `docker/docker-compose.yml` never sets (grep: zero hits) and `docker/.env.example` leaves commented out. `docs/guides/DEPLOYMENT.md` never mentions it. (b) The session cookie is `AES-GCM(JSON {userId, sessionId})` (`AuthenticationService.cs:306-312`), and on validation the session is fe

**Последствие.** Full account takeover of any user, including Admin, by any attacker. Register → log in → `GET /v1/account/sessions` returns your own `sessionId` (`AuthenticationApiService.cs:184`) → encrypt `{"userId":"<victim guid>","sessionId":"<your own>"}` with the published key → set it as `dm_session`. The victim's session store is untouched, so nothing looks anomalous. Independently of the key leak, (b) means any future key compromise or crypto downgrade converts directly into universal impersonation rather than into forging one specific session.

**Исправление.** Rotate the key out of band, purge `appsettings.json` from the index (`git rm --cached`), make `CryptoConfiguration.KeyBase64` required-from-environment with no in-repo default, and set it in `docker-compose.yml`/`.env.example` with a generation command. Separately and regardless: change `IAuthenticationRepository.FindUserSession(Guid sessionId)` to `FindUserSession(Guid userId, Guid sessionId)` scoped by the owning `UserSession` document, so a token whose userId and sessionId disagree fails.

**Проверка оппонентом.** Both halves verified at the cited lines; attempts to refute made it worse.

(a) KEY LEAK — CONFIRMED, and there is no working override path anywhere in the repo.
- `git ls-files` returns `src/DM.Web.API/appsettings.json` (tracked despite `.gitignore:11` `**/appsettings.json`). Line 38 holds `"KeyBase64": "QUJDREVGR0hJSktMTU5PUFFSU1RVVldYWVphYmNkZWY="` = literal `ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdef` (32 bytes, so it passes every guard).
- It reaches the runtime image: `src/DM.Web.API/DM.Web.API.csproj:56-59` explicitly sets `<Content Update="appsettings.json"><CopyToPublishDirectory>PreserveNewest`, `docker/app.Dockerfile:41` publishes to `out`, `:46` `COPY --from=build /app/out ./`. `docker/app.Dockerfile.dockerignore` does not exclude it.
- `WebHostBuilderExtensions.cs:29` registers it as a live source; `:37` `AddEnvironmentVariables("DM_")` is the only override.
- `docker/docker-compose.y

### [КРИТИЧНО] SEC-03 — docker-compose defaults ASPNETCORE_ENVIRONMENT to Development, unlocking an unauthenticated seed endpoint and self-promotion to Admin

**Статус: Исправлено.** Обе половины: дефолт окружения в compose переведен на Production (6b5fa1de), а сама поверхность - самоназначение роли и анонимные сиды - удалена из API вместе с сидером (7887c089). Окружение больше не единственное, что отделяет анонима от прав администратора: отделять нечего

**Доказательство.** `docker/docker-compose.yml:18`: `ASPNETCORE_ENVIRONMENT: ${ASPNETCORE_ENVIRONMENT:-Development}`. `docker/.env.example` never defines the variable (grep across the repo returns only this line, one line in `docs/guides/MIRRORING.md`, and a logging fallback). The dev gates are per-endpoint runtime checks in `src/DM.Web.API/Features/Moderation/Profiles/ModerationController.cs`: `SetMyRole` (`:63-77`) carries only `[AuthenticationRequired]` and lets any authenticated user assign themselves any `UserRole` including Admin; `SeedTestUsers` (`:152-165`) and `SeedComprehensiveData` (`:189-202`) carry **no** auth attribute at all and, per their own XML docs, create `SolohinLex (Admin)` and friends with the fixed password `Test123!`. Swagger is also exposed on the same condition (`Startup.cs:411-416`) and HSTS is suppressed (`SecurityHeadersMiddleware.cs:70`).

**Последствие.** A deployment following `docs/guides/DEPLOYMENT.md` (clone, copy `.env.example`, `docker compose up`) runs in Development. An anonymous attacker POSTs `/v1/moderation/seed`, then logs in as a seeded Admin with a documented password. Any registered user can alternatively POST `/v1/moderation/users/me/role/5`. Both are unauthenticated-to-Admin in one step.

**Исправление.** Default the compose variable to `Production` and set it explicitly in `.env.example`. Do not rely on a runtime `IsDevelopment()` check inside a production-registered controller for endpoints of this power — move seeding and role-assignment into a controller that is only registered when `env.IsDevelopment()` (conditional application part / `#if DEBUG`), so the routes do not exist in a release image at all.

**Проверка оппонентом.** Every cite holds and the surrounding code makes it worse, not better. docker-compose.yml:18 forces ASPNETCORE_ENVIRONMENT=Development via the *workload-env anchor consumed by dmapi (:536); neither docker/.env.example (56 lines, read in full), docker/.env, nor app.Dockerfile ever defines the variable, so the compose default is unconditional. ModerationController.cs:63-78 (SetMyRole, [AuthenticationRequired] only) and :152-165 / :189-202 (SeedTestUsers, SeedComprehensiveData — no auth attribute at all) rely solely on a runtime IsDevelopment() check, and Startup.cs:350 registers plain AddMvc with no Filters.Add and no authorization FallbackPolicy, so there is no global gate behind them. Implementation matches the XML docs: ModerationApiService.cs:317-323 writes any submitted UserRole onto the caller's own user row unbounded; :382 const defaultPassword = "Test123!"; :394 seeds Login="Solohin

### [ВЫСОКАЯ] SEC-04 — All endpoint-level rate limiting is dead code — UseRateLimiter() runs before UseRouting()

**Статус: Исправлено.** То же, что API-01 (304e618a)

**Доказательство.** `src/DM.Web.API/Startup.cs:431-432`: `.UseMiddleware<CsrfProtectionMiddleware>().UseRateLimiter().UseRouting()`. The rate-limiting middleware resolves the policy from endpoint metadata, which does not exist until routing has run, so every `[EnableRateLimiting(...)]` attribute is ignored. I verified empirically on the same runtime the repo targets (ASP.NET Core 8.0.22): a minimal app with `UseRateLimiter(); UseRouting();` and an endpoint carrying a 1-request-per-minute policy returned 200/200/200 across three consecutive calls, and an endpoint referencing a *nonexistent* policy also returned 200 instead of throwing. That kills the `auth` 5/min policy on `AuthenticationController` (`:25`), `uploads` (`UploadController.cs:127`), `sliding`, `username-check` and `email-check`. What remains is the global limiter, which partitions on `context.Connection.RemoteIpAddress` (`Startup.cs:212`) with 

**Последствие.** No brute-force or credential-stuffing protection at the HTTP layer; the only remaining control is the per-email lockout (see SEC-08), which does nothing against spraying one password across many accounts. Simultaneously a self-DoS: roughly ten concurrent visitors exhaust the single shared global bucket and everyone gets 429.

**Исправление.** Move `.UseRateLimiter()` to after `.UseRouting()` (and after `.UseAuthentication()` if you want the `uploads`/`sliding` policies to actually partition per user — `context.User.Identity?.Name` at `:259` and `:275` is never populated by this pipeline either, since identity lives in `IIdentityProvider`, not `HttpContext.User`). Add `UseForwardedHeaders` with `KnownProxies` so the partition key is the real client. Fix SEC-05 in the same change or the move causes an outage.

**Проверка оппонентом.** Could not refute — reproduced empirically. (1) Pipeline order is exactly as cited: src/DM.Web.API/Startup.cs:430-433 is `.UseMiddleware<CsrfProtectionMiddleware>().UseRateLimiter().UseRouting().UseAuthentication()`. (2) No auto-routing rescue: src/DM.Web.API/Program.cs:36-39 uses the classic hosting model (Host.CreateDefaultBuilder -> ConfigureWebHostDefaults -> UseDefault<Startup> -> UseStartup, src/DM.Infrastructure.Core/Extensions/WebBuilderExtensions.cs:28-31), so the WebApplication behavior that injects UseRouting at the head of the pipeline does not apply; there is no IStartupFilter in production code. (3) I built a minimal net8.0 app on the installed ASP.NET Core 8.0.22 with the same classic Startup and the same two lines: with `UseRateLimiter(); UseRouting();` an endpoint carrying a 1-req/min policy returned 200/200/200 and an endpoint referencing an unregistered policy returned 

### [ВЫСОКАЯ] SEC-05 — Six controllers reference a rate-limit policy named "default" that is never registered — 500 on every request the moment SEC-04 is fixed

**Статус: Исправлено.** Политика default зарегистрирована (304e618a)

**Доказательство.** `[EnableRateLimiting("default")]` appears at `Features/Personal/Profiles/ProfileController.cs:23`, `Personal/Preferences/PreferencesController.cs:25`, `Personal/Subscriptions/SubscriptionController.cs:26`, `Personal/Invitations/InvitationController.cs:24`, `Personal/Blacklists/BlacklistController.cs:22`, `Blog/Blacklists/BlogBlacklistController.cs:23`. `Startup.cs:205-301` registers only `auth`, `username-check`, `email-check`, `uploads`, `sliding` — in both the enabled and the disabled branch. I ran this against 8.0.22: an endpoint requesting an unregistered policy throws `InvalidOperationException: This endpoint requires a rate limiting policy with name default, but no such policy exists.` and the request returns 500. It is latent today only because of SEC-04.

**Последствие.** The correct fix for SEC-04 (moving `UseRateLimiter` after `UseRouting`) instantly 500s the profile page, user preferences, subscriptions, invitations and both blacklists — i.e. the security fix ships an outage. It also means these six controllers have had zero rate limiting since commit 9fa0535c while appearing protected in code review.

**Исправление.** Either register a `default` policy in `Startup.cs` alongside the others, or delete the six attributes and let the global limiter apply. Add a startup assertion that every `EnableRateLimitingAttribute.PolicyName` present in the endpoint data source exists in the policy map — this class of typo should fail at boot, not at request time.

**Проверка оппонентом.** I tried to refute this and could not — every checkable claim holds, and I reproduced the runtime behavior on the exact framework version.

FACTS VERIFIED IN THE REPO
- The six `[EnableRateLimiting("default")]` sites exist exactly as cited: D:\Code Projects\dm3\src\DM.Web.API\Features\Personal\Profiles\ProfileController.cs:23, ...\Personal\Preferences\PreferencesController.cs:25, ...\Personal\Subscriptions\SubscriptionController.cs:26, ...\Personal\Invitations\InvitationController.cs:24, ...\Personal\Blacklists\BlacklistController.cs:22, ...\Blog\Blacklists\BlogBlacklistController.cs:23.
- D:\Code Projects\dm3\src\DM.Web.API\Startup.cs:205-301 registers only "auth" (221), "username-check" (232), "email-check" (243), "uploads" (257), "sliding" (273) in the enabled branch and the same five in the disabled branch (291-300). A repo-wide grep for `AddPolicy(` finds no "default" anywhere — no t

### [ВЫСОКАЯ] SEC-06 — Unvalidated non-image upload path writes attacker-chosen Content-Type into a public-read, same-origin bucket

**Статус: Исправлено.** PostAttachment пущен через существующий конвейер валидации, второй путь UploadNonImageAsync удален; тест не дает оставить тип вне конвейера (d4307c9b)

**Доказательство.** `ImageProcessingService.cs:46-47`: `IsImageType(type) => type is UserAvatar or CharacterAvatar` — `PostAttachment` is excluded, so `UploadApiService.cs:255-258` routes it to `UploadNonImageAsync`. That method (`:326-363`) takes the extension straight from `Path.GetExtension(file.FileName)` (`:329`) and passes `file.ContentType` — the client-supplied multipart header — as the S3 object's `ContentType` (`:333`, stored at `:408-423`). No magic-byte check, no allow-list, no normalization. The bucket is created with an anonymous `s3:GetObject` policy for `*` (`StorageBucketInitializer.cs:57-71`) and is proxied on the *application origin* by `docker/nginx/nginx.conf:93-104` (`location /dm-uploads/`, `auth_basic off`).

**Последствие.** `POST /v1/uploads?type=3&targetId=<any post id>` with a `.html` file and `Content-Type: text/html` (or `image/svg+xml`) yields a same-origin document under the attacker's control at `/dm-uploads/posts/…`. `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff` is irrelevant when the declared type is genuinely `text/html`. Same blast radius as SEC-01, reachable by any authenticated user, and it survives a fix to the BBCode renderer.

**Исправление.** Run every upload through the same validation gate: sniff the content, allow-list the resulting type, and derive both the stored `Content-Type` and the object extension from the sniffed type — never from `IFormFile.FileName`/`ContentType`. For anything not on the allow-list, force `application/octet-stream` plus `Content-Disposition: attachment`. Better still, serve user uploads from a separate origin so a mistake here cannot reach the session cookie.

**Проверка оппонентом.** Could not refute — every cited line reads as claimed. ImageProcessingService.cs:46-47 excludes PostAttachment from IsImageType, so UploadApiService.cs:255-258 sends it to UploadNonImageAsync (:326-363), which derives the extension from Path.GetExtension(file.FileName) (:329) and stores the client-supplied file.ContentType as the S3 object's Content-Type (:333 -> PutToS3Async :408-423) with no magic-byte sniff, no allow-list and no normalization — the exact opposite of the image path (:264-301). UploadController.cs:133-137 binds `type` from the query with no allow-list and no intention check, so any authenticated user reaches it (a real game Post GUID is needed only because of the FK/CHECK at DmDbContext.cs:540-562, trivially obtainable). The bucket really is anonymous-read (StorageBucketInitializer.cs:59-73) and really is proxied on the app origin with auth_basic off (docker/nginx/nginx.

### [ВЫСОКАЯ] SEC-07 — Mirror session transfer hands a raw session token to JavaScript over an unauthenticated endpoint, for a feature the frontend never implemented

**Статус: Исправлено.** Решение владельца - удалить. Сняты оба эндпоинта, выдача transfer-токена, методы сервиса и заглушка на клиенте; e2e-тест держит 404. Обоснование SameSite=Lax переписано на настоящее - переход по ссылке из письма (f19bd399)

**Доказательство.** `Features/General/Mirror/MirrorController.cs:124-141` — `POST /v1/mirrors/transfer/accept` has no `[AuthenticationRequired]`, takes the transfer token from the query string, and returns `TransferAcceptResponse { AuthToken = identity.AuthenticationToken! }`: the exact byte-for-byte value of the `dm_session` cookie, in a JSON body readable by script. The transfer token itself is placed in a URL (`:107-108`, `transferUrl += "?transfer=" + …`) and the client navigates to it with `window.location.href` (`shared/lib/composables/useRegion.ts:75`). `AuthenticationService.AuthenticateWithTransferToken` (`:336-414`) validates only a 5-minute timestamp — no single-use marking, no binding to the target mirror, no session rotation on redemption. Meanwhile the consumer is a stub: `pages/account/TransferPage.vue:7` reads `// Session transfer is not implemented - just redirect home` and renders "Перенос

**Последствие.** A bearer-equivalent credential valid for the session's full lifetime (up to a year) is transported in a URL — browser history, `Referer` on same-site navigations, nginx access logs, sync services, extensions — and any holder can exchange it for a session token via an unauthenticated endpoint, replayably within the 5-minute window. All of this risk is being carried for zero delivered functionality, and it weakens the cookie's CSRF posture as a side effect.

**Исправление.** Delete the endpoint and the token issuance until the feature is actually built. When built: mark the token single-use (store and consume it), bind it to the target mirror id, mint a *new* session on redemption rather than aliasing the source session, deliver it via `Set-Cookie` from the target origin, and never return a session token in a response body. Then reconsider `SameSite=Strict`.

**Проверка оппонентом.** I tried to break this one and could not. Every load-bearing claim reproduces in the source, and two of the refutation angles I chased (config disables the feature; CSRF middleware blocks redemption) turned out to make it worse, not better.

VERIFIED FACTS

1. The endpoint is real, unauthenticated, and returns a session credential to script. `src/DM.Web.API/Features/General/Mirror/MirrorController.cs:124-141`: `[HttpPost("transfer/accept")]` carries no `[AuthenticationRequired]` (contrast the 100+ sites that do, e.g. `Features/Account/Credentials/CredentialsController.cs:27`), takes `[FromQuery] string transferToken`, and returns `TransferAcceptResponse { AuthToken = identity.AuthenticationToken! }` in the JSON body. `Startup.cs:350` registers MVC with no global authorization filter, so nothing gates it.

2. The returned token is a bearer credential for the *source* session. `Authenticati

### [ВЫСОКАЯ] SEC-08 — X-Forwarded-For is trusted verbatim while nginx appends to it — the entire security audit trail is client-forgeable

**Статус: Исправлено.** Доверенные сети по умолчанию пусты; подделка заголовка проверена на живом стенде до и после (2107db2b, c91d8d4d)

**Доказательство.** `Shared/Authentication/WebAuthenticationService.cs:63-76` and a verbatim duplicate at `Features/Account/Authentication/AuthenticationApiService.cs:156-169` both take `Request.Headers["X-Forwarded-For"].First()` as the client IP. `docker/nginx/nginx.conf:76` uses `proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for`, which *appends* the real peer to whatever the client sent — so a request with `X-Forwarded-For: 8.8.8.8` arrives as `8.8.8.8, <real ip>` and `.First()` returns the forgery. The solution registers no `UseForwardedHeaders` and no `KnownProxies` anywhere. This value is written to `UserLoginRecord.IpAddress` (`AuthenticationApiService.cs:140`), to `SecurityAuditEntry` (`AuthenticationService.cs:114,131`), to session `DeviceInfo`, and it is the input to `ISuspiciousLoginDetector.IsSuspiciousAsync` (`WebAuthenticationService.cs:90-93`).

**Последствие.** An attacker who has stolen credentials replays the victim's habitual IP and the "suspicious login" email — the one control that would alert the victim — never fires. Conversely an attacker can poison another user's login history and the Admin-visible IP list (`ModeratedProfileApiService.cs:79`) with arbitrary addresses, making the moderation evidence trail worthless and enabling framing. The forged value also lands in the `linked profiles` correlation used to identify alt accounts.

**Исправление.** Add `UseForwardedHeaders` with an explicit `KnownProxies`/`KnownNetworks` list before `UseAuthentication`, delete both hand-rolled `ExtractClientIp` copies, and read `HttpContext.Connection.RemoteIpAddress` afterwards. If a raw header must be parsed, take the rightmost untrusted hop, not the leftmost.

**Проверка оппонентом.** Every cited line checks out verbatim, and no guard exists anywhere in the solution.

VERIFIED AS CITED:
