Причина отклонения: The finding's load-bearing evidence is false. It claims "NotificationBotSender.SendDiscordMessage performs an unguarded outbound POST to discord.com" and "the email sender already does this [per-recipient try/catch] — the bot sender does not." Reading the cited file, src/DM.Workers.NotificationDispatcher/Implementation/Bot/NotificationBotSender.cs:177-220 wraps the ENTIRE SendDiscordMessage body —
- **NO-SCALEOUT** — The API is structurally single-instance while DEPLOYMENT.md documents `replicas: 3`. Причина отклонения: The headline mechanism is factually inverted, and three of the four "independent blockers" collapse with it.

(1) REFUTED — the cited code does the opposite of the claim. `Exclusive = true` at src/DM.Web.API/Realtime/RealtimeNotificationConsumer.cs:39 does NOT declare a fixed-name exclusive queue. In Jamq 0.10.0 (`src/Jamq.Client.Rabbit/Ensure.cs`, the only place `RabbitConsumerParameters.Exclusiv

## Средние и низкие

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] DUPLICATE-REALTIME-CLASSES — Two copies of the realtime consumer and processor exist; the stale copy is missing the global-chat branch

**Статус: Опровергнуто.** Дублирующей пары больше нет: удалена в c91d8d4d, остался один консьюмер и один процессор, причем тот, у которого есть ветка глобального чата

`Web.API/Realtime/RealtimeNotificationConsumer.cs` and `Web.API/Notifications/RealtimeNotificationConsumer.cs` are byte-for-byte identical except for the namespace and the mangled log emoji. `Web.API/Realtime/RealtimeNotificationProcessor.cs:30-50` branches on `EventType.NewGlobalChatMessage` to broadcast via `Clients.All`; `Web.API/Notifications/RealtimeNotificationProcessor.cs:22-32` has no such branch and only ever targets the connection map. Which one runs is decided by namespace resolution: Startup.cs:30 imports `DM.Web.API.Realtime` and there is no `using DM.Web.API.Notifications`, so `services.AddHostedService<RealtimeNotificationConsumer>()` at Startup.cs:155 binds the Realtime one. Both processors are additionally picked up by the blanket `builder.RegisterDefaultTypes()` scan and registered as `IProcessor<string, RealtimeNotification>`.

Последствие: The live behaviour hinges on a `using` directive, not on an explicit choice. Adding `using DM.Web.API.Notifications;` to Startup.cs — the sort of thing an IDE auto-import does — silently switches the app to the copy that cannot broadcast to guests, and nothing would fail to compile or test. Anyone fixing the global-chat routing gap has a 50% chance of editing the dead file.

Исправление: Delete `Web.API/Notifications/RealtimeNotificationConsumer.cs` and `Web.API/Notifications/RealtimeNotificationProcessor.cs`. The `Notifications/` folder should keep only the hub, its interface, and the settings repository; everything transport-related already lives in `Realtime/`.

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] OUTBOX-DESIGN-FLAWS — Even if it were wired, the outbox processor would not survive production: no index, no cleanup, no locking, payload discarded

**Статус: Исправлено.** То же, что OUTBOX-DECORATIVE (1876e028)

Five defects in ~70 lines. (1) The hot query `Where(e => !e.IsProcessed && (e.NextRetryUtc == null || e.NextRetryUtc <= now)).OrderBy(e => e.Id).Take(100)` (OutboxProcessor.cs:70-74) runs every 5 seconds against a table whose only index is the PK (InitialCreate.cs:97-115 declares nothing but `PK_OutboxEvents`). (2) Processed rows are never deleted or archived, so that unindexed table grows monotonically. (3) No `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED` or lease column, while the processor is registered in the API (Startup.cs:372) which the docs want replicated 3x. (4) `OutboxEvent.Payload` (OutboxEvent.cs:30) is written to the schema and read by nobody — line 87 publishes only `(EventType)evt.EventType, evt.AggregateId`. (5) "Dead-lettering" at line 105 is `evt.IsProcessed = true`, making a poison event indistinguishable from a delivered one except by a non-null `LastError`.

Последствие: Sequential scans over an ever-growing table every 5s degrade steadily until they dominate the OLTP database. Multiple replicas would publish every event N times. The unused `Payload` column means the outbox cannot carry anything the consumer needs beyond an id — consumers must re-read the aggregate, which reintroduces the read-your-own-write race the outbox exists to eliminate. And there is no operational way to list or replay poisoned events.

Исправление: Add a partial index `CREATE INDEX ... ON "OutboxEvents" ("Id") WHERE NOT "IsProcessed"`, claim batches with `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED`, replace the boolean dead-letter with an explicit `Status` enum (Pending/Processed/DeadLettered), and add a retention job that deletes Processed rows older than N days. Do this only as part of finishing OUTBOX-DECORATIVE — otherwise delete the whole thing.

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] UNORDERED-INDEXING — `ProcessingOrder.Unmanaged` on the search and notification queues makes create/change/delete races silently corrupt the index

**Статус: Открыто.** 

`SearchIndexerConsumer.cs:54` and `NotificationDispatcherConsumer.cs:38` both declare `ProcessingOrder.Unmanaged` (the mail and realtime consumers correctly use `Sequential`). The search queue binds NewTopic, ChangedTopic, DeletedTopic, NewTopicComment, ChangedTopicComment, DeletedTopicComment (SearchIndexerConsumer.cs:57-66) and `CompositeIndexer.Process` (CompositeIndexer.cs:30-32) additionally runs all matching indexers concurrently via `Task.WhenAll`. Nothing carries a version, sequence number, or timestamp: `InvokedEvent` is just `{Type, EntityId}`.

Последствие: Edit-then-delete or create-then-edit within the same second is a coin flip. If `DeletedTopicComment` is processed before `ChangedTopicComment` for the same id, the change handler re-inserts a document for content that no longer exists — a permanent ghost result in search that no subsequent event will ever clear, because the delete has already been consumed. The notification queue has the analogous exposure for the `StatusCharacter*` and `StatusGame*` families, where an out-of-order pair produces a notification describing a state the entity is no longer in.

Исправление: Switch both consumers to `ProcessingOrder.Sequential` (throughput is nowhere near needing otherwise for this product), or make the indexers last-write-wins by carrying the aggregate's `LastUpdateUtc` in `InvokedEvent` and having each indexer no-op when the stored document is newer. Sequential is the right call here — the parallelism buys nothing at this scale and costs correctness.

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] READINESS-COUPLING — RabbitMQ is tagged `ready`, so a broker outage removes the entire API from load-balancer rotation

**Статус: Открыто.** 

`Web.API/Startup.cs:187-190` registers `.AddRabbitMQ(..., tags: new[] { "messaging", "ready" })`, and Startup.cs:450-454 maps `/_ready` to `Predicate = check => check.Tags.Contains("ready")` — the endpoint DEPLOYMENT.md:180 designates for the load balancer. Nothing in the read path depends on the broker: browsing forums, blogs, games, profiles and search all resolve from Postgres/Mongo/OpenSearch.

Последствие: Inverts the entire point of having asynchronous messaging. The bus exists so that email and notifications do not block the request path — but a broker failure now takes the site fully offline at the LB instead of degrading to "notifications are delayed". A five-minute RabbitMQ restart becomes a five-minute total outage. The worker healthchecks have the opposite problem: docker-compose.yml uses `test -d /proc/1` for all three workers, which passes as long as the process exists even if its Rabbit subscription never succeeded.

Исправление: Move RabbitMQ to a `messaging`-only tag so `/_ready` covers Postgres and Mongo, and surface the broker under `/_health/detail` for alerting. Give the workers a real readiness check that asserts the consumer is subscribed, rather than `test -d /proc/1`.

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] NGINX-HUB-PATH — The checked-in nginx config proxies `/notifications` for SignalR, but the hub is mounted at `/whatsup`

**Статус: Исправлено.** location /whatsup + map $connection_upgrade; конфиг проверен nginx -t (a0250f1f)

`Startup.cs:439` maps the hub with `c.MapHub<NotificationHub>("/whatsup")` and the client connects to `${apiHost}/whatsup` (client.ts:215, useSignalR.ts:153). `docker/nginx/nginx.conf:80-91` defines the only WebSocket-tuned location — including `proxy_read_timeout 86400` — as `location /notifications`, a path that exists nowhere in the application. `/whatsup` matches no specific location and falls through to `location /` (nginx.conf:105-113), which proxies to `http://frontend` (the static asset container) with no `Upgrade` headers. `.env.preview` sets `VITE_API_HOST=` (empty), so the browser resolves the hub URL against the nginx origin.

Последствие: Behind the preview reverse proxy, the SignalR negotiate request is served by the static file container. Realtime never connects at all — the client falls back to the 30s poll, which masks the failure well enough that it reads as "working, just slow". Separately, nginx.conf:72-73 sets `Connection "upgrade"` unconditionally on `/v1/`, which is wrong for ordinary REST traffic (it needs the `$connection_upgrade` map idiom).

Исправление: Rename the location block to `/whatsup` and add the standard `map $http_upgrade $connection_upgrade { default upgrade; '' close; }` at the http level, referencing `$connection_upgrade` from both blocks instead of the hardcoded literal.

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] API-REFERENCES-WORKER — DM.Web.API takes a project reference on DM.Workers.SearchIndexer to borrow generated gRPC stubs

**Статус: Исправлено.** Дубль backend-arch:api-references-worker-project-for-protos

`DM.Web.API.csproj` lists `<ProjectReference Include="..\DM.Workers.SearchIndexer\DM.Workers.SearchIndexer.csproj" />` with the comment "Proto types are provided by DM.Workers.SearchIndexer reference", and Startup.cs:33 does `using DM.Workers.SearchIndexer.Grpc;` purely to reach the generated `SearchEngine.SearchEngineClient`. This drags the worker's entire closure into the API: its consumer, its indexers, its OpenSearch client, its `SearchEngineService` gRPC implementation, and its transitive `DM.Domain.Forum` reference.

Последствие: Inverts the deployment topology in the build graph — a service that is supposed to be independently deployable is now a compile-time dependency of the thing it decouples from. Every change to the indexer forces an API rebuild and redeploy, and the API process loads types for a worker it never runs. It also makes the API's blanket Autofac scan (`RegisterDefaultTypes`, ModuleRegistrationExtensions.cs:86-108) a larger surface than intended, which is precisely the class of problem the recent `PreserveExistingDefaults` fix was chasing.

Исправление: Extract `Protos/search.proto` and its generated client into a small `DM.Contracts.Search` project referenced by both the API and the worker, and drop the API→Worker reference.

### [НИЗКАЯ] GLOBALCHAT-GHOST-DOCS — Global-chat notifications persist an empty-recipient Mongo document per message

**Статус: Исправлено.** Уведомления без получателей больше не пишутся в Mongo; фильтр в NotificationService, а не в процессоре - иначе широковещание ушло бы с Guid.Empty. 2 теста red-green (00f65939)

`NewGlobalChatMessageNotificationGenerator.cs:53-57` deliberately yields a `CreateNotification` with `UsersInterested = []` (documented at lines 55-56 as intentional, since fan-out happens in the API). `NotificationProcessor.cs:63-68` only short-circuits when the generator list is *empty*, so the one-item list falls through to `_notificationService.CreateAsync`, which inserts a `Notification` document with an empty `UsersInterested` array (NotificationRepository.cs:52-66).

Последствие: One permanently unreadable Mongo document per global-chat message, growing without bound and matched by no `UserInterested` filter. Harmless today only because the routing gap (ROUTING-GAP) means the generator never runs — it becomes a real storage leak the moment that is fixed.

Исправление: Filter `notificationsToCreate` to entries with a non-empty `UsersInterested` before calling `CreateAsync`, and publish the realtime message from the unfiltered list.

### [НИЗКАЯ] DEAD-USESIGNALR — Two competing SignalR composables ship; the simpler one is unused

**Статус: Исправлено.** Мертвый per-component композабл удален вместе с экспортом из публичного барреля; он открывал второе соединение в обход lease-машинерии (00f65939)

`useSignalR.ts:13-113` exports a per-component `useSignalR()` that owns its own `HubConnection`, refuses to connect for guests (line 30-33), and disconnects on unmount. It is exported from `shared/lib/composables/index.ts:69` and imported by nothing — both call sites (App.vue:146 and GlobalChatPage.vue:43) use `useGlobalSignalR`. The two implementations have divergent semantics: the dead one hard-blocks anonymous connections, the live one explicitly supports them (useSignalR.ts:149-152).

Последствие: 113 lines of plausible-looking dead code sitting in front of the real implementation, with the opposite guest policy. A developer reaching for "the SignalR composable" has even odds of picking the one that silently refuses to work for guests, and would then open a second socket per component alongside the shared one.

Исправление: Delete `useSignalR()` and its export from `composables/index.ts`. `useGlobalSignalR` is the correct abstraction and should be the only one.

### [НИЗКАЯ] STATIC-SINGLETON-STATE — `UserConnectionService` keeps its state in `static` fields despite being registered as a singleton

**Статус: Исправлено.** static снят с двух карт: процессное время жизни дает SingleInstance, а static добавлял выживание между экземплярами контейнера (00f65939)

`Realtime/UserConnectionService.cs:12,16` declares `private static readonly ConcurrentDictionary<Guid, HashSet<string>> Connections` and `ConnectionOwners`, while Startup.cs:382-384 already registers the type `.SingleInstance()`. The `static` is therefore redundant for production and harmful for tests: state survives container disposal, so any integration test that connects a hub leaks connection entries into every subsequent test in the same assembly.

Последствие: Cross-test contamination in `DM.Web.API.IntegrationTests` that presents as order-dependent flakiness, and no way to reset the map between `WebApplicationFactory` instances. It also quietly encodes the single-process assumption in a way that makes NO-SCALEOUT harder to spot.

Исправление: Drop `static` from both fields — the `SingleInstance()` registration already provides the lifetime the class needs.


---

# Стратегия и качество тестов — C

The suite is broad, disciplined and deterministic, with a genuinely real Testcontainers integration layer and half a dozen exemplary regression tests — but the count is inflated by ~30% validator/mapping-config tests, the API's entire write surface is unexercised (every PATCH integration test asserts only 401), the hand-edited migration and the outbox delivery loop are never executed by any test, ban enforcement has zero coverage at any level, and the 231-test e2e tier never runs in CI.

## Что сделано хорошо

- Integration tests are genuinely integration tests, not mocked units in disguise: test/DM.Web.API.IntegrationTests/DatabaseFixture.cs:28-43 boots real PostgreSQL 16, MongoDB 7 and RabbitMQ containers, and CustomWebApplicationFactory.cs:240-251 wires the real Autofac composition root through WebApplicationFactory<Program>. 241 tests run against the actual HTTP pipeline and a real database.
- test/DM.Web.API.IntegrationTests/Controllers/LoginControllerShould.cs:104-200 is the best test file in the repo: real registration→activation→login through the API (Helpers/UserTestHelper.cs:27-69), real Set-Cookie extraction, then session invalidation asserted by re-calling a protected endpoint (line 142-144), logout-others asserted by proving session1 still works and session2 does not (165-170), and account lockout driven by 6 real failed attempts (191-199). No mocks, no shortcuts.
- test/DM.Infrastructure.Core.Tests/PermissionFilteringVisitorShould.cs is a proper security matrix, not a smoke test: 20 cases across (tag × surface × role × context) asserting that [private] is stripped for non-addressees (118-133), that a plain Moderator gets no bypass (150-158), that room/post overrides open it (161-178), and that [mod] must NOT force a per-viewer cache bucket (246-261) — that last one guards a cache-poisoning class of bug that is invisible to ordinary rendering tests.
- test/DM.Web.API.Tests/Features/General/DbContextScopeResolutionShould.cs is a model structural regression test: it reproduces the exact production wiring order (AddDbContextPool via Populate, then PersistenceModule), then asserts same-instance-per-scope, distinct-across-scopes, repository-shares-the-scope-context via reflection, and that ContextId.Lease is positive so the pool is actually engaged (89-98). The XML doc (17-27) records the failure mode, not just the fix.
- Time and identity are injected, not read: IDateTimeProvider/IGuidFactory are pinned in test constructors (e.g. test/DM.Domain.Account.Tests/Features/Authentication/SessionFactoryShould.cs:40-52 asserts exact ExpirationUtc equality). The 166 DateTimeOffset.UtcNow occurrences in tests are seed values fed to the provider, not live clock reads — only one tolerance-based assertion exists in the whole suite (DateTimeProviderShould.cs:14). Genuine flakiness risk is near zero on the backend.
- Information-leak assertions are done properly: BanControllerShould.cs:52-61 and WarningControllerShould.cs:58-67 assert both the positive shape and the negated fields (comment, moderator, user, entityType absent), then add a raw-string check that the moderator's private text does not appear anywhere in the payload (line 88 / 67). That is the right way to test a projection boundary.
- test/DM.Domain.Game.Tests/Features/Posts/DiceRollerShould.cs injects a deterministic RNG queue and covers real edge cases — explosion cap arithmetic (83-97), natural-max/min criticals (57-69), and a d1 being dropped without ever calling the RNG (124-134).
- DatabaseFixture seeding carries explanatory comments about why a row exists (lines 389-398, 430-437: "without this seed the endpoint would return an empty list and the assertions couldn't distinguish 'mapping drops the field' from 'no posts in DB'"). That is rare, and it is exactly the reasoning that prevents vacuous green tests.
- Frontend component tests, where they exist, test contracts rather than rendering: src/features/comment/__tests__/CommentItem.spec.ts asserts the editor is seeded from fetched raw BBCode and not from the displayed HTML, and that a failed source fetch does not open the editor — a real past-bug contract, with tiptap stubbed deliberately and the reason documented (lines 18-25).
- Authorization intention resolvers that are tested are tested as behaviour, not interaction: GameIntentionResolverShould.cs covers master/assistant/player asymmetry (90-150) and comment-access modes (152-184) against the real resolver with no mocks.

## Подтвержденные находки

### [КРИТИЧНО] TS-03 — Ban enforcement has no test at any level; the integration harness makes it untestable by construction

**Статус: Исправлено.** Обе половины. Обеспечение бана: его не было вовсе - правило и проверки входа читали колонку, в которую не пишет никто; починена цепочка, реализована семантика владельца, 21 тест, независимая проверка 62 поверхностей нашла ошибки и в самой правке (ca37e954, cb9a5dd4, 80fc6a38, 050c9ede). Фикстура: переведена с EnsureCreated на прогон миграции, 262 интеграционных теста идут на реальном стартовом сиде. Переход вскрыл три настоящих дефекта - двойной сид глобального чата, сторож сида тем, отключавший все форумные тесты, и подмену боевой доски тестовой под тем же идентификатором (c8afd670)

**Доказательство.** `AccessPolicy.FullBan` is checked in three places in src/DM.Domain.Account/Features/Authentication/AuthenticationService.cs (lines 109, 182, 386). A grep for `AccessPolicy.` across all of test/ returns exactly one non-`NotSpecified` hit outside the unrelated BoardAccessPolicy enum: test/DM.Domain.Moderation.Tests/Features/Warnings/BanServiceShould.cs:214, which asserts the ban row is *persisted* with DemocraticBan. No test anywhere constructs a subject carrying a ban policy. test/DM.Domain.Account.Tests/Features/Authentication/AuthenticationServiceShould.cs has 9 tests covering pending-registration, lockout, user-not-found and wrong-password — none for a banned user. The integration harness cannot cover it either: TestAuthenticationMiddleware.cs:56 hard-codes `AccessPolicy = AccessPolicy.NotSpecified` on every injected identity. Separately, `AccessPolicy.DemocraticBan` (the read-only ban

**Последствие.** Moderation's primary sanction is unverified end to end. A refactor that drops the FullBan check in AuthenticationService lets banned users log back in with every test green. Worse, the test shape actively hid a live defect: DemocraticBan is issued through the API and stored, but no code enforces it, so a read-only ban currently does nothing — and because the only ban test asserts persistence rather than effect, nothing flags it.

**Исправление.** Add AuthenticationServiceShould cases for FullBan at all three call sites (login, session refresh, token path). Give TestAuthenticationMiddleware an `X-Test-User-AccessPolicy` header so integration tests can drive a banned identity, then add HTTP-level tests that a FullBan'ed and a DemocraticBan'ed user are rejected from write endpoints. The DemocraticBan test will fail on write — that is the point; it exposes the missing enforcement.

**Проверка оппонентом.** Attempted refutation failed on every ground; the finding is real and, if anything, understates the defect.

VERIFIED CITATIONS (all accurate):
- src/DM.Domain.Account/Features/Authentication/AuthenticationService.cs:109, 182, 386 — three `user.AccessPolicy.HasFlag(AccessPolicy.FullBan)` guards returning `Identity.Fail(AuthenticationError.Banned)` (login switch, token path, mirror-transfer path).
- test/DM.Domain.Account.Tests/Features/Authentication/AuthenticationServiceShould.cs:116, 150 — the only two subjects built there both hard-set `AccessPolicy = AccessPolicy.NotSpecified`. No banned-user test exists.
- test/DM.Domain.Moderation.Tests/Features/Warnings/BanServiceShould.cs:191-214 (`PersistDemocraticBanScopeWhenRequested`) asserts only `capturedEntity.AccessRestrictionPolicy.Should().Be(AccessPolicy.DemocraticBan)` — persistence, not effect. Repo-wide grep for `AccessPolicy`/`Democ

### [НИЗКАЯ] TS-06 — UnitTestBase's verification hook is a no-op and every mock is loose, so tests cannot fail on unexpected collaborator use

**Статус: Исправлено.** Хук проверялся: MockRepository.Verify() проверяет только setup-ы с .Verifiable(), а их в наборе ноль - то есть Dispose во всех ~1275 unit-тестах не проверял ничего, но читался как страховка. UnitTestBase перестал быть IDisposable, четыре класса с настоящей уборкой (два DbContext, два ServiceProvider) объявляют IDisposable сами. Ожидания остаются на месте утверждения, где их видно

**Доказательство.** test/DM.Testing/UnitTestBase.cs:8 creates `new MockRepository(MockBehavior.Loose)` and line 13 disposes with `repository.Verify()`. `MockRepository.Verify()` only verifies setups explicitly marked `.Verifiable()`. A grep across test/ returns 0 occurrences of `Verifiable()` and 0 of `MockBehavior.Strict`. So the base-class Dispose verifies nothing, in every one of the ~1275 unit tests that inherit it.

**Последствие.** The suite's only structural safety net is decorative. Because all mocks are loose, any call to an un-stubbed collaborator silently returns default/null instead of failing, so a service that starts calling a new dependency — or calls an existing one with wrong arguments outside the one path a test happens to Verify — produces no failure. This is the mechanism by which service tests drift into asserting only the two or three interactions someone remembered to write, which is exactly the pattern seen in GameServiceShould and GameInactivityProcessorShould.

**Исправление.** Either mark the intended interactions `.Verifiable()` so the Dispose hook does real work, or delete the Verify() call so it stops reading as a guarantee it does not provide. For services whose contract is 'calls exactly these collaborators' (authorization managers, event producers), switch those specific mocks to MockBehavior.Strict.

**Проверка оппонентом.** The mechanical claim is factually exact and I could not refute it: test/DM.Testing/UnitTestBase.cs:8 creates the repository with MockBehavior.Loose, line 13 disposes via repository.Verify(), and Moq 4.20.70 (Directory.Packages.props:49) implements Mock.Verify() as a filter on setup.IsVerifiable. A grep across test/ returns 0 Verifiable(), 0 MockBehavior.Strict, 0 VerifyNoOtherCalls — MockBehavior appears in exactly two places, both inside UnitTestBase.cs. So line 13 verifies nothing in all 209 inheriting classes. That part stands.

Everything built on top of it does not, and the severity is inflated by two levels. (1) The impact claims this is "the suite's only structural safety net" — false. There are 355 explicit .Verify(...) assertions with exact-argument matchers and Times.Once/Times.Never counts, plus FluentAssertions on return values and a separate DM.Web.API.IntegrationTests proje

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] TS-07 — The 231-test Playwright e2e tier never runs in CI and a quarter of it is skipped

**Статус: Открыто.** 

**Доказательство.** .github/workflows/dotnet.yml runs `npm run type-check`, `npm run build` and `npm run test:coverage` in the frontend job — `npm run test:e2e` appears in no workflow. src/DM.Web.Client/playwright.config.ts declares 9 browser/device projects with `retries: process.env.CI ? 2 : 0`, so it was written for CI and never wired in. The suite has 231 `test(` declarations across 32 files and 57 `test.skip`/`test.fixme` (≈25%); two files declare zero live tests (e2e/tests/forum/likes.spec.ts, e2e/tests/messaging/edit-delete.spec.ts). It also depends on a live API at localhost:5000, a live dev server, and a seeded user whose password comes from E2E_TEST_PASSWORD (e2e/fixtures/auth.ts:8-19), with tests navigating hard-coded seeded content such as `/forum/Новости`.

**Последствие.** The only tier that exercises the real browser, the real SPA and the real API together — the tier that would catch the API-contract drift, routing and reveal/animation regressions the owner keeps hitting — provides zero automated signal. Unrun tests also rot: 25% are already disabled and the two empty files show the decay is active. The suite is a maintenance liability with no payoff.

**Исправление.** Add a CI job that brings up docker/ compose (API + PG + Mongo), seeds a fixed E2E user, and runs Playwright against chromium + mobile-chrome only (drop the 9-project matrix for CI). Make it required. Convert the skipped tests to failing tests or delete them — a skipped test is a lie about coverage.

**Проверка оппонентом.** CORE CLAIM SURVIVES — the tier genuinely has zero CI wiring. The repo contains exactly two workflow files. .github/workflows/dotnet.yml:55-57 runs only `type-check`, `build`, `test:coverage` in the frontend job; .github/workflows/security.yml is a ZAP baseline scan. A repo-wide grep for `test:e2e`/`playwright test` returns hits in ONE file — src/DM.Web.Client/package.json:12-14. No other CI system exists (no azure-pipelines, .gitlab-ci, or scripts/ runner). And playwright.config.ts is unambiguously CI-authored: FOUR separate `process.env.CI` branches (forbidOnly :6, retries :7, workers :8, reuseExistingServer :34) plus a 9-project browser/device matrix (:15-30). Written for CI, never plugged in. Corroborating: docs/plans/ROADMAP.md:25 lists "E2E тесты (Playwright, cookie auth)" as DONE under MVP, while docs/guides/TESTING.md:5-10 — the project's own testing guide — declares exactly two l

## Опровергнуто оппонентом (в работу не берем)

- **TS-01** — The single hand-edited InitialCreate migration is never executed by any test. Причина отклонения: The finding's load-bearing premise is factually wrong, and I disproved its predicted symptom by running the very check it proposes.

1) "Per the project's own rule it is edited in place by hand rather than regenerated" — false. The rule is documented as *regeneration*, not hand-editing: docs/conventions/DATA_STORAGE.md:136-139 ("После изменения Entity — пересоздать миграцию" / `dotnet ef migration
- **TS-02** — Outbox delivery, retry, backoff and dead-lettering are 100% untested. Причина отклонения: The evidence about the tests is accurate, but the "critical" rating rests on a premise the code contradicts: that domain events flow through the outbox. They do not. There is no write side.

Grep across the whole repo for `new OutboxEvent`, `OutboxEvents.Add/AddAsync/AddRange`, raw SQL inserts, `SaveChanges` overrides on `DmDbContext`, and `ISaveChangesInterceptor` returns ZERO hits. The only refe
- **TS-04** — The API's entire write surface is unexercised: every PATCH integration test asserts only 401. Причина отклонения: The headline and the central evidentiary claim are contradicted by the files the finding itself cites. (1) "There is not one test that writes through the API and reads the row back" is false: FundraisingControllerShould.cs:74-127 (PutFundraising_AsAdmin_UpdatesAndPersistsValues) PUTs as admin, asserts the 200 payload, then re-reads via anonymous GET /v1/fundraising and asserts the persisted goalAm
- **TS-05** — Integration tests bypass the real authentication pipeline via a header-injection middleware. Причина отклонения: The mechanism exists as described, but every load-bearing number and impact claim in the finding is wrong, and the causal link between the header middleware and the alleged coverage hole does not hold.

(1) The central quantitative claim is fabricated. "24 of the 26 controller test classes use CreateAuthenticatedRequest" — actual count is 10 of 26 files, with 17 call sites total across 241 [Fact]/

## Средние и низкие

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] TS-08 — 219 of 1584 tests assert nothing but Moq interactions, with duplicated arrange blocks split one-Verify-per-test

**Статус: Открыто.** 

A parse of every [Fact]/[Theory] body across test/ gives 1365 tests with a real assertion (Should/Assert/FluentValidation helper) and 219 whose only assertion is `.Verify(...)`. The worst offender is test/DM.Domain.Game.Tests/Features/Inactivity/GameInactivityProcessorShould.cs (19 such tests): lines 76-140 contain five tests with byte-identical 6-line arrange blocks differing only in the final Verify — Create-per-game, SetInactivityWarning, SendAsync, IncrementAsync, system user id. test/DM.Domain.Game.Tests/Features/Games/GameServiceShould.cs:187-213 is the same pattern (AuthorizeUpdateGameAction and PublishEventWhenUpdatingGame are the same execution with a different Verify), and its constructor wires 20 mocks over 90 lines (46-138).

Последствие: Five tests cost five times the maintenance of one and detect exactly one behaviour between them: any change to the arrange block must be applied five times, which is how these files rot into copy-paste. More importantly, `_intentionManager.Verify(m => m.ThrowIfForbidden(GameIntention.Read, game))` proves the call was made, not that the authorization decision is correct — the mock is set up to do nothing (line 65-66). GameInactivityProcessor auto-warns and closes games (a destructive, user-visible action) and no test asserts idempotency, re-warning suppression, or behaviour when a game was already warned.

Исправление: Collapse the one-Verify-per-test families into a single test per behaviour asserting the full interaction set, and add the state-machine cases that are actually risky (already-warned games, already-closed games, empty batches with partial failures). The 20-mock GameService constructor is the real signal here — the test is telling you the service has too many responsibilities.

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] TS-09 — EF query correctness is untested: 3 of 69 repositories have tests, all on the InMemory provider

**Статус: Открыто.** 

src/DM.Infrastructure.Persistence contains 69 *Repository.cs files over 35,936 LOC. test/DM.Infrastructure.Persistence.Tests has repository tests for exactly three (BlogRepositoryShould.cs:30, PostRepositoryShould.cs:39, RoomRepositoryShould.cs:28), each using `.UseInMemoryDatabase(Guid.NewGuid().ToString())`. PostRepositoryShould.cs:46-52 documents the problem in its own comment: the production hydration step "relies on GroupBy shapes the EF Core InMemory provider cannot translate, so it is stubbed." Of that project's 62 tests, 23 are AutoMapper config assertions and 12 are validators — 27 test real persistence code. The integration suite has a real Postgres container available and does not use it for repository-level testing.

Последствие: The InMemory provider is LINQ-to-Objects: it cannot fail on untranslatable expressions, it does not apply relational NULL semantics, string collation, or index behaviour, and it silently succeeds on queries that throw at runtime against Npgsql. Every ordering, filtering and paging assertion made there is therefore evidence about LINQ, not about the query that ships. Given the project runs raw SQL and pg_trgm (DatabaseFixture.cs:72), the divergence is not theoretical.

Исправление: Move repository tests onto the existing Testcontainers Postgres fixture (they can share DatabaseFixture via the "Database" collection) and drop Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.InMemory from the project. Prioritise the repositories carrying non-trivial SQL: search, leaderboard/stats aggregation, unread counters, and the post rating/ordering queries.

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] TS-10 — The frontend coverage gate is set below current coverage so it can never fail

**Статус: Открыто.** 

src/DM.Web.Client/vite.config.ts:38-45 sets thresholds lines:7, functions:5, branches:5, statements:7, with the comment "Current coverage is ~7.65%, threshold set slightly below". CI runs `npm run test:coverage` (.github/workflows/dotnet.yml, frontend job), so the gate executes and passes unconditionally. The 844 tests live in 38 spec files against 325 .vue + 380 .ts source files, and are concentrated in shared/ui (BBCodeEditor 169 tests across 5 files, Filters 127 across 8) plus four Pinia stores; pages/ and widgets/ have 4 spec files between them.

Последствие: A threshold calibrated to current state is not a gate, it is a comment. New untested code cannot lower a 7% floor, so the number will drift downward as the frontend grows and CI will stay green. Combined with the e2e tier not running, the SPA effectively has no regression net outside shared/ui — which is precisely why 'still broken' recurs as an environment-staleness debugging pattern rather than a test failure.

Исправление: Set the threshold with `autoUpdate` or a ratcheting script so it tracks the high-water mark and fails on regression, and add per-directory thresholds so shared/ui's high coverage stops masking pages/ and widgets/ at ~0%.

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] TS-11 — No contract test binds the C# DTOs to the hand-written TypeScript models

**Статус: Открыто.** 

src/DM.Web.Client/src/shared/api/models contains hand-written TS interfaces (account, community, game, moderation, personal, …) with no codegen — package.json has no openapi/swagger generator and no generated-file markers exist. Frontend store tests mock the API module wholesale (src/entities/game/model/store.spec.ts:29-60 hoists 15 vi.fn() replacements for every endpoint). Backend integration tests assert JSON shape ad hoc with `JsonDocument` + `TryGetProperty` (e.g. BanControllerShould.cs:52-61). The two representations are maintained independently and nothing compares them.

Последствие: Renaming or retyping a field on a C# DTO passes all 1584 backend tests (the mapping profile still validates, the integration test still finds its own hand-listed properties) and all 844 frontend tests (the mock returns whatever the test author typed), then breaks at runtime. This is a structural blind spot at the highest-traffic boundary in the system, and it is the class of failure that manifests as 'the UI shows nothing and there is no error'.

Исправление: Emit the OpenAPI document at build time and either generate the TS models from it, or add a CI check that diffs the committed schema against the generated one so DTO changes must be acknowledged. Cheaper interim: a single integration test per aggregate that deserialises the response into the shape the client expects, so the property list lives in one place.

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] TS-12 — Test fixtures are copy-pasted across projects and have already diverged

**Статус: Исправлено.** AuthenticatedUserBuilder (5 копий) и Identity (4 копии) переехали в DM.Testing/Dsl одним экземпляром - объединением возможностей, а не пересечением: Community и Moderation не умели WithAccessPolicy (то есть не могли собрать забаненного), Account умел WithCredentials, Forum строил Settings через new UserSettings() вместо UserSettings.Default. Create.Game() заменен на new GameBuilder(), GameBuilder остался в своем проекте. Тип DSL переименован в Identities: он тенил производственный DM.Domain.Account.Features.Authentication.Identity, и после переезда в общую библиотеку столкновение стало возможно в любом тестовом проекте

test/DM.Testing (the shared test library) contains exactly one 14-line file. Meanwhile Dsl/AuthenticatedUserBuilder.cs exists in 5 domain test projects and diffs to nothing but the namespace line (verified Game vs Moderation: single-line diff). Dsl/Identity.cs exists in 4 projects and has already drifted: test/DM.Domain.Game.Tests/Dsl/Identity.cs:15 uses `Settings = UserSettings.Default` and adds two overloads, while test/DM.Domain.Forum.Tests/Dsl/Identity.cs:15 uses `Settings = new UserSettings()` with one overload. Dsl/Create.cs is duplicated 5 times.

Последствие: Two test projects now build identities with different default user settings, so a settings-sensitive authorization change will be caught in one module and missed in another for no reason a reader can see. Every fix to the builder must be applied 4-5 times, and the divergence proves that in practice it is not. DM.Testing exists precisely to hold this and is not being used.

Исправление: Move AuthenticatedUserBuilder, Identity and the Create entry point into DM.Testing (it already references Domain.Core transitively), delete the copies, and reconcile the UserSettings default to one value — the divergence should be resolved deliberately, not by picking whichever compiles.

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] TS-13 — E2E assertions are frequently vacuous: tautologies and assertions hidden behind visibility guards

**Статус: Открыто.** 

src/DM.Web.Client/e2e/tests/gaming/games-list.spec.ts:52 asserts `expect(count).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0)` on a locator count — mathematically incapable of failing, with the comment admitting it ("Just verify paging components are rendered (may be empty…)"). 43 assertions across the e2e suite sit inside `if (await …isVisible())` guards, several with `.catch(() => false)` so a locator error also silently skips the assertion (tooltip.spec.ts:134, 257; polls.spec.ts:16,28,32; comments.spec.ts:13,28; forum-index.spec.ts:30; characters.spec.ts:10). 49 assertions are bare `toBeTruthy()`/`toBe(true)`.

Последствие: These tests report as passing whether the feature works, is missing, or the page failed to load — the guard converts a failure into a pass. In a suite that already does not run in CI, this means even a manual run gives false assurance. It also trains the pattern: the guards exist because the tests depend on unpinned seeded data, so the fix people reach for is to make the assertion conditional.

Исправление: Delete the conditional guards; if a locator may legitimately be absent, assert which of the two states holds rather than skipping. Replace count>=0 style assertions with real expectations. Seed a deterministic e2e dataset so tests can assert on known content instead of defending against whatever happens to be in the dev database.

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] TS-14 — 15 of 26 authorization intention resolvers have no test

**Статус: Частично.** Замер подтвержден: 26 резолверов, 11 файлов тестов. Покрыты два кросс-модульных, которые находка называет самыми дорогими при ошибке: CommentIntentionResolver (через него авторизуются комментарии форума, блогов, публикаций и игр - инвертированная проверка тут это утечка сразу в четырех модулях) и UploadIntentionResolver (гейт модераторских страниц со списком чужих файлов). 27 тестов, включая границу роли (Mentor ниже Moderator), запрет лайкать свое даже администратору и закрытость ветки по умолчанию. Затем покрыт CharacterIntentionResolver - тринадцать защищенных веток, где смысл именно в защитах: мастер не редактирует лист игрока без явного разрешения, флаг PostEditAllowed не считается за EditAllowed, воскресить можно только реально погибшего, вернуться только ушедшему добровольно, а роль на сайте вообще не открывает чужого персонажа. 31 тест. Остаются непокрытыми 13 резолверов, включая RoomIntentionResolver

src/ contains 26 *IntentionResolver.cs files; test/ contains 11 *IntentionResolverShould.cs. Untested: PublicationIntentionResolver, CommunityIntentionResolver, UserEndorsementIntentionResolver, WebsiteTestimonialIntentionResolver, AttributeSchemaIntentionResolver, CharacterIntentionResolver, GameReviewIntentionResolver, NotepadIntentionResolver, PostReviewIntentionResolver, RoomIntentionResolver, GlobalChatEventIntentionResolver, NotificationIntentionResolver, CommentIntentionResolver (Infrastructure.Core), UploadIntentionResolver (Infrastructure.Core), BoardIntentionResolver is covered only under the misnamed ForumIntentionResolverShould. At the HTTP layer the gap is worse: only 6 Forbidden assertions exist across all 241 integration tests, and 53 of 77 controllers have no integration test at all.

Последствие: Resolvers are pure decision functions — the cheapest thing in the codebase to test and the most expensive to get wrong. CharacterIntentionResolver and RoomIntentionResolver gate private game content; CommentIntentionResolver and UploadIntentionResolver are cross-cutting and used by every module. An ownership check inverted in any of them is a silent data-exposure bug with no failing test.

Исправление: Mirror the GameIntentionResolverShould pattern for the remaining 15 — each is 10-15 pure-function cases with no mocks. Prioritise CharacterIntentionResolver, RoomIntentionResolver, CommentIntentionResolver and UploadIntentionResolver.

### [НИЗКАЯ] TS-15 — Mapping-profile tests pad the API test project and 24 of 26 carry a dead duplicated setup

**Статус: Исправлено.** Замер подтвердил находку точно: 26 файлов с полем _mapper, из них 24 его не используют. В 24 конструктор удален целиком, оставшиеся 2 объявляют MapperConfiguration один раз полем, а не строят дважды. Сами AssertConfigurationIsValid оставлены - они дешевы и ловят разрыв профиля

58 *MappingProfileShould.cs files produce 60 [Fact]s, essentially all `AssertConfigurationIsValid()`. They are 36 of the 68 tests in DM.Web.API.Tests (53%) and 23 of the 62 in DM.Infrastructure.Persistence.Tests (37%) — so 'the API unit test project' is mostly AutoMapper config validation, with 7 non-mapping files in it. The shape is also copy-pasted wrong: test/DM.Web.API.Tests/Features/Game/GameMappingProfileShould.cs builds a MapperConfiguration into an unused `_mapper` field in the constructor (15-29) and then rebuilds the identical configuration inside the test (34-43). 24 of the 26 files with an `_mapper` field never reference it.

Последствие: AssertConfigurationIsValid is cheap and worth having, but it only proves every destination member has a source — it asserts nothing about values, so a profile that maps the wrong source property passes. Counting these as a third of a test project's coverage overstates the protection on the API layer. The dead duplicated setup is pure copy-paste noise that a reader must decode 26 times.

Исправление: Delete the unused _mapper fields (or use them and add one value-level assertion per profile for the non-trivial members — the flattened/computed ones). Consider collapsing to a single test that walks every Profile in the assembly and asserts the combined configuration, which is stronger than 58 isolated ones because it catches cross-profile conflicts.

### [НИЗКАЯ] TS-16 — Test names overclaim relative to what they assert

**Статус: Исправлено.** Отладочный throw new Exception перед assert снят (два места). GetOwnGames_...ReturnsEmptyList теперь действительно проверяет пустоту массива. GetGames_WithQueryParameters слал size/number, которых API не принимает (реальные - skip/take), переименован и проверяет, что пагинация возвращает запрошенное. Все шесть BeOneOf заменены на детерминированный исход, проверено прогоном: неверный GUID в маршруте дает 400, неизвестный блог 404, поиск без движка 503, активного события чата в фикстуре нет - 204

GameControllerShould.cs:64-71 `GetOwnGames_WhenNotAuthenticated_ReturnsEmptyList` asserts only `StatusCode.Should().Be(OK)` — it never inspects the body for emptiness. GameControllerShould.cs:41-55 `GetGames_WithQueryParameters_ReturnsOk` asserts nothing about paging. GlobalChatControllerShould.cs:95 `GetActiveGlobalChatEvent_WhenNotAuthenticated_ReturnsOkOrNoContent` uses `BeOneOf` to accept either outcome (6 such assertions across the suite). GameControllerShould.cs:31-35 also throws a raw Exception before the assertion as a debugging leftover.

Последствие: Names are the index into what a suite covers. When a name promises a behaviour the body does not check, later readers assume coverage exists and skip adding it — the false confidence is worse than the missing test. BeOneOf assertions in particular pass in both the working and broken state.

Исправление: Either assert what the name says (parse the body, assert the resources array is empty) or rename to match. Replace BeOneOf with a deterministic expectation by controlling the seed data. Remove the throw-before-assert debugging block.


---

# Архитектура фронтенда (FSD) — C+

The FSD skeleton is unusually well-disciplined for 700+ files (only ~10 real import violations, correct `@x` cross-import convention, public API per slice), but the data layer under it is the weak half: singleton "current entity" stores, eight hand-rolled caches with no mutation-invalidation, zero request cancellation, and hand-maintained DTO mirrors that have already silently drifted from the C# contract and produced a live UI bug.

## Что сделано хорошо

- Layer discipline is real, not aspirational. A full import scan of src/ (all `@/`-aliased and relative imports across 697 .ts/.vue files) found exactly ONE upward import (shared/api/client.ts:61 -> @/app/providers/router), 4 feature->feature imports, 1 widget->widget, and 6 deep imports bypassing a slice's index.ts. Of 7 relative imports that cross a directory, all 7 are inside app/. For a codebase this size that is a top-decile result.
- FSD v2.1 `@x` cross-import convention is actually used, not just named. entities/user/@x/game.ts and entities/user/@x/testimonial.ts are explicit, documented one-way doors, and entities/game/ui/CharacterCard.vue|PostReviewItem.vue|RoomLink.vue import through them rather than reaching into the sibling slice. Every entity/feature slice has an index.ts public API (150+ of them).
- URL-as-SSOT filtering is a genuinely good abstraction. shared/lib/composables/createFilterDispatcher.ts implements a reducer + 50ms-debounced navigation so rapid filter clicks batch into one route push, and filter state is *computed from the URL* rather than mirrored in a store (widgets/games-table/GamesDataTable.vue:30 comment "filterState is computed from URL (single source of truth, no sync needed)"). Consequence: table widgets take zero props and there is no store/URL desync class of bug at all.
- The persistent-shell routing pattern is deliberate and correctly reasoned. All three forum levels are children of one route record so ForumPage.vue keeps a single live BoardNavigation instance (router.ts:256-266, ForumPage.vue:1-10), and shell/leaf error coordination uses a typed provide/inject token (pages/forum/forumShell.ts) instead of a global — one of only two provide/inject uses in the whole app, and it is the right one.
- Router-level resilience that most SPAs skip: chunk-load-error recovery with a sessionStorage reload-loop guard (router.ts:884-938), hash deep-linking that defers to CommentsList's own handler rather than fighting it (router.ts:890-908), and document.title only reset on genuine route change so dynamic titles survive pagination (router.ts:918-920).
- Component contracts are uniformly modern: 153 files use type-based `defineProps<...>()`, zero use the runtime object form; 67 use typed `defineEmits<>`; shared/ui/DataTable/DataTable.vue:48-52 declares typed template-literal slots (`cell-${string}`). Prop drilling is essentially absent because widgets read stores + route directly.
- createRequestGuard (shared/lib/utils/requestGuard.ts) is a clean SSOT extraction of the stale-response counter, with the extraction rationale documented — the pattern is right where it is applied.
- Comment quality is exceptional and load-bearing: nearly every non-obvious decision (why v-show not v-else in ForumPage.vue:103-105, why onMounted not immediate-watch in OwnedGames.vue:167-172, why moderation-zone pages are top-level records in router.ts:430-434) states the *why*, not the what.

## Подтвержденные находки

### [ВЫСОКАЯ] FE-01 — Hand-maintained DTO mirror has silently drifted from the C# contract; master/mentor games are dropped from the sidebar

**Статус: Исправлено.** Клиент зеркалил не тот энум: participation типизировался доменным GameRole (Master/Assistant/Mentor), тогда как по проводу идут флаги GameParticipation (Owner/Authority/Moderator). Пересекались ровно два имени, поэтому игры мастера не попадали ни в один из четырех бакетов сайдбара и блок Мои игры выходил пустым - причем без надписи про отсутствие игр, потому что список был непустой. Введен GameParticipation по образцу проводного контракта, семь потребителей переведены на него, два места, где расхождение уже обходили кастом as unknown as string[], избавились от каста. Добавлен регрессионный тест на разбор флагов

**Доказательство.** src/DM.Web.Client/src/entities/game/model/types.ts:105 declares `participation: Served<GameRole[]>` where GameRole = Master|Assistant|Mentor|Player|Reader|Applicant (types.ts:32-40). The server actually serializes `IEnumerable<GameParticipation>` (src/DM.Web.API/Features/Game/Games/GameRef.cs:57), whose values are Owner|Authority|PendingAssistant|Player|Reader|Moderator (Game.cs:13-47), flattened by GameParticipationResolver.cs:68-72 and stringified globally by JsonConfiguration.cs:31. So the wire payload is e.g. ["Owner","Authority"]. widgets/sidebar/OwnedGames.vue:128-134 filters that array with `GameRole.Master`/`GameRole.Assistant`/`GameRole.Mentor` — strings that can never appear. entities/game/model/store.ts:357-358 works around the same lie with `game.value?.participation as unknown as string[]` and then compares against the *correct* wire values ("Owner", "Authority", "Moderator"

**Последствие.** A game master or mentor opening the site sees their own games missing from the left-sidebar "Мои игры": hasAnyOwnerRole is false, hasRole(Player) is false, hasRole(Reader) is false, so the game falls into none of the four render groups and is silently dropped. Beyond this instance, the whole 4.5k-line hand-written model set (shared/api/models/** plus entities/*/model/types.ts) is unverifiable against the backend — every C# DTO rename is a silent runtime break that TypeScript reports as fully type-safe.

**Исправление.** Generate the client model layer from the running Swagger document (NSwag/openapi-typescript) into a checked-in `shared/api/generated`, fail CI on drift, and delete the hand-written mirrors. As an immediate patch: replace GameRole in GameRef.participation with a GameParticipation union matching the flags enum, and rewrite OwnedGames.vue:128-134 against it (Owner/Authority/Moderator), reusing the isMaster/isAssistant/isMentor computeds already in entities/game/model/store.ts:359-366 rather than re-deriving them.

**Проверка оппонентом.** Evidence holds on both sides of the wire. Server: GameRef.Participation is IEnumerable<GameParticipation> (src/DM.Web.API/Features/Game/Games/GameRef.cs:57), mapped via .ForMember(d => d.Participation, s => s.MapFrom<GameParticipationResolver>()) (GameMappingProfile.cs:73); the resolver ORs Owner|Authority for the master and flattens the flags enum into individual members (GameParticipationResolver.cs:47-71) whose names are Owner/Authority/PendingAssistant/Player/Reader/Moderator (Game.cs:12-49), stringified by JsonConfiguration.cs:31. The sidebar calls GET games?participating=true&projection=ref (entities/game/api/gameApi.ts:210-215) which routes to GameApiService.GetRefs -> _mapper.Map<GameRef> (GameApiService.cs:44-48), so a master's payload is ["Owner","Authority"]. Client: types.ts:105 types it Served<GameRole[]> with GameRole = None|Reader|Applicant|Player|Mentor|Assistant|Master (

### [ВЫСОКАЯ] FE-02 — Detail stores are global singletons for "the current entity", so cross-entity navigation renders one game's title over another game's data

**Статус: Исправлено.** Стор деталей остался одним мешком на текущую сущность, но переход между сущностями теперь чистит его первым делом. Причина поломки конкретнее формулировки находки: /game/A/... и /game/B/... делят одну запись маршрута, поэтому оболочка не размонтируется и reset в onUnmounted при смене id не срабатывает вовсе - посты, персонажи, комментарии, блокнот и черный список предыдущей игры оставались на экране под новым заголовком, пока каждая подстраница не перезапросит свое. То же самое применено к оболочке блога

**Доказательство.** entities/game/model/store.ts:290-345 defines `useGameDetailsStore` as a single flat bag: game/rooms/currentRoom/posts/characters/comments/chatRooms/notepad/blacklist/users, each with its own `X`, `XLoading`, `XError` triple (27 refs). It is not keyed by game id. pages/game/GamePage.vue:19-31 reloads only `loadGame`+`loadRooms` when `p.id` changes, and calls `gameStore.reset()` only in `onUnmounted` (GamePage.vue:33-35) — but /game/A/... and /game/B/... share one route record, so the shell is never unmounted and reset never runs on an id change. pages/game/GameRoom.vue:83-98 watches only `p.num` and `q.number`, never `p.id`. entities/blog/model/store.ts has the identical shape for blogs.

**Последствие.** Navigating /game/A/rooms/1 -> /game/B/rooms/1 (a normal sidebar click) renders game B's H1 with game A's posts, and they are never refetched because the room number did not change. Characters, comments, notepad and blacklist likewise carry over from the previous game until each sub-page happens to refetch. This is stale-data-across-tenants, not just a flicker.

**Исправление.** Either key the detail store by entity id (`Map<GameId, GameDetails>` with an `activeId`), or make GamePage.vue call `gameStore.reset()` at the top of the `p.id` watcher callback before loading. The narrow fix for the visible bug is to add `p.id` to GameRoom.vue's param strategies; the structural fix is the keyed cache, otherwise the same class of bug returns for every new sub-page added under /game/:id.

**Проверка оппонентом.** Core mechanism verified end to end. (1) `useGameDetailsStore` (D:\Code Projects\dm3\src\DM.Web.Client\src\entities\game\model\store.ts:299-347) is a flat, unkeyed singleton with 27 refs; `reset()` (store.ts:714-753) is called from exactly one place in the app — `onUnmounted` in GamePage.vue:33-35 (grep for `.reset()` returns only GamePage.vue:34 and BlogPage.vue:59). (2) router.ts:596-670 defines ONE parent record `/game/:id` whose `page` component is GamePage, with all sub-pages as children; App.vue:18 renders `<router-view name="page" />` with no `:key`, GamePage.vue:44 renders the child `<router-view />` with no `:key`, and there is no store-resetting global guard (router.ts:869-889 only handles auth redirect, expandable registry, hash scroll). So an in-zone id change reuses GamePage and reset() never fires — the p.id watcher (GamePage.vue:23-31) refreshes ONLY `game` and `rooms`, lea

### [ВЫСОКАЯ] FE-04 — Eight independent hand-rolled caches with five different invalidation models and no link between mutations and cached lists

**Статус: Частично.** Закрыта та часть, которую пользователи реально ловят: создание игры и удаление игры или блога больше не оставляют список протухшим. Создание шло мимо стора прямо в API-клиент, поэтому ни кеш поиска, ни participating не трогались - новой игры не было ни в /games, ни в сайдбаре, и заставить обновиться было нечем, кроме ожидания. Переписывание слоя кеширования (девять самописных кешей, пять моделей инвалидации) не делалось: это долг, а не поломка, и на этой аудитории выигрыш от единого query-слоя чисто эстетический

**Доказательство.** (1) shared/lib/composables/useApiResource.ts — TTL + SWR; (2) entities/game/model/store.ts:76-101,152-157 — Map keyed by sorted params, 30s TTL, keep-last-20 eviction; (3) entities/blog/model/store.ts:46-49 — same Map, declared at MODULE level, outside defineStore; (4) entities/user/model/communityStore.ts:42-46 — same, also module level; (5) entities/forum/model/store.ts:108 and :333 — two more Maps; (6) entities/game/model/pulseStore.ts:87-94; (7) entities/game/model/ratedPostsStore.ts:23,64,108,150 — three parallel timestamp schemes in one file; (8) shared/stores/testimonials.ts:24-25,47-48 — single-slot lastQueryKey+lastFetchTime. Nothing invalidates on mutation: features/create-game/ui/CreateGameForm.vue:104 calls `gameApi.createGame` directly, bypassing the store, so neither the 30s games searchCache nor the 60s `participating` useApiResource is touched; entities/blog/model/store.t

**Последствие.** Create a game, then open /games or reload the sidebar: for up to 30-60s the new game is absent, with no way for the user to force a refresh other than waiting. Same for delete. Debugging a staleness report means figuring out which of eight cache implementations is responsible. Module-scope caches make store tests order-dependent.

**Исправление.** This is the point where a query cache earns its keep — TanStack Query (@tanstack/vue-query) already ships as a sibling of the @tanstack/vue-virtual dependency in package.json. Adopt it for list/detail reads with real query keys, and replace all eight caches; mutations then declare `invalidateQueries` and the create/delete staleness class disappears. If a dependency is unacceptable, at minimum collapse to useApiResource + a single keyed cache primitive and move every mutation behind a store action that invalidates.

**Проверка оппонентом.** Every cited location checks out line-for-line: useApiResource.ts:44-128 (TTL+SWR), game/model/store.ts:76-80,152-157 (keyed Map, 30s TTL, keep-20), blog/model/store.ts:46-49 and user/model/communityStore.ts:42-46 (both Maps declared at MODULE level, outside defineStore; the community one has no eviction at all), forum/model/store.ts:108 and :333, pulseStore.ts:87-94, ratedPostsStore.ts:23,64,108,150, testimonials.ts:24-25,47-48. The mutation-bypass claims are also exact: CreateGameForm.vue:104 calls gameApi.createGame then router.push at :125 without touching any store; CreateBlogForm.vue:74 is identical; blog store deleteBlog (:614-618) returns !error with no cache clear; resetAllGames/resetAllBlogs/clearSearchCache have callers only in .spec.ts. There is no central alternative: shared/api/client.ts:25 sends Cache-Control: no-cache, so no HTTP-level dedup exists either. Two parts of the

## Опровергнуто оппонентом (в работу не берем)

- **FE-03** — No request cancellation anywhere in the client; race protection is opt-in and applied to a minority of fetch sites. Причина отклонения: Refuted as stated: the raw greps are accurate but the causal chain and severity are wrong, and the two artifacts the finding leans on carry no risk. (1) useApiResource is fingered as "the shared fetch primitive backing the games, blogs, forum, poll and subscription stores", yet every single call site passes a PARAMETERLESS fetcher — entities/blog/model/store.ts:72-79, entities/game/model/store.ts:

## Средние и низкие

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] FE-05 — useApiResource's stale-while-revalidate and in-flight guard are both incorrect

**Статус: Исправлено.** Обе половины подтвердились и обе починены. Ин-флайт гейт отдавал уже разрешенный промис вместо присоединения к летящему запросу, поэтому await fetch() возвращался до появления данных - три сайдбарных блока читают состояние сразу после await и решают по нему 'запрос упал', то есть рисовали ошибку с кнопкой Повторить на полностью исправном запросе. Теперь второй вызов присоединяется к тому же промису. SWR-ветка и блокирующий путь писали результат без проверки актуальности: медленный первый ответ затирал свежий форсированный, а ответ, пришедший после reset(), воскрешал данные предыдущего пользователя при выходе. Обе ветки закрыты существующим SSOT createRequestGuard, reset() двигает счетчик. loading поднимается синхронно до первого await - на это опирается существующий тест хранилища опросов. Публичный контракт композабла не менялся. Четыре теста на все три сценария

shared/lib/composables/useApiResource.ts:105-109 — on the awaited path, an error sets `error.value` AND `data.value = null`, discarding the cached data the SWR contract promised to keep (contrast entities/game/model/store.ts:143-148, which explicitly keeps stale data on error). Line 68: `if (fetchInProgress && !force) return;` — forced fetches bypass the guard entirely, so two concurrent `fetch(true)` calls both run with no request id and last-writer-wins. Line 73-95: the background-revalidate branch swallows failures into `console.warn` and never surfaces them. This composable backs the games, blogs, forum, poll and subscription stores.

Последствие: A transient network blip on a background refresh blanks an already-rendered list to its empty state. Two components mounting simultaneously and both calling `fetch(true)` (a normal pattern for a page + its sidebar panel) race with no ordering guarantee.

Исправление: On the awaited path keep `data.value` when a previous value exists and only set `error`; add a request counter (createRequestGuard is already in shared/lib/utils) so late responses are dropped; make `force` join the in-flight promise instead of starting a second request.

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] FE-06 — Domain API surface is split across three layers with no rule, and the reason is documented as team ownership rather than architecture

**Статус: Открыто.** 

shared/api holds 12 domain clients totalling ~2 300 lines (moderationApi.ts 672, communityApi.ts 283, accountApi.ts 272, supportApi.ts 143, achievementApi.ts 144, notepadApi.ts 163, personalApi.ts 97, subscriptionApi.ts 58, notificationApi.ts 57, blacklistApi.ts 51, uploadApi.ts 73, fundraisingApi.ts 33), while entities/*/api holds 6 more totalling ~1 840 lines (gameApi 731, blogApi 398, globalChatApi 263, forumApi 230, messagingApi 153, pollApi 64). A third location exists: features/moderation-actions/api.ts:6-10 states verbatim that shared/api/moderationApi.ts "is owned by the moderation-pages work stream, so this feature talks to the shared Api client directly (same pattern as features/message-search/api)".

Последствие: There is no answer to "where does the call for X live" other than grep, and the moderation endpoints now have two client implementations that can diverge. The shared layer, which by FSD definition must be domain-agnostic, currently owns the majority of the domain vocabulary — which is why shared/stores also had to grow domain stores (see FE-07).

Исправление: One rule, applied mechanically: every domain client lives in `entities/<slice>/api`; shared/api keeps only the transport (client.ts, envelope.ts, audience.ts) and truly cross-domain primitives. Split moderationApi.ts by target entity and delete the feature-local duplicate.

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] FE-07 — Three entity data stores live in shared/stores, splitting slices across layers

**Статус: Открыто.** 

shared/stores/ contains testimonials.ts (97 lines), statistics.ts (98, including a setInterval poll at :69) and subscriptions.ts (132) alongside the legitimately-global auth.ts and ui.ts. Meanwhile entities/testimonial/index.ts exports only `./ui` — the slice's model half is in shared. The consumers are all higher layers (features/create-testimonial, features/testimonial-delete, pages/about/TestimonialsPage.vue, pages/home/RandomTestimonials.vue), so nothing forces the placement. entities/user/model/store.ts is a 5-line file whose entire content is a re-export of shared/stores' auth store.

Последствие: Deleting or refactoring the testimonial feature requires touching two layers; the shared layer accumulates domain state, which is exactly the pressure that produced FE-06. New contributors reasonably conclude "shared/stores is where stores go" and the entity model layer keeps hollowing out.

Исправление: Move testimonials/statistics/subscriptions into entities/testimonial/model, entities/statistics/model (new slice) and entities/subscription/model, re-exporting through each slice index.ts. Keep only auth and ui in shared/stores.

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] FE-08 — The single upward import inverts the shared->app boundary and is patched with a dynamic import to hide the cycle it creates

**Статус: Открыто.** 

shared/api/client.ts:56-62 — inside the 401 interceptor: `// Lazy import to avoid circular dependency` then `const router = (await import("@/app/providers/router")).default; router.push({ name: "home" });`. The comment names the problem it is working around. The same interceptor also directly manipulates auth state (`localStorage.removeItem("user")`, client.ts:57) rather than going through shared/stores/auth.ts, whose `updateUser(null)` (auth.ts:41-48) additionally resets the theme and is what every other logout path calls.

Последствие: The transport layer knows about route names and session storage keys, so it cannot be tested or reused without the router, and the cycle is only invisible because the import is deferred to runtime. On a 401 the user's theme is not reset while their session is, and the auth store's `user` ref is left non-null until something else re-reads localStorage — the header can keep showing a logged-in state after the session died.

Исправление: Have the interceptor emit an event / call an injected `onUnauthenticated` callback registered by app/providers, and route the state change through useAuthStore().updateUser(null). shared then depends on nothing above it.

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] FE-09 — Router repeats the shell in 57 route records instead of using one layout route; omission is silent

**Статус: Открыто.** 

app/providers/router.ts contains `left: LeftSidebar, right: RightSidebar` 57 times across ~830 lines of route table — roughly 230 lines of pure repetition. LeftSidebar/RightSidebar are statically imported (router.ts:4) so they are in the initial chunk, while every page is lazy. One record omits the pair — `auth-callback` at router.ts:690-696 declares only `page` — and nothing distinguishes that deliberate choice from a mistake.

Последствие: Every new route is an opportunity to ship a page with no navigation, discoverable only by looking at it. Any change to the shell (adding a third sidebar slot, a breadcrumb region) is a 57-site edit. The route table is also unreadable at this length.

Исправление: One top-level route record rendering an AppLayout component that owns the named views, with all current records as its children; routes that genuinely want a bare shell opt out via a sibling record or `meta.bare`.

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] FE-10 — Page layer holds widget-scale logic while the features layer sits unused for the same interactions

**Статус: Открыто.** 

pages/global-chat/GlobalChatPage.vue is 1 739 lines with a 1 099-line `<script setup>` containing ~55 functions: infinite scroll setup/teardown, hover-toolbar show/hide across mouse+focus, a setInterval poll (line 646) running alongside SignalR, permission predicates (canEditMessage/canDeleteMessage/canLikeMessage, lines 825-837), edit/like/delete/warn handlers, archive-date navigation, image replace/restore, and `function isOnline(author: any)` at line 817. pages/profile/ProfilePage.vue is 1 348 lines (522 script). This coexists with a features layer that already contains slices for comparable interactions (poll-vote, block-user, user-subscribe, testimonial-delete). Related shape problems: shared/ui/BBCodeEditor/BBCodeEditor.vue is 1 938 lines (917 script) inside shared/ui; widgets/sidebar is a flat 30-file mega-slice holding two distinct widgets (LeftSidebar and RightSidebar) plus every zone panel, with no ui/model segmentation.

Последствие: None of this logic is reachable, testable or reusable outside its page — global-chat message actions duplicate concepts that features/comment and features/moderation-actions already own. The chat page cannot be code-split meaningfully and is effectively single-owner code.

Исправление: Extract the chat interaction set into features (chat-message-actions, chat-archive-nav) and the scroll/poll machinery into entities/global-chat/model composables, leaving the page as composition. Split widgets/sidebar into widgets/left-sidebar and widgets/right-sidebar with ui/model segments.

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] FE-11 — Error handling has four competing idioms, and one page issues a second network request to recover a status code the store discarded

**Статус: Открыто.** 

pages/profile/ProfilePage.vue:86-95: after `communityStore.trySelectProfile(name)` returns false, the page calls `communityApi.getUserProfile(name)` a second time purely to read `error?.status` — the in-code comment says "The store collapses every failure to a boolean". The four idioms: (a) global toasts in the axios interceptor for 401/403/429/5xx (shared/api/client.ts:52-96); (b) ad-hoc toasts at ~113 call sites in pages/widgets/features; (c) per-resource `xError` string refs in stores rendered via ErrorState (40 files); (d) useAsyncAction (5 files), which catches thrown Errors even though Api never throws — so call sites must convert `{error}` into `throw new Error("Не удалось изменить пароль")` (pages/account/sections/AccountSecuritySection.vue:186), discarding the server's title and invalidProperties.

Последствие: A wrong current password and a rate-limit both render the identical generic string. Stores throw away HTTP status, so distinguishing 404 from 500 costs an extra round trip. Adding a new page means picking one of four conventions with no guidance.

Исправление: Store the `GeneralError` object (not a boolean or a hardcoded string) on every store's error ref — the type already exists at shared/api/models/common/index.ts:57-63 — and let ErrorState/ErrorPage map status to presentation. Delete useAsyncAction or reimplement it over ApiResult so no call site has to fabricate an Error.

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] FE-12 — useFetchData fires at most one callback per change batch and compares array-valued query params by reference

**Статус: Не проблема.** Проверка развела половины. Первая (break, один коллбэк на пачку изменений) - факт, но это намеренный контракт, а не дефект: мест, где в одном watch больше одной стратегии, ровно два (BlogFeed и TopicPage), в обоих коллбэки идентичны и перечитывают состояние из маршрута целиком. Снятие break, как предлагала находка, дало бы два одинаковых запроса и гонку устаревших ответов при переходе по ссылке на другую рубрику со страницы 3. Вторая половина (сравнение массивов по ссылке) не воспроизводится: ни один геттер во всем клиенте не возвращает массив, все восемь построителей query возвращают Record<string,string>, повторяющихся ключей в URL приложение не пишет. Контракт записан в докблоке композабла

shared/lib/composables/useFetchData.ts:36-41 and :52-57 — both watchers loop over the strategy list and `break` after the first index whose value differs. Values come from `route.params`/`route.query`, so `LocationQueryValue[]` entries (multi-value query params, which this app uses — client.ts:39-42 configures the serializer for repeated keys) are compared with `!==` on a freshly built array and therefore always look changed.

Последствие: A navigation that changes two watched params at once silently under-fetches the second resource, which is invisible until a specific route shape appears. Array query params either always refire or never do, depending on whether vue-router reuses the array identity.

Исправление: Drop the `break` and fire every strategy whose value changed; compare with a shallow-array-aware equality for the query case. Both are three-line changes in a composable with 17 call sites.

### [НИЗКАЯ] FE-13 — No automated enforcement of the layer rules the codebase actually follows

**Статус: Открыто.** 

.eslintrc.cjs extends only `plugin:vue/vue3-essential` (the weakest Vue preset), eslint:recommended, @vue/eslint-config-typescript and prettier. There is no steiger, no eslint-plugin-boundaries, no `import/no-restricted-paths`, and no FSD config file anywhere in src/DM.Web.Client. The 10 violations found in this review (shared->app, 4 feature->feature, 1 widget->widget, 6 deep imports past index.ts) were all introduced without any signal.

Последствие: Discipline this good is currently one distracted afternoon away from erosion, and the violations that exist are invisible in review. `vue3-essential` also misses the ordering/naming rules that would catch several of the smells above.

Исправление: Add steiger (the official FSD linter) or eslint-plugin-boundaries with the six-layer rank plus a public-API rule, run it in the existing .github/workflows/dotnet.yml frontend job, and baseline the 10 known violations so only new ones fail. Move to `plugin:vue/vue3-recommended` at the same time.

### [НИЗКАЯ] FE-14 — Deep imports and feature->feature imports bypass the public APIs the project otherwise maintains

**Статус: Открыто.** 

Bypassing a slice's index.ts: widgets/sidebar/GameTagCloud.vue -> @/entities/game/model/store; features/user-filter/model/types.ts and widgets/users-table/UsersDataTable.vue -> @/entities/user/model/communityStore; pages/game/CharacterCreate.vue, CharacterEdit.vue and GameSettings.vue -> @/features/attribute-schema-editor/model. Same-layer imports without an @x door: features/comment/model/useCommentWarnDialog.ts -> @/features/moderation-actions; features/create-game/ui/CreateGameForm.vue -> @/features/attribute-schema-editor; features/publication/ui/PublicationCard.vue -> @/features/topic; features/topic/ui/TopicView.vue -> @/features/leaderboard; widgets/pulse-feed/PulseFeed.vue -> @/widgets/game-post.

Последствие: The public APIs stop being a refactoring boundary — renaming entities/user/model/communityStore.ts breaks two unrelated slices despite the entity exporting `useCommunityStore` from its index. The feature->feature edges also point at a taxonomy problem: `topic`, `publication`, `comment`, `roster` are nouns, i.e. entity/widget material misfiled as features, which is why they need to import each other.

Исправление: Route the six deep imports through the existing index.ts exports (all six symbols are already exported). For the same-layer edges, either add @x doors (the convention is already established in entities/user/@x/) or reclassify: move TopicCard/PublicationCard/CommentItem to entities|widgets, which dissolves three of the four feature->feature edges outright.

### [НИЗКАЯ] FE-15 — Deprecated aliases and legacy identifiers are shipped through the public APIs rather than removed

**Статус: Открыто.** 

shared/stores/auth.ts:136-137 marks `useUserStore` `@deprecated`, yet it is re-exported from shared/stores/index.ts:9, from entities/user/index.ts, and even through the sanctioned cross-import door at entities/user/@x/game.ts:9 — and used in 60 files including app/App.vue:133, widgets/header/Header.vue:2 and widgets/sidebar/LeftSidebar.vue:41. entities/user/model/store.ts is a 5-line deprecation shim. The auth store's Pinia id is still `"root"` (auth.ts:21). shared/api/index.ts:37-45 exports every client twice (PascalCase and camelCase) "for backward compatibility", and shared/api/models/community/user-settings.ts:2 re-exports Theme/Paging/Preferences from ../personal for the same stated reason while ../personal/index.ts:10 re-exports UserSettings back from it.

Последствие: Two names for the same store means grep-based reasoning about auth usage is unreliable, and the deprecation can never be completed because the @x door itself depends on it. The mutual model re-exports mean a symbol's home is ambiguous. Devtools shows the session store as "root".

Исправление: One codemod: replace `useUserStore` with `useAuthStore` in all 60 files, delete the alias, the entities/user/model/store.ts shim, and the camelCase duplicates in shared/api/index.ts; give the store id `"auth"`. Pick one home for Theme/Paging/Preferences and make the other side import, not re-export.
