# Качество фронтенда: дизайн-система, a11y, производительность — B-

A genuinely real design system (near-total token discipline — 6 hardcoded hex values across 325 .vue files — plus button/input/link mixins, z-index/breakpoint/animation scales and a fully code-split router) undermined by four measured, shipping-quality defects: 360 KB of TipTap pulled onto the critical path by an FSD barrel, 11.5% of all CSS duplicated by a side-effectful mixin file, a broken DataTable virtualization branch reachable from a user setting, and core tokens that fail the project's own documented 4.5:1 contrast rule.

## Что сделано хорошо

- Token discipline is close to total, and rare. Across 325 .vue files there are exactly 6 hardcoded hex colors (2 of them in the dev-only page, 2 in comments) and 7 `color: white` keywords — everything else flows through src/DM.Web.Client/src/assets/styles/ThemeVariables.css → Themes.sass. The overlay alphas are not eyeballed: ThemeVariables.css:65-78 and :174-183 carry the actual composition arithmetic showing the rest/hover delta holds on --bg-page, --bg-element and --bg-element-accent alike. That is design-system engineering, not theming by vibe.
- Route-level code splitting is complete — every one of ~70 route records in src/DM.Web.Client/src/app/providers/router.ts uses a dynamic import, including nested game/blog/forum/moderation children. It also ships a stale-chunk self-heal (router.ts:926-938) that reloads once on ChunkLoadError with a sessionStorage loop guard, which is an operational detail most teams discover only after a bad deploy.
- src/DM.Web.Client/src/entities/user/ui/AvatarImg.vue is exemplary: srcset/sizes for DPR-aware variant selection, width/height attributes for CLS, `eager` → fetchpriority=high for the LCP avatar, a theme-aware SVG fallback, and a `noDefault` mode for characters. It is the SSOT — the entire app contains only 13 raw `<img>` tags.
- The filter subsystem is the strongest architectural choice in the frontend: URL query is the single source of truth, state is derived via computed, and every mutation goes through a pure reducer over a discriminated-union action type, dispatched through one shared debouncer (shared/lib/composables/createFilterDispatcher.ts). Eight filter modules follow it identically, and src/assets/styles/_Filters.sass:7-15 documents its own color rules so the shared chrome cannot drift by accident.
- Realtime is handled with more care than usual: useSignalR.ts:122-242 implements lease-based ownership of one shared socket (App.vue holds "app", GlobalChatPage holds its own named lease), re-negotiates when the auth context changes under a held lease, and serializes connect/disconnect through a promise queue. Both unread-count stores debounce SignalR-triggered fetches by 2s (entities/message/model/store.ts:222-238, entities/notification/model/store.ts:12-27) — actual storm protection, not a comment about it.
- Chat lists at scale are virtualized: shared/lib/composables/useVirtualScroll.ts wraps @tanstack/vue-virtual with reactive options and dynamic measurement, and is used by both pages/messenger/ChatView.vue:84 and pages/global-chat/GlobalChatPage.vue:485, combined with IntersectionObserver-based bidirectional paging.
- The reveal/animation layer is a real system, not per-component transitions: one duration+easing pair for every expand on the site (_Animations.sass:21-27), two global idioms (.expand-zone height-pin and .expand-fold grid-rows, Reset.sass:96-117), a cross-page expand/collapse-all registry, and a prefers-reduced-motion override that uses 0.01ms rather than 0 so transitionend still fires (Reset.sass:199-205).
- The one v-html helper used in 12 call sites is safe by construction: shared/lib/utils/highlight.ts:26-48 escapes every split part before wrapping matches in `<mark>`, and highlightDom() operates on text nodes only. For a codebase with 25 v-html usages, that is the right place to have been careful.
- Accessibility is present where it was deliberately engineered: shared/ui/Select/Select.vue keeps a native `<select>` under appearance:none rather than rebuilding a listbox; entities/user/ui/UserAutocomplete.vue implements the full combobox/listbox/option + aria-activedescendant pattern; shared/ui/Tooltip/Tooltip.vue fires on focusin/focusout with aria-describedby and role=tooltip; shared/ui/ConfirmDialog/ConfirmDialog.vue has a working Tab trap plus focus restore on close and unmount.

## Подтвержденные находки

### [ВЫСОКАЯ] FE-01 — TipTap (360 KB) is on the critical path of every page load, dragged in by an FSD barrel re-export

**Статус: Открыто.** Не закрыто, но диагноз находки уточнен двумя проверенными опровержениями. (1) Цепочка не через CharacterSheetFields: перевод его импорта редактора на defineAsyncComponent не сдвинул стартовый чанк ни на байт (430.64 КБ до и после), то есть барьер-реэкспорт entities/game не является причиной. (2) Настоящая причина видна в составе чанка: в стартовый попадают SpoilerView и NsfwView - вью-компоненты tiptap-расширений, которые Rollup поднимает как общую зависимость нескольких ленивых страниц, и вместе с ними приезжает статический импорт всей библиотеки. Попытка лечить это функциональной формой manualChunks (редактор в один чанк с библиотекой) уменьшила стартовый чанк с 430 до 298 КБ, но tiptap остался в modulepreload, то есть с критического пути не ушел, а топология обзавелась лишним чанком роутера - изменение откачено как не достигающее цели. Правильное лечение, судя по всему, в том, чтобы вью-нод-вью расширений не были достижимы из общего графа, но это переделка структуры редактора

**Доказательство.** dist/index.html emits `<link rel="modulepreload" href="/assets/tiptap-D1akJhCD.js">`, and dist/assets/index-C2fMmdbK.js contains a top-level static `import{n as Al,N as $l,a as Pl,b as Fn,m as Wt,V as Ol,M as Cs}from"./tiptap-D1akJhCD.js"`. I traced the eager import closure from main.ts: app/main.ts → app/App.vue:142 → widgets/sidebar/index.ts → widgets/sidebar/OwnedGames.vue:119 (`import { useGamesStore, GameRole, type GameRef } from "@/entities/game"`) → entities/game/index.ts (which re-exports `./ui` at the bottom) → entities/game/ui/index.ts → entities/game/ui/CharacterSheetFields.vue:29 → shared/ui/BBCodeEditor → @tiptap/*. Measured: tiptap chunk 360,150 B raw / 114,380 B gzip; entry JS 426,269 B / 137,964 B gzip; entry CSS 199,571 B / 25,533 B gzip. vite.config.ts:66-68 states the opposite in a comment: "TipTap editor - загружается только на chat/messenger". package.json has no `"s

**Последствие.** Every visitor — including a logged-out guest on the homepage who will never open an editor — downloads, parses and executes a rich-text editor. TipTap alone is ~40% of the ~290 KB gzip JS+CSS critical path. The vite.config comment means nobody will look for this; and because the cause is the barrel pattern, any future `entities/*/index.ts` that adds a heavy UI re-export silently repeats it.

**Исправление.** Change widgets/sidebar/OwnedGames.vue:119 (and any other eager consumer) to import from the deep model path (`@/entities/game/model/store`, `@/entities/game/model/types`) instead of the barrel; or split entities/game/index.ts into a model-only public API and a separate `@/entities/game/ui` entry point that no eager module touches. Re-run the build and assert that index.html contains no tiptap modulepreload — that assertion belongs in CI. Then fix the false comment in vite.config.ts:66-68.

**Проверка оппонентом.** Reproduced on current HEAD with a fresh `npx vite build` (not the 5-day-old checked-in dist). The fresh index.html emits `<link rel="modulepreload" href="/assets/tiptap-Co735Y9A.js">` and the fresh entry chunk index-DAQLyYZ1.js contains a top-level static `import{n as Ll,N as Sl,a as Al,b as Fn,m as Wt,V as $l,M as Es}from"./tiptap-Co735Y9A.js"`, so the 359.39 kB / 114.63 kB gzip tiptap chunk is downloaded, parsed and evaluated before the app boots on every page load, for guests included. Measured critical path: entry 418.77 kB/139.22 gzip + vue-vendor 110.87/43.55 + signalr 55.54/14.34 + tiptap 359.39/114.63 — tiptap is ~37% of critical JS gzip. I traced the module graph with a Rollup buildEnd plugin over getModuleInfo().importedIds (static edges only) and confirmed the claimed chain: src/app/main.ts -> app/App.vue:141 (`import { LeftSidebar } from "@/widgets/sidebar"`) -> widgets/sideb

### [ВЫСОКАЯ] FE-02 — DataTable's virtualization branch produces a structurally broken table, and a user setting turns it on

**Статус: Исправлено.** Ветка виртуализации DataTable удалена, а не починена. Она рендерила вторую таблицу внутри td без colgroup и без ширин колонок, тогда как внешний thead ширины уважает: после 50 строк колонки тела расходились с шапкой, строки накладывались при переносе содержимого, а вся группа строк объявлялась скринридеру одной ячейкой с таблицей внутри. Достижимо было в бою - настройки аккаунта предлагают 100 и 200 строк на страницу. Виртуализации никто не просил, потолок 200 строк, браузер их рисует сам. Композабл useVirtualScroll оставлен: им пользуются чаты, где список действительно неограничен

**Доказательство.** shared/ui/DataTable/DataTable.vue:9 sets `VIRTUAL_THRESHOLD = 50`; :65 `useVirtual = data.length > 50`. The virtual branch (:186-235) renders `<tbody style="height:600px">` → a single spacer `<tr>` → a `<td colspan>` → **a second nested `<table class="virtual-inner-table">`** whose rows are absolutely positioned. Styles at :353-357 give that inner table `table-layout: fixed` with no `<colgroup>` and no per-column widths, while the outer `<thead>` honours `column.width` (:121). Row height is the hardcoded `ROW_HEIGHT = 40` (:62) with no `measureElement`. The 600px viewport is hardcoded (:191). This is reachable in production: pages/account/sections/AccountSettingsSection.vue:110 offers `[5, 10, 20, 30, 40, 50, 100, 200]` items per page, and 18 .vue files render `<DataTable>`.

**Последствие.** Any user who picks 100 or 200 per page — a normal choice for moderators scanning bans, violators, uploads or the games list — gets a table whose body columns no longer align with its header, whose rows overlap whenever content wraps to a second line, and whose row group is announced to screen readers as a single cell containing a table. It fails silently and only for users who changed a setting, which is the worst possible failure profile for bug reports.

**Исправление.** Delete the virtual branch. Page size is capped at 200 and the server already paginates; 200 plain rows render fine. If virtualization is genuinely wanted later, it must be built on a CSS-grid/flex "table" (or a synced `<colgroup>` + measured rows), not a nested `<table>` inside a `<td>`. Until then the branch is untestable dead weight that fires only in production.

**Проверка оппонентом.** Survives adversarial verification, with two evidence corrections that do not save the code. REACHABILITY CONFIRMED end-to-end: AccountSettingsSection.vue:110 offers page sizes up to 200 -> usePaging.ts:60-65 exposes entitiesPerPage from user settings -> useGamesFilter.ts:484 (also useUsersFilter.ts:439, useBlogsFilter.ts:293, usePulseFilter.ts:289) sets params.size = entitiesPerPage -> gameApi.ts:195 sends take -> server PagingQuery.cs:23 is [Range(1,100)] and ACCEPTS 100 -> GamesDataTable.vue:215 passes 100 rows to DataTable.vue:61 (useVirtual = data.length > 50). The virtual branch therefore fires in production on /games, /users, /blogs and profile tables. EVIDENCE ERRORS IN THE ORIGINAL FINDING: (1) "no per-column widths" is false - DataTable.vue:237 does set :style="column.width ? { width: column.width } : {}" on the inner tds; (2) "picks 100 or 200" is half wrong - 200 is rejected s

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] FE-03 — Core color tokens and the primary button fail the project's own documented WCAG AA rule, worst in dark theme

**Статус: Открыто.** 

**Доказательство.** docs/conventions/CODE_STYLE.md:274 mandates "Контрастность WCAG AA — минимум 4.5:1 для текста". Measured against ThemeVariables.css: (a) shared/ui/Button/Button.vue:36-39 paints `.primary` as `#fff` on `$link`; in dark theme `--link: #7cb0e0` (ThemeVariables.css:128) → 2.30:1, and hover `--link-hover: #9cc8f0` is worse. The comment at Button.vue:33-35 asserts "Navy is dark, so white text keeps a high, theme-stable contrast" — the token is not theme-stable. The same white-on-$link recipe is copied into create-game/ui/TagSelector.vue:162-164, BlogNotepad.vue:479-480, GameNotepad.vue:481-482, NotepadPage.vue:437-438, ModerationTags.vue:401-402. (b) `--text-muted: #999` on `--bg-page: #fff` → 2.85:1, used 412 times. `--text-muted-muted: #bbb` → 1.92:1. (c) ConfirmDialog.vue:259-264 `.danger` overrides only `background-color: $accent-red` while inheriting `color: $text` from `+button` → `#333

**Последствие.** The single most emphasised button in every form is effectively unreadable for dark-theme users, and the destructive-confirm button — the one place where misreading has consequences — is the worst offender at 1.5-1.9:1. Muted secondary text, used 412 times at 12-14px, sits at 2.85:1 in light theme. Because the rule is written down and the tokens violate it, the documentation is actively misleading anyone who trusts it.

**Исправление.** Introduce explicit on-surface pairs the way the toast tokens already do (`--toast-*-bg` / `--toast-*-text` at ThemeVariables.css:95-106 get this right): add `--action-bg`/`--action-text` and `--danger-bg`/`--danger-text` per theme, and make `.primary` and `.danger` set both. Darken `--link` in the dark theme or stop using it as a fill. Raise `--text-muted` in the light theme to ~#6b6b6b (4.6:1) — a one-token change that fixes 412 call sites. Then add a build-time contrast check over ThemeVariables.css pairs so this cannot regress.

**Проверка оппонентом.** SURVIVES on facts — I could not refute the measurements. Every cited line is accurate and I recomputed each ratio from the tokens I read in src/DM.Web.Client/src/assets/styles/ThemeVariables.css (the src/app/styles/ one is a 2-line `@import` re-export, so the cited line numbers are the live ones).

Verified: (a) Button.vue:36-39 sets `.primary { background-color: $link; color: #fff }`; Themes.sass maps `$link` -> `var(--link)`, dark = `#7cb0e0` (ThemeVariables.css:128) -> white = 2.30:1; light `#304060` -> 10.35:1. So the comment at Button.vue:33-35 ("Navy is dark, so white text keeps a high, theme-stable contrast") is factually false — the token is not theme-stable. Dark theme is genuinely reachable (App.vue:193-195 stamps `theme_Dark`, shared/stores/ui.ts:40 auto-detects). The white-on-$link copies exist verbatim at TagSelector.vue:162-164, BlogNotepad.vue:479-480, GameNotepad.vue:481-

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] FE-04 — Inputs.sass mixes mixin definitions with global base rules, duplicating them into 85 scoped stylesheets — 11.5% of all shipped CSS

**Статус: Исправлено.** Замерено на сборке: побочные правила Inputs.sass выносились в 84 scoped-стиля побайтово, 99 708 байт - 19,6 процента всего отгружаемого CSS, а не 11,5 (в находке была цифра по gzip). 54 из 85 импортеров не имеют в шаблоне ни одного контрола, то есть у них копия не совпадала ни с чем. Inputs.sass стал чисто миксинным, вывод переехал в новый InputsGlobal.sass, подключаемый один раз из main.ts на том же месте каскада. Проверено сравнением множеств правил двух сборок: исчезли ровно 7 правил базового блока в scoped-варианте, не появилось ни одного, счетчик копий не изменился ни у одного выжившего правила. Единственное расхождение - переименованный хеш scoped-кейфреймов у трех skeleton-правил, определение и ссылка переименованы согласованно. CSS 508 951 -> 409 243 байта, из них 35 610 сняты с index-чанка, который грузится на каждой странице

**Доказательство.** src/assets/styles/Inputs.sass:208-214 ends with two bare global rules (`input, textarea, select` → `+input()`, and `input[type="checkbox"]` → `+checkbox()`) in the same file as the `+button` / `+input` / `+muted-link` mixin library. That file is imported globally in app/main.ts:19 **and** by 85 SFC `<style scoped>` blocks, each of which re-emits those base rules scoped to its own data-v hash. Measured in the built output: the entry stylesheet alone contains 124 `input[data-v-…]` selectors, 104 `textarea[data-v-…]`, 96 `appearance:none` and 130 `checkbox` occurrences. Across all 112 emitted CSS files (515,035 B total) the duplicated base-input blocks account for ~58,984 B — 11.5%. The entry CSS is 199,571 B raw, a render-blocking resource on every page.

**Последствие.** One eighth of every stylesheet the user downloads is the same input reset repeated under different scope attributes. It also creates a specificity hazard: `input[data-v-x]` beats the global `input`, so a component that merely imports the mixin file silently wins the cascade over any later global change to input styling.

**Исправление.** Split the file: `_InputMixins.sass` (declaration-free, safe to import anywhere) and `Inputs.sass` (the two global base rules, imported only by main.ts). Update the 85 SFC imports to the mixin file. This is mechanical and should cut ~59 KB of CSS with zero visual change — verify by diffing the built CSS byte counts before/after.

**Проверка оппонентом.** The mechanism is real and, if anything, understated. src/DM.Web.Client/src/assets/styles/Inputs.sass:208-214 does terminate a mixin library with two bare global rules (`input, textarea, select` -> +input(); `input[type="checkbox"]` -> +checkbox()); the file is loaded globally via src/app/main.ts:19 -> src/app/styles/Inputs.sass:2 AND @import-ed by exactly 85 SFCs, all of which I verified use <style scoped>. Built output reproduces it: dist/assets/index-DkIkgtdT.css is 199,571 B with 124 `input[data-v-]` / 104 `textarea[data-v-]` / 96 `appearance:none`; an exact-body-match pass over all 112 CSS files (515,039 B) finds 429 byte-identical scoped copies totalling 76,457 B = 14.8% raw (a LOWER bound - it misses the :focus/:disabled sub-rules). So the auditor's 11.5% is conservative and the proposed split into _InputMixins.sass + Inputs.sass is the right fix. TWO CLAIMS DO NOT SURVIVE. (1) Per

## Средние и низкие

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] FE-05 — Two competing dialog stacks, in direct contradiction of a documented ban on self-rolled overlays

**Статус: Открыто.** 

docs/conventions/UI_STANDARDS.md:78 states "Самокатные оверлеи запрещены" and names shared/ui/Layout/Dialog.vue as the primitive. Dialog.vue wraps vue-final-modal (esc-to-close, click-to-close, lock-scroll) and has 17 consumers. In parallel, three components hand-roll a Teleport + backdrop + container: shared/ui/ConfirmDialog/ConfirmDialog.vue:135-196 (26 consumers), shared/ui/BBCodeEditor/InputDialog.vue:232, features/attribute-schema-editor/ui/AttributeSchemaEditor.vue:502. Dialog.vue:41-43 normalises the violation in a comment: "Align vfm modals with the self-rolled dialog tier (ConfirmDialog, BBCodeEditor, InputDialog all sit at $z-modal)". The two stacks differ behaviourally: the vfm tier locks scroll, the self-rolled tier does not; ConfirmDialog listens for Escape via `@keydown` on the backdrop div (ConfirmDialog.vue:141), which only works while focus stays inside the dialog, and never marks the page behind as `inert`/`aria-hidden`.

Последствие: The most-used dialog in the app (26 call sites) is the one that does not follow the documented dialog contract. Background scroll under a confirm, and a screen reader that can still walk the page behind an `aria-modal` dialog, are the concrete symptoms. More structurally: the rule is written, violated, and then re-justified in a code comment — which is how a convention dies.

Исправление: Pick one. Either port ConfirmDialog/InputDialog/AttributeSchemaEditor onto the `Dialog` primitive, or promote the self-rolled shell (which already has the better focus trap) into `Dialog` and drop vue-final-modal entirely — the latter also removes a dependency. Whichever wins, UI_STANDARDS.md:76-89 must describe what actually exists.

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] FE-06 — Seven destructive actions still use native window.confirm() next to 26 ConfirmDialog consumers

**Статус: Открыто.** 

entities/user/model/useAvatarUpload.ts:101, pages/game/GameNotepad.vue:121, pages/personal/NotepadPage.vue:109, pages/profile/moderation/ModerationNotes.vue:88, pages/moderation/ModerationAwardsSeries.vue:116, pages/account/sections/AccountBlacklistSection.vue:194, pages/profile/ProfilePage.vue:339. Meanwhile 26 .vue files import ConfirmDialog. The drift is visible inside a single copy-pasted feature: pages/blog/BlogNotepad.vue was migrated to ConfirmDialog (`pendingDelete` + `confirmDelete`) while its twin pages/game/GameNotepad.vue:121 still calls `confirm(...)`.

Последствие: Native confirm() is unstyled and theme-blind, blocks the main thread, is suppressible by the browser after repeated use, and cannot carry the `danger` affordance. Seven of them sit on irreversible actions (delete note, reset avatar, unblock user, delete award series).

Исправление: Replace all seven with ConfirmDialog and add an ESLint `no-restricted-globals` rule for `confirm`/`alert` under src/ so the pattern cannot come back.

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] FE-07 — BBCode display has an SSOT component that most call sites bypass — and one of them forgot the interactive init entirely

**Статус: Исправлено.** Забытое место найдено и починено: ChatEventsPanel рисовал описание события руками собранным div с v-html и не звал initBbcodeInteractive вовсе - панель отрисована соседом контейнера сообщений, поэтому контейнерный init до нее не доставал, и спойлеры внутри описания не раскрывались. Переведено на SSOT-компонент ContentText, у него watch на html покрывает ленивую загрузку описания. Заодно закрыта вторая дыра того же класса: тело раскрытого удаленного сообщения в ChatMessage появляется по клику, много позже контейнерного init. Снят мертвый импорт BbcodeContent из ChatEventsPanel. Проверено сравнением множеств правил двух сборок: CSS не изменился ни на одно правило. Унификация остальных пяти мест на ContentText не делалась - там init проброшен вручную и работает, это вопрос стиля, а не дефект

shared/ui/Content/ContentText.vue is the intended SSOT: it renders `.bbcode-content` and runs `initBbcodeInteractive` + search highlighting on mount and on content change. It has 8 consumers. Seven other sites hand-wire the same thing themselves: features/comment/ui/CommentItem.vue:245, features/topic/ui/TopicCard.vue:164, widgets/chat-message/ChatMessage.vue:136, pages/profile/ProfileAbout.vue:34-38, pages/game/GameChatRoom.vue:131, pages/global-chat/GlobalChatPage.vue:568, pages/messenger/ChatView.vue:442. And pages/global-chat/ChatEventsPanel.vue:347-350 renders `<div class="overlay-description bbcode-content" v-html="overlayDetails.description">` with **zero** calls to initBbcodeInteractive anywhere in the file.

Последствие: In the chat-event description overlay, every interactive BBCode construct is dead: spoiler heads (rendered by the server as `<a href="javascript:void(0)" class="…spoiler-head">Показать изображение</a>`, see DM.Infrastructure.Core/Parsing/BbParserProvider.cs:64), NSFW veils and collapse blocks do nothing when clicked. This is the predictable cost of an SSOT that is optional: the eighth call site forgot, and nothing caught it.

Исправление: Make ContentText the only sanctioned way to render server BBCode HTML — replace the seven hand-wired sites and fix ChatEventsPanel.vue:347-350 — then add an ESLint rule forbidding `v-html` on any element carrying the `bbcode-content` class outside ContentText.vue.

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] FE-08 — createFilterDispatcher keeps mutable state at module scope with no teardown, so a filter click can rewrite the next page's URL

**Статус: Исправлено.** Механика подтверждена построчно. Диспетчер живет всю вкладку, таймер читает маршрут в момент срабатывания, а не в момент клика, и replace({name, query}) в vue-router наследует недостающие параметры из НОВОГО маршрута - то есть клик по фильтру за 50 мс до перехода штампует query одной страницы на другую и сносит ей номер страницы. Исправлено фиксацией пути на момент dispatch и выходом при расхождении; путь, а не имя, потому что имя forum одинаково у всех досок. Заодно снято застревание: ранний выход при isNavigating бросал pendingState осиротевшим и терял изменение фильтра - теперь перепланирование. Teardown в восьми композаблах не добавлялся: после проверки пути он избыточен

shared/lib/composables/createFilterDispatcher.ts:46-50 declares `debounceTimer`, `pendingState`, `isNavigating` and `routerInstance` at module scope (the doc comment at :16-18 mandates this). The 50 ms timer at :88-137 builds `query` from the stale `pendingState` but reads the route *at fire time* — `routerInstance.currentRoute.value` (:98) — and then calls `router.replace({ name: currentRoute.name, query })` (:122-125). No `onScopeDispose`/`onUnmounted` cancels the timer; none of the eight filter modules (useGamesFilter, useUsersFilter, useBlogsFilter, useTopicsFilter, useCommentsFilter, usePollsFilter, usePulseFilter, useTestimonialsFilter) registers cleanup either.

Последствие: Click a filter chip and navigate within 50 ms (a fast click on a sidebar link, or a keyboard user tabbing away) and the pending query is applied to the destination route instead — replacing whatever query params that page had. `routerInstance` also outlives the component, so a stale router reference is retained for the lifetime of the tab. Low frequency, but it is a silent URL corruption in the one subsystem that treats the URL as the source of truth.

Исправление: Add a `cancel()` to the dispatcher that clears the timer and nulls `pendingState`, and call it from `onScopeDispose` inside each composable. Additionally capture the route name at dispatch time and bail in the timer if `currentRoute.name` no longer matches.

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] FE-09 — vue-i18n is installed, configured, documented and never used, on top of 5,524 hardcoded Russian strings with no stated posture

**Статус: Открыто.** 

app/providers/plugins.ts:3,11-14,23 creates and installs an i18n instance. Grep across src for `useI18n`, `$t(` or any message catalog returns exactly zero hits outside that file. The built entry chunk contains 63 `i18n` symbol occurrences (node_modules/vue-i18n is 1.6 MB on disk). Meanwhile 5,524 lines across 424 non-spec files contain Cyrillic literals. docs/conventions/PERFORMANCE.md:518-524 documents the i18n configuration as though it were a live concern.

Последствие: Worst of both worlds: the bundle pays for a localization runtime that translates nothing, and the docs give a future reader the impression the app is i18n-ready when in fact localization would be an unbounded 424-file refactor. A Russian-only product is a perfectly defensible choice — but it has to be stated as a choice, not simulated with a dead plugin.

Исправление: Decide and record it. If the site stays Russian-only, remove vue-i18n from package.json and plugins.ts, delete the PERFORMANCE.md section, and add one line to UI_STANDARDS.md saying UI copy is inline Russian by design. If localization is on the roadmap, the cost must be booked now while it is 5,524 lines rather than 15,000.

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] FE-10 — Three near-identical notepad pages (~1,500 LOC) that have already drifted apart

**Статус: Открыто.** 

pages/blog/BlogNotepad.vue (507 lines), pages/game/GameNotepad.vue (539), pages/personal/NotepadPage.vue (465). A normalised diff of the first two (substituting the entity name) differs almost only in the access-check computed and the API object. They have already diverged in behaviour: BlogNotepad uses ConfirmDialog for delete while GameNotepad.vue:121 uses native `confirm()`. They are also the epicentre of the token drift in this codebase — the hardcoded `font-size: 0.75rem/0.8rem/0.85rem/0.9rem/1rem/1.5rem` values cluster here (BlogNotepad.vue:317,361,400,432,476,489,496 and the same lines ±2 in GameNotepad.vue), as do the `color: white` and `$text-on-red`-on-red contrast bugs.

Последствие: Every notepad change is a three-file change that nobody will remember to make three times — as the delete-confirmation divergence already proves. Three copies also triple the surface for the contrast and font-size defects listed above.

Исправление: Extract a `features/notepad` component parameterised by `{ api, canAccess, emptyCopy }` and reduce the three pages to thin route shells. While collapsing them, replace the rem font sizes with `$secondary-font-size`/`$tertiary-font-size` and fix the red-on-red foregrounds.

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] FE-11 — No card/panel mixin — the one surface recipe used everywhere is the only one not abstracted

**Статус: Открыто.** 

The system has mixins for buttons (`+button`, `+button-link`, `+inline-link-button`, `+expand-toggle-button`), inputs (`+input`, `+input-base`, `+checkbox`), link idioms (`+muted-links-line`, `+muted-link`) and dropdown panels (`+_dropdown-panel` in _Filters.sass). It has none for the card/panel surface, which is hand-written instead: `border: 1px solid $border` appears 86 times across 53 .vue files, `border-radius: $border-radius` 149 times across 84 files, `padding: $medium` 94 times, `background-color: $bg-element` 78 times. Related: only 8 media queries use the `$bp-*` tokens while 28 hardcode pixels across 8 distinct values (13× 768px, 4× 600px, 3× 620px, 3× 480px, 2× 640px, plus one-offs 699px, 489px, 339px) — _Breakpoints.sass:8-11 admits this explicitly.

Последствие: The tokens hold, so nothing looks broken today — but the recipe does not. Changing the site's card treatment (radius, border weight, elevation) means editing 80+ files, and the one-off breakpoints (699px, 489px, 339px) mean responsive behaviour cannot be reasoned about from the scale. This is the difference between a token set and a design system.

Исправление: Add `+card($padding: $medium)` and `+panel` to a `_Surfaces.sass` and migrate the 53 border/84 radius call sites; enforce with a stylelint rule banning bare `border: 1px solid $border` outside the mixin. Separately, replace the 28 hardcoded media queries with `$bp-*` and delete the 699/489/339 one-offs.

### [НИЗКАЯ] FE-12 — Dead useSignalR composable exported from the public barrel — a second socket waiting to be opened

**Статус: Исправлено.** То же, что DEAD-USESIGNALR (00f65939)

shared/lib/composables/useSignalR.ts:13-113 defines a per-component `useSignalR()` that calls `api.establishHubConnection("whatsup")` on its own, entirely bypassing the lease-based singleton defined in the same file at :118-255. It is re-exported from the public barrel (shared/lib/composables/index.ts:69) and has zero consumers anywhere in src.

Последствие: The next developer who needs realtime in a component will autocomplete `useSignalR` — the shorter, more obvious name — and open a second WebSocket per mounted component, defeating the lease system that the rest of the file was carefully built to provide.

Исправление: Delete `useSignalR` and its barrel export, or rename it to something unusable by accident and document why it exists. Given zero consumers, delete.

### [НИЗКАЯ] FE-13 — Dev-only mockup page ships in the production router with no guard

**Статус: Исправлено.** То же, что HYG-17. Уточнение проверки: перепись hex-цветов в находке неверна - в файле один литерал, а не два, и по .vue их пять, а не один (c0d9d638)

app/providers/router.ts:831-842 registers `/dev/style-variants` → pages/dev/StyleVariantsPage.vue (1,126 lines, 33 KB source), with the comment "removed once the owner picks the variants". Nothing links to it (grep for `dev-style-variants` outside router.ts returns nothing) and there is no `import.meta.env.DEV` guard — the codebase uses that guard elsewhere (shared/lib/utils/bbcodeInteractive.ts:323,336,366,446,671), just not here. The page also carries the only two remaining hardcoded hex colors in .vue files (StyleVariantsPage.vue:824) and 10 raw `rgba()` probe values (:771-798), plus a second copy of the `.primary` button rule (:820-826) that will drift from Button.vue.

Последствие: A public URL exposing internal design-iteration scaffolding, plus a duplicated button implementation that no test covers and that will silently diverge from the real one.

Исправление: Wrap the route record in `if (import.meta.env.DEV)` so it is tree-shaken from the production build — one line, keeps the page usable locally.

### [НИЗКАЯ] FE-14 — User-content BBCode images render without lazy-loading or intrinsic dimensions, and the two emitters have already diverged

**Статус: Открыто.** 

The server emits `<img src=… class="bb-image" data-bb-tag="img" referrerpolicy="no-referrer" alt=… data-alt=… />` (DM.Infrastructure.Core/Parsing/BbParserWrapper.cs:351-352) and the client-side editor emitter produces `<img src=… alt=… class="bb-image" data-bb-tag="img" referrerpolicy="no-referrer" />` (DM.Web.Client/src/shared/lib/utils/bbcode.ts:585-587). Neither sets `loading`, `decoding`, `width` or `height`. The client copy is already missing the `data-alt` attribute the server adds. This contrasts sharply with AvatarImg, which gets all of this right.

Последствие: A forum topic or game room renders 20 posts per page (usePaging.ts:12-19); every embedded image in every post fetches eagerly and reflows the list as it arrives, on a page whose scroll position users depend on. The `data-alt` divergence means any consumer reading that attribute works on server-rendered content and not on editor-preview content.

Исправление: Add `loading="lazy" decoding="async"` to both emitters, and — since BBCode `[img]` already carries optional width/height (both emitters handle them) — emit them as real `width`/`height` attributes when present, not only as CSS vars. Then add a round-trip test asserting the two emitters produce byte-identical markup; the `data-alt` drift shows there is currently nothing enforcing that.


---

# Документация — C

A handful of docs (BBCODE_RENDERING, UPLOADS, URL_STRUCTURE) are genuinely excellent invariant-first convention writing that matches the code line for line, but the set as a whole fails its own SSOT rule: the published API error contract is fabricated, `[mod]` visibility is stated three times with two of them false, session lifetime contradicts itself inside one file, two architecture docs name a message-bus library the project does not use, DEPLOYMENT recommends a scale-out that would break realtime, and the DI/lifetime decision that just caused a systemic bug is written down nowhere.

## Что сделано хорошо

- docs/architecture/BBCODE_RENDERING.md is the best doc in the repo and one of the better contract docs I've read anywhere. It leads with the invariant and the reason for it (filtering must happen before JSON serialization, because a client-side filter is defeated by DevTools), enumerates [private] visibility as an explicit OR of five named rules, and defines 'zero-information erase' as a testable property. I checked it against src/DM.Infrastructure.Core/Parsing/Visitors/PermissionFilteringVisitor.cs:98-135 and PermissionBucket.cs:67-112 — the doc and the code agree rule for rule, including the deliberate exclusion of Moderator+ from [private]. Section 'Как добавить новый тег' (:142-152) names the five real extension points a new tag must touch. This is a doc that will still be correct after the author leaves.
- docs/architecture/UPLOADS.md is written as invariants plus an explicit forbidden-patterns list (:83-92) rather than a description of what exists. Every claim I sampled is real: Idempotency-Key with 1h TTL (src/DM.Web.API/Features/General/Upload/UploadApiService.cs:52-53,162-167), UserAvatarChanged SignalR broadcast (src/DM.Web.API/Notifications/RealtimeAvatarBroadcaster.cs:47), magic-byte detection before decode, no pre-generated thumbnails. The forbidden-patterns framing is what makes it enforceable — '❌ Polymorphic FK для target'а upload'а' is a rule a reviewer can apply, unlike 'use good design'.
- docs/conventions/URL_STRUCTURE.md opens with a decision tree (:5-10) and then works two hypothetical entities (Турниры, Достижения, :252-278) all the way through it. That is the correct shape for a convention doc: it teaches the decision procedure instead of listing current URLs, so it stays useful for entities that don't exist yet. The Numeric-vs-SortOrder distinction (:127-136) is a genuinely non-obvious invariant that would otherwise be rediscovered by bug.
- The 'Записанные исключения' idiom — API_DESIGN.md:81-83 (DELETE /polls/{id}/vote returns 200 with a body), API_DESIGN.md:151-161 (four semantic-shortcut GETs), URL_STRUCTURE.md:217-218, UI_STANDARDS.md:282 (global-chat compact date format). Naming and justifying each deviation instead of leaving it as silent inconsistency is discipline most teams never develop. It converts 'the codebase is inconsistent' into 'the codebase has four recorded exceptions', which is a reviewable state.
- PATTERNS.md:660-668 ('Что НЕ используем' — MediatR, CQRS, Event Sourcing, Specification, BDD, each with a one-line reason) and :600-656 ('Частые ошибки', each phrased as the wrong claim followed by 'Нет.'). Recording the rejected options and the anti-claim is more durable than the positive rule alone — it pre-empts the exact arguments a new contributor will make.
- The layer dependency rule in SYSTEM.md:61-69 is not aspirational — it is actually enforced by the project graph. I checked every src/DM.Domain.*/*.csproj: all eight domain modules reference DM.Domain.Core and nothing else. The doc's strongest claim ('Domain.Game НЕ может зависеть от Domain.Blog') is structurally true, which is rarer than it sounds.
- Numeric parameters that are cheap to get wrong are right: rate limits (API_DESIGN.md:167-172 vs Startup.cs:210-249 — 100/5/20/10, all four match), AES-256-GCM key/nonce/tag sizes, lockout 15→30min (AuthenticationConfiguration.cs:59,65), middleware order (SYSTEM.md:144-153 vs Startup.cs:421-436, correct apart from where Routing sits), the Postgres-tsvector-for-private-search decision (DATA_STORAGE.md:126-128 vs DmDbContext.cs:222-242), and the FSD @x cross-import convention (PATTERNS.md:143-152 — src/DM.Web.Client/src/entities/user/@x exists and is the only one).

## Подтвержденные находки

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] DOC-02 — The documented API error contract does not exist — API_DESIGN.md publishes a shape the API never returns

**Статус: Исправлено.** Мертвые ErrorEnvelope/ApiError удалены; API_DESIGN.md и CODE_STYLE.md описывают реальный ProblemDetails; убрано ложное обещание заголовков X-RateLimit; попутно приведены к общей форме Content-Type и ответ CSRF (7640b6b6)

**Доказательство.** docs/conventions/API_DESIGN.md:48 and :57-58 declare the error format as `ErrorEnvelope`: `{ "errors": [{ "code": "not_found", "message": "User not found" }] }` — an array of code+message objects. Reality: src/DM.Web.API/Middleware/ErrorHandlingMiddleware.cs:77-81 sets `ContentType = "application/problem+json"` and writes an RFC7807 ProblemDetails for every exception path. The `ErrorEnvelope` type exists (src/DM.Web.API/Shared/Dto/ErrorEnvelope.cs) but its `Errors` property is a single `ApiError` object, not an array — so even the documented type doesn't have the documented shape. Usage counts across src/DM.Web.API: `ErrorEnvelope` 2 occurrences, `GeneralError` 803. The actual declared error DTOs are `GeneralError { message }` and `BadRequestError : GeneralError { invalidProperties }`. SYSTEM.md:147 ('exceptions → ProblemDetails') contradicts API_DESIGN.md. And the only place in the enti

**Последствие.** A frontend or bot author reading API_DESIGN.md writes `errors[0].message` and gets `undefined` on every error path. Three error models coexist in the code (ProblemDetails, GeneralError/BadRequestError, the near-dead ErrorEnvelope/ApiError) with no doc saying which is canonical or which the other two are migrating toward — so the inconsistency has no forcing function to resolve.

**Исправление.** Rewrite API_DESIGN.md:42-63 to document what ErrorHandlingMiddleware actually emits (problem+json, with the fields the ProblemDetailsFactory populates), move the BadRequestError-vs-GeneralError rule out of .claude/CLAUDE.md into API_DESIGN.md, and either delete ErrorEnvelope/ApiError as dead code or record it as the migration target with an explicit 'legacy' marker on the current shape — the same 'Записанные исключения' idiom already used elsewhere in this file.

**Проверка оппонентом.** All citations verified independently and hold exactly. docs/conventions/API_DESIGN.md:48,57-58 declares the error format as ErrorEnvelope with `errors` as an ARRAY of {code,message}; src/DM.Web.API/Middleware/ErrorHandlingMiddleware.cs:77-81 sets ContentType "application/problem+json" on every catch path, and ExceptionProblemDetailsFactoryExtensions.cs:13-65 routes all six exception cases through the stock ProblemDetailsFactory (no custom factory or CustomizeProblemDetails is registered anywhere in src/). src/DM.Web.API/Shared/Dto/ErrorEnvelope.cs:29 is `public ApiError Errors { get; }` — a single object, so even the named type lacks the documented shape; ErrorEnvelope/ApiError/ErrorCodes appear nowhere outside their own definition files. Refutation attempts failed. (1) "Docs are ideals not current state" (the project's own doc rule) does not rescue it: an ideal contradicting the very cl

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] DOC-03 — `[mod]` visibility is stated in three documents; the two most likely to be read are false

**Статус: Исправлено.** AUTHORIZATION.md и GLOSSARY.md исправлены по SSOT (BBCODE_RENDERING.md): [mod] публичен на чтение, ограничен на запись; заодно поправлен устаревший комментарий в PermissionFilteringVisitor (2b6d1497)

**Доказательство.** docs/architecture/BBCODE_RENDERING.md:55-57 (the declared SSOT) is correct: '[mod] — публичный на чтение, ограниченный на запись … Читательской фильтрации по правам у [mod] нет'. But docs/architecture/AUTHORIZATION.md:128 says '`[mod]` — виден только `UserRole >= Moderator`', and docs/references/GLOSSARY.md:420 repeats 'Виден только `UserRole >= Moderator`'. The code agrees with BBCODE_RENDERING and not with the other two: src/DM.Infrastructure.Core/Parsing/Visitors/PermissionFilteringVisitor.cs:62-63 `IsPrivacySensitiveTag(tagName) => tagName is PrivateTagName` — [mod] is never filtered on read; src/DM.Domain.Core/Content/ModBlockSanitizer.cs:9-14 confirms the restriction is write-time unwrapping. AUTHORIZATION.md:126 even asserts the wrong claim under a heading promising the filtering happens 'до сериализации JSON'.

**Последствие.** This is the concrete cost of restating one rule in six places (BBCODE_RENDERING, AUTHORIZATION, SECURITY, PATTERNS, CODE_STYLE, GLOSSARY): two copies rotted, and they are precisely the two a reader consults for permissions questions. Someone reasoning about moderator-visibility from AUTHORIZATION.md will conclude that non-moderators cannot see a mod block — and may 'fix' the renderer to match the doc, breaking a feature that works.

**Исправление.** Cut the visibility claim from AUTHORIZATION.md:124-137 and GLOSSARY.md:420 down to a one-line pointer ('[mod] — публичный на чтение, Moderator+ на запись; полный контракт — BBCODE_RENDERING.md'). The general rule: a document that is not the SSOT for a topic may state at most the topic's name and a link, never the rule's content.

**Проверка оппонентом.** The evidence survives verification, but the severity is inflated. VERIFIED: docs/architecture/AUTHORIZATION.md:128 ("[mod] — виден только UserRole >= Moderator") and docs/references/GLOSSARY.md:420 ("Виден только UserRole >= Moderator") do contradict the declared SSOT at docs/architecture/BBCODE_RENDERING.md:55 ("публичный на чтение, ограниченный на запись … рендерится одинаково для всех зрителей, включая гостей"), and the code sides with the SSOT. src/DM.Infrastructure.Core/Parsing/Visitors/PermissionFilteringVisitor.cs:62-63 defines IsPrivacySensitiveTag(tagName) => tagName is PrivateTagName, so [mod] short-circuits to visible at line 78 before any role logic; SourceContainsPrivacyTagMarker (lines 154-173) scans only for "[private". I grepped the entire rendering infrastructure for a read-side role gate on [mod] and found none — the sole UserRole.Moderator reference is PermissionBucket

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] DOC-05 — Session lifetime is documented wrong in two places and SECURITY.md contradicts itself 15 lines apart

**Статус: Исправлено.** Реальные значения в AUTHENTICATION.md, SECURITY.md больше не дублирует числа а ссылается; зафиксировано следствие: с поставляемым конфигом галочка «запомнить меня» ничего не меняет (2b6d1497)

**Доказательство.** docs/architecture/AUTHENTICATION.md:86 and docs/conventions/SECURITY.md:102 both state 'Длительность (Remember me ✗) | 24 часа'. src/DM.Web.API/appsettings.json:42 sets `"SessionExpirationHours": 8760` — one year. The 24h figure is only the C# default (AuthenticationConfiguration.cs:13), which the shipped config overrides. SECURITY.md then says the opposite of itself at :117: 'Таймауты сессий | ✓ 1 год (обычная и "запомнить меня")' — same file, correct value, 15 lines below the wrong one. ROADMAP.md:94 also carries the correct '1 год (обычная и "запомнить меня")'.

**Последствие.** A security parameter documented at 1/365th of its real value, in the two documents whose entire purpose is to be the reference for that parameter. Anyone doing a session-security review, threat model, or NIST 800-63 self-assessment from these docs draws the wrong conclusion about exposure window for a stolen non-persistent cookie. The in-file self-contradiction means neither number can be trusted without opening appsettings.json — which defeats the document.

**Исправление.** Fix AUTHENTICATION.md:86 and SECURITY.md:102 to 1 year, delete the duplicate NIST table at SECURITY.md:108-119 (it restates the requirements table above it), and note in both that non-persistent sessions are deliberately configured to the same lifetime as persistent ones — because if that is intentional, the 'Remember me' distinction is currently cosmetic and that is itself worth recording.

**Проверка оппонентом.** I tried to break this and could not — every load-bearing fact holds.

VERIFIED:
- `docs/architecture/AUTHENTICATION.md:85` (finding says :86 — off by one, immaterial) and `docs/conventions/SECURITY.md:102` both read `| Длительность (Remember me ✗) | 24 часа |`.
- `src/DM.Web.API/appsettings.json:42` sets `"SessionExpirationHours": 8760`, and so does the deployment template `src/DM.Web.API/appsettings.template.json:55`. There is no other appsettings file and no env override anywhere in `src/` or `docker/` — grep for `SessionExpirationHours` returns only these two (plus bin/ copies). So 8760 is the shipped value in every environment, not a dev-only quirk.
- `src/DM.Domain.Account/Configuration/AuthenticationConfiguration.cs:13` is indeed only the C# default (`= 24`), overridden by config.
- `src/DM.Domain.Account/Features/Authentication/SessionFactory.cs:36-38`: `ExpirationUtc = persistent
