## Средние и низкие

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] DOC-07 — Two architecture documents name MassTransit; the project uses Jamq and has no MassTransit reference anywhere

**Статус: Исправлено.** MassTransit убран из диаграммы SYSTEM.md и блюпринта воркеров в PATTERNS.md (c91d8d4d)

docs/architecture/SYSTEM.md:35 labels the entry-point row 'DM.Workers.* (MassTransit)'. docs/conventions/PATTERNS.md:110 specifies the worker blueprint file as '{Name}Consumer.cs # MassTransit consumer'. A grep for MassTransit across all of src/ (both .cs and .csproj) returns zero results. The actual client is Jamq — Jamq.Client.Rabbit.DependencyInjection is referenced by DM.Web.API, all three workers, DM.Infrastructure.Mail and DM.Infrastructure.Messaging.

Последствие: The messaging library is a top-three infrastructure choice and it is misidentified in the document whose title is 'Система DM3'. A new contributor searching NuGet or Stack Overflow for MassTransit consumer patterns will write code against the wrong API surface and wrong middleware model. It also means whichever comparison led to Jamq — a much less common choice — was never recorded, so the decision cannot be revisited.

Исправление: Correct SYSTEM.md:35 and PATTERNS.md:110 to Jamq, and add one sentence on why Jamq over MassTransit to SYSTEM.md's Domain Events section (which already carries a '**Зачем:**' rationale line at :73 and is the natural home for it).

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] DOC-08 — CODE_STYLE.md carries a duplicate 'Стандарты API' section that contradicts API_DESIGN.md on the response envelope

**Статус: Исправлено.** Дублирующий раздел "Стандарты API" в CODE_STYLE.md заменен указателем; он противоречил API_DESIGN.md, утверждая что одиночный ресурс отдается без конверта (b3c8215f)

docs/conventions/CODE_STYLE.md:124-153 restates API_DESIGN.md's philosophy (four numbered principles, verbatim), the three endpoint types table, the response format, and the pagination parameters. On the one point where the wording diverges, it is wrong: CODE_STYLE.md:145 says '**Одиночный ресурс** — напрямую', while API_DESIGN.md:45 and the '> Правило' at :61-63 say single resources use `Envelope<T>` with a `resource` field and that unwrapped DTOs are legacy to be migrated. src/DM.Web.API/Shared/Dto/Envelope.cs confirms API_DESIGN is the intended rule.

Последствие: Two conventions files give opposite instructions for the most common thing an API author does. Because CODE_STYLE.md is the doc linked from the pre-commit checklist, the wrong one is the more likely to be read. This is the SSOT rule stated in README.md:113 ('Минимум дублирования — ссылки вместо копирования') failing inside the conventions folder itself.

Исправление: Delete CODE_STYLE.md:124-153 entirely and replace with the one-line pointer style already used at :87-93 for testing and security ('> [API_DESIGN.md](./API_DESIGN.md)').

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] DOC-09 — PERFORMANCE.md contains a 263-line verbatim dump of one composable's source

**Статус: Исправлено.** 263-строчный дамп реализации композабла свернут в правила; дамп уже разошелся с кодом - описывал module-level state и интерфейс FilterComposable, которых нет (b3c8215f)

docs/conventions/PERFORMANCE.md:267-529, 'URL-Synced Filters: Action Queue Pattern' — an ASCII architecture diagram, a bad-example snippet, then a complete implementation including the FilterComposable interface, the FilterAction discriminated union, the full reducer switch with project-specific cases (SET_STATUS resetting recruitmentFilter), module-level debounce/pending/isNavigating state, the dispatch function with its try/catch on NavigationDuplicated, and the composable body. Roughly 45% of the file. It ends with a vue-i18n config snippet (:520-529) that has nothing to do with performance.

Последствие: This is source code that happens to live in a markdown file. It will drift from the real composable within weeks and there is no test or type-check binding the two. It also buries the parts of PERFORMANCE.md that are real conventions — the fast-path caching rules (:135-143) and the polling TTL rule (:145-151) are genuinely good and are now surrounded by noise. It violates the project's own stated principle 'Код > документация — паттерны смотреть в коде' (README.md:114).

Исправление: Collapse :267-529 to the nine 'Ключевые правила' at :499-509 plus the symptom→cause table at :511-518, and point at the actual composable. Move the vue-i18n `legacy: false` note to a troubleshooting section or drop it.

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] DOC-10 — PERFORMANCE.md documents a Redis caching layer and distributed session cache; neither exists

**Статус: Исправлено.** Redis и distributed session cache удалены: их нет ни в docker, ни в src. Строка про сессии в distributed cache противоречила AUTHENTICATION.md и SECURITY.md. Часть находки про CDN опровергнута - CDN настроен и immutable-заголовки выставлены (b3c8215f)

docs/conventions/PERFORMANCE.md:200-207 — 'Caching Layer: Redis — для distributed cache; Инвалидация — pub/sub для синхронизации между инстансами'. PERFORMANCE.md:129 — 'Distributed Cache | Сессии, частые запросы'. There is no redis service in docker/docker-compose.yml (grep -i redis: zero hits) and no Redis package reference in src/. The only ICache implementation is src/DM.Infrastructure.Core/Caching/MemoryCache.cs, in-process. Sessions are in MongoDB, as AUTHENTICATION.md:19 and SECURITY.md:104 correctly state — so PERFORMANCE.md:129 also contradicts two other docs on where sessions live.

Последствие: Compounds DOC-01: a reader who believes there is a Redis-backed distributed cache with pub/sub invalidation will reasonably conclude the system is already scale-out-ready. The 'CDN' section immediately below (:209-212) is the same problem — it describes an aspirational topology in the present tense, in a conventions document where present tense means 'this is the rule'.

Исправление: Move :192-212 (Infrastructure: Database/Caching Layer/CDN) into ROADMAP.md's planned-infrastructure table where the aspirational tense is correct, or rewrite in the conditional ('если появится distributed cache — инвалидация через pub/sub'). Fix :129 to say sessions are in MongoDB or drop the row.

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] DOC-11 — PATTERNS.md's entity map and directory blueprints are inventories, and they have already drifted

**Статус: Исправлено.** Карта сущностей и восемь аннотированных деревьев заменены generic-скелетами; ложное "workers - тонкий слой оркестрации" снято и заменено правилом про границу с Domain (b3c8215f)

docs/conventions/PATTERNS.md:253-264 is a 'Карта сущностей' table listing named classes per module (Session, Token, Credentials, LoginAttempt, PersonalProfile, …) — a class-name inventory. :410-596 are eight directory trees annotated with concrete type names. Drift already present: the Domain.Core tree (:413-436) omits `Content/`, which holds ModBlockSanitizer — the write-time control point that BBCODE_RENDERING.md:65 names as authoritative. The Persistence tree (:475-493) omits `MongoIntegration/` and `RelationalStorage/`. The Web.API tree (:515-541) omits `Assets/`. Worst is the Workers blueprint (:573-580), which prescribes exactly three files and asserts 'Минимальная структура — workers это тонкий слой оркестрации'; the real workers carry Implementation/, Interceptors/, Protos/, per-worker retry middleware, and MailSendingProcessor.cs.

Последствие: Direct hit on the owner's rule (docs must not contain file lists or class names). More practically: a blueprint that no longer matches the tree is worse than no blueprint, because a contributor who follows it puts files in the wrong place and a reviewer who checks against it raises false findings. The Workers case is the sharp one — the doc asserts a property ('thin orchestration only') that the code has already outgrown, so it reads as a lie rather than a rule.

Исправление: Keep the *rules* (Infrastructure organizes by technical concern; Domain organizes by Features/{Feature} with the service/repo/DTO/validator quintet; tests mirror production) and cut the annotated trees to a single generic {Placeholder} skeleton per project type with no real folder or class names. Delete the entity map at :253-264 — the 'Кто владеет данными?' question table at :245-251 directly above it already teaches the decision, which is the part that can't be read off the code.

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] DOC-12 — No decision-record practice anywhere; every large architectural choice is stated as a rule with no rationale or reversal criteria

**Статус: Исправлено.** Каталог ADR намеренно не создан - он продублировал бы обоснования, уже лежащие рядом с правилами. Вместо него закрыты сами пробелы: Jamq и Autofac в SYSTEM.md, граница доменного и API-сервиса в PATTERNS.md, PG/Mongo и триггер окончания правила единственной миграции в DATA_STORAGE.md (b3c8215f)

No ADR directory, no decision-log file, no dated decision entries anywhere under docs/. The docs do carry inline '**Зачем:**' one-liners (SYSTEM.md:11,30,73; PATTERNS.md:13,24,43,72,86,103,118,170,176) which are a partial substitute, but the choices with the largest blast radius have none: Autofac over the built-in MS.DI container (unmentioned entirely, see DOC-06); Jamq over MassTransit (misdocumented, see DOC-07); the double service layer IGameService + IGameApiService (PATTERNS.md:39 states only 'Где применяется', with no boundary rule and no reason for the second layer); the single-InitialCreate-migration policy (stated as a bare rule in DATA_STORAGE.md:134, .claude/CLAUDE.md and LOCAL_SETUP.md:187, with no rationale and no escape hatch for when production data exists); and the PostgreSQL+MongoDB split, where DATA_STORAGE.md:14-31 gives a generic when-to-use-which table rather than the actual decision and its trade-off.

Последствие: Rules without recorded rationale are either cargo-culted or discarded on the first inconvenience, and nobody can tell which is correct. The single-migration rule is the acute case: it is enforced by a hook and repeated in three files, but nothing records what happens the day the site goes live with real user data — at which point the rule becomes impossible and there is no written trigger for retiring it. Likewise, PATTERNS.md declares 'Бизнес-логика остается в Domain' (:99,632) while UploadApiService.cs is 545 lines and CommunityStatsApiService.cs is 396 lines in the Web.API layer; with no recorded boundary rule, there is no way to say whether that is a violation or the intended design.

Исправление: Start docs/decisions/ with one short record per choice (context / decision / consequences / when to revisit) for: Autofac, Jamq, PG+Mongo, the single migration (with an explicit 'this policy ends at first production deploy' trigger), and the Domain-service/Api-service boundary. Five files. The '**Зачем:**' lines already in SYSTEM.md and PATTERNS.md are the seed text for half of them.

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] DOC-13 — docs/PROGRESS.md and docs/plans/ROADMAP.md both claim to answer 'what is done', and they disagree

**Статус: Исправлено.** Из ROADMAP.md удалены раздел "Готово" и NIST-матрица, дублировавшая SECURITY.md. Посылка находки про удаление PROGRESS.md отклонена: живой прогресс-док нужен владельцу (b3c8215f)

README.md:75 assigns ROADMAP the user story 'Что готово? Что в работе? Что планируется?' — so it owns completion status. ROADMAP.md:11-27 is a 'Готово' list; :30-32 says the single item in flight is 'Frontend страницы'. PROGRESS.md:10-29 covers the same question at higher resolution and marks essentially every functional zone ✅, including the frontend zones ROADMAP calls in-progress. The two files were last touched in different eras (PROGRESS.md carries a 2026-07-14 test run; the ROADMAP security table at :87-96 duplicates SECURITY.md:110-119 verbatim, including the correct session figure that AUTHENTICATION.md gets wrong). ROADMAP also mixes four distinct genres — completion log, plan, security compliance matrix, and a three-row tech-debt table naming a specific file (:119).

Последствие: Two competing answers to the same question, neither authoritative, both partly stale. The security compliance matrix duplicated across ROADMAP and SECURITY is a third copy of the session-lifetime fact that DOC-05 shows is already inconsistent. And a roadmap that opens with a 'Готово' section is a completion snapshot wearing a plan's title — the same rule violation as PROGRESS.md, just less blatant.

Исправление: With PROGRESS.md deleted (DOC-04), strip ROADMAP.md:11-27 ('Готово') as well — a roadmap should contain only what is not yet done. Move the NIST compliance matrix out of ROADMAP entirely; SECURITY.md is its home. Keep ROADMAP as: planned, considering, tech debt.

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] DOC-14 — GLOSSARY.md is a 469-line mirror of enum members rather than a terminology contract, and mirrors drift

**Статус: Исправлено.** GLOSSARY.md 469 -> 116 строк: транскрипции enum убраны, остались спорные термины. Заодно исправлена ложная строка про наставника блога - по коду у него премодерация публикаций, черновик и блокнот, а настройки недоступны (b3c8215f)

docs/references/GLOSSARY.md sections 3-10 enumerate essentially every enum in the domain with its members and Russian labels — ModuleStatus, DraftVisibility, ClosedReason, RoomType, RoomAccessType, AttributeSpecificationType, CharacterStatus, GameRole, BlogRole, RubricAccessType, TicketStatus, AccessPolicy, PremoderationStatus. Every one of these is readable from src/DM.Domain.Core/Enums/ in seconds. The drift this invites has already happened at :420 (the false [mod] rule, DOC-03). The parts of the file that genuinely cannot be derived from code are a small minority and are the good parts: the Mentor-UserRole vs Mentor-GameRole disambiguation (:43,163,235), the 'four independent review entities, not a unified Review' warning (:293-309), and the MessageLayout terminology contract (:450-455) that pins the same word across UI, code, CSS and docs.

Последствие: The owner's rule bars class-name and current-state inventories; an enum-member mirror is exactly that, at 469 lines. It also creates maintenance drag with no payoff — every enum change now requires a doc edit that nothing enforces, and the one time it wasn't done, a permissions rule became false.

Исправление: Cut GLOSSARY down to the RU↔EN naming *rule* plus only the terms that are genuinely ambiguous or contested: the two Mentors, the four review entities, module vs game vs blog, the BBCode vocabulary (section 12), MessageLayout. Drop every table that is a straight transcription of an enum.

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] DOC-15 — TESTING.md is a test-project inventory with no testing expectations, and its one code example contradicts the BBCode rule

**Статус: Исправлено.** Инвентарь тест-проектов заменен ожиданиями: когда integration вместо unit, зеркальная структура, обязательство matrix-тестов (перенесено из BBCODE_RENDERING.md). Пример переписан с BBCode-конвертера на pluralize (b3c8215f)

docs/guides/TESTING.md:48-63 lists all fourteen test projects by name and description — an inventory, and one that must be edited every time a project is added (ROADMAP.md:72-74 already plans two more). What the doc does not contain is any expectation: no coverage target, no rule for when an integration test is required over a unit test, no statement of what the DM.Testing project is for, no policy on the DSL folders that PATTERNS.md:590 mandates, and no guidance on the matrix-test obligation that BBCODE_RENDERING.md:151 imposes ('Добавить matrix-тесты для каждой строки (audience × роль × контекст)') — a real, specific testing convention that lives in the wrong document. Separately, the frontend example at :127-134 imports `bbcodeToHtml` from '@/shared/lib/utils/bbcode' as an ordinary shared utility, while BBCODE_RENDERING.md:11 and CODE_STYLE.md:348 both insist that converter 'используется только в модуле редактора'. The file does physically live in shared/lib/utils, so the rule as written is already false about its own location.

Последствие: The guide answers 'which test projects exist' — a question `ls test/` answers better — and not 'what is expected of me when I add code', which is the only question a testing guide should answer. The BBCode example teaches the opposite of the project's own confidentiality convention to anyone skimming for a template.

Исправление: Replace the project inventory with the testing pyramid expectations, the matrix-test obligation for privacy-sensitive tags (moved here from BBCODE_RENDERING.md:151), and the mirrored-structure rule. Change the vitest example to a non-BBCode utility, and either move bbcode.ts under the editor module or amend the convention to say what is actually true (the converter is only *imported* by the editor).

### [НИЗКАЯ] DOC-16 — Snapshots and rule violations inside UI_STANDARDS.md, plus README navigation missing a third of the conventions folder

**Статус: Исправлено.** Снимок миграции брейкпоинтов удален; три диакритические е, елочки и две латинские буквы внутри русских слов исправлены; в README добавлены три недостающих конвенции (b3c8215f)

docs/conventions/UI_STANDARDS.md:42 — 'На данный момент по токенам мигрирован только шелл (App.vue, Header.vue) и мобильный drawer — остальные компоненты с захардкоженными 768/640/600 и т.п. не трогались (будущая консолидация)': a migration-progress snapshot naming specific files, in a standards document. UI_STANDARDS.md:155 breaks two owner-mandated writing rules in one line — 'зелёный', 'приглушённый', 'передаётся' use ё (the only ё in the whole doc set outside the binary .docx), and «новый» / «архив» use ёлочки, which are reserved for the motto. Line 38 also has a Latin 'o' inside 'Однoколоночная' and line 42 a Latin 'flow' inside 'Реflow'. Separately, README.md:53-60 lists six conventions documents; docs/conventions/ contains nine — PERFORMANCE.md, UI_STANDARDS.md and URL_STRUCTURE.md are absent from the index, as is docs/PROGRESS.md and the .docx product spec.

Последствие: Three convention documents totalling ~1,200 lines are unreachable from the project's documentation index, so their rules are effectively opt-in. The ё and ёlochki instances are small but they are rules the owner has restated repeatedly, and they sit in the file most likely to be copy-pasted from when writing UI strings.

Исправление: Delete UI_STANDARDS.md:42 (or move the breakpoint-migration status to the tech-debt table in ROADMAP); fix the ё, the ёlochki and the two mixed-script typos; add PERFORMANCE, UI_STANDARDS and URL_STRUCTURE to README's conventions table and the .docx to the index.

### [НИЗКАЯ] DOC-17 — Preview environment credentials hardcoded in two docs; dev-only endpoint policy and migration escape hatch undocumented

**Статус: Исправлено.** Пароль preview убран из двух документов; в CODE_STYLE.md добавлено правило dev-only кода; у правила единственной миграции появился триггер окончания (b3c8215f)

docs/guides/DEPLOYMENT.md:53 and docs/guides/LOCAL_SETUP.md:266 both publish 'Basic Auth: `preview` / `dm2026preview`' in a repository whose deployment doc also links a public one-line VPS installer (DEPLOYMENT.md:50). Separately: src/DM.Web.API/Features/Moderation/Profiles/ModerationController.cs gates at least four endpoints on `_environment.IsDevelopment()` — including the seed endpoint that LOCAL_SETUP.md:89 documents as 'доступен только в Development' — but no conventions document states the policy for dev-only code shipped in production images (where such endpoints may live, how they must be gated, whether the gate is sufficient). And the single-migration rule (DATA_STORAGE.md:134, LOCAL_SETUP.md:187, .claude/CLAUDE.md) has no documented exit: `dotnet ef migrations remove` on a database with real data is destructive, and nothing says what to do instead once one exists.

Последствие: The preview password is a real (if low-value) credential in version control and in two places, so rotating it means remembering both. The dev-only pattern is used in production controller code with no written rule, which is how an ungated debug endpoint eventually ships. The migration policy is enforced by a hook with no documented off-ramp, which means the first production deploy will hit it as a surprise rather than a plan.

Исправление: Replace both credential lines with a pointer to docker/nginx/.htpasswd and the existing rotation command at DEPLOYMENT.md:70-73. Add a short 'Dev-only код' rule to CODE_STYLE.md (allowed shape, required gate, required test that the gate holds). Add the migration escape hatch to DATA_STORAGE.md:132-141 as an explicit trigger condition.


---

# Сборка, CI/CD, деплой, эксплуатация — D

The scaffolding is broad and thoughtfully written (healthchecks, resource limits, provisioned observability, backup scripts, layered Dockerfile), but almost every path that was never executed end-to-end is broken — CI publishes an image name nobody pulls, 3 of 4 publish matrix entries reference projects that do not exist, the frontend has no deployable path at all, cron-installed backups cannot execute, and the one-migration policy makes the next schema change a silent production data-corruption event.

## Что сделано хорошо

- Central package management with exact pinned versions and zero floating ranges (Directory.Packages.props:1-77) — no `*` or `[8.0,9.0)` anywhere; reproducible restore is genuinely solved at the package level.
- docker/docker-compose.yml is above-average operationally: every service has a healthcheck, `deploy.resources.limits`, and dependency conditions that actually express intent (`service_healthy` for infra, `service_completed_successfully` for the migration gate at lines 537-539). This is the part of the setup that shows real production thinking.
- docker/app.Dockerfile:8-37 does csproj-first restore layering correctly (solution + props + 18 csproj copied before `dotnet restore`, source copied after), and the runtime stage drops to a non-root `dmuser` (lines 58-59). Multi-stage discipline is right.
- Observability is provisioned as code, not clicked: docker/prometheus/alerts.yml has 7 real rules with sane `for:` windows, Grafana datasources and dashboards are auto-provisioned (docker/grafana/provisioning/**), Jaeger OTLP is wired. Most projects this size have Grafana configured by hand.
- .gitattributes:1-25 forces `eol=lf` on *.sh and *.ps1 — this is the single thing that prevents the classic "CRLF breaks `curl | bash` on Ubuntu" failure for setup-server.sh, and it was done deliberately.
- The backup scripts themselves are well written even though they are not wired up: `set -euo pipefail`, empty-output check (docker/scripts/backup-postgres.sh:20-23), retention pruning, optional off-site S3 replication, and docker/scripts/verify-backup.sh actually validates age, size and gzip integrity rather than just checking the file exists.
- TreatWarningsAsErrors is honored in practice: I ran a full `dotnet build DM.sln -c Release -t:Rebuild` and it completed with 0 Warning(s), 0 Error(s). The codebase is genuinely warning-clean, not warning-suppressed.
- CI does the cheap-but-valuable things right: npm cache keyed on the lockfile, GHA docker layer cache (`cache-from/to: type=gha`), and `vue-tsc` type-check enforced as a separate step rather than trusting `vite build` (which does not type-check).

## Подтвержденные находки

### [ВЫСОКАЯ] CD-01 — CI publishes to an image name that no deployment ever pulls — the delivery pipeline delivers nothing

**Статус: Исправлено.** Имена образов совпали с compose (bb5d8d2d)

**Доказательство.** .github/workflows/dotnet.yml:11 sets `IMAGE_NAME: ${{ github.repository }}` and line 119 builds `images: ${{ env.REGISTRY }}/${{ env.IMAGE_NAME }}-${{ matrix.image_suffix }}`. With `origin https://github.com/dm-am/dm3.git` (verified via `git remote -v`), CI publishes **ghcr.io/dm-am/dm3-api**. docker/docker-compose.yml:527 pulls `${API_IMAGE:-ghcr.io/dm-am/dm-api:latest}` and docker/.env.example:16 repeats that default. `dm3` != `dm-am/dm-api`. DEPLOYMENT.md:27 states a third, also-wrong name (`ghcr.io/<username>/dm3`).

**Последствие.** Watchtower (compose line 561-570, 5-minute poll, label-gated to dmapi only) polls `ghcr.io/dm-am/dm-api:latest`, a tag CI has never pushed. Every merge to main appears to "deploy" and nothing changes on any server. The documented mirror update flow (DEPLOYMENT.md:103-110 "Обновление — автоматически!") has never worked. Worse: because the tag does not exist, `docker compose up -d` on a fresh server silently falls back to building from local source, so what runs in production is whatever git state the server happened to clone — not a CI-verified artifact.

**Исправление.** Pick one canonical name and use it in all three places. Set `IMAGE_NAME: dm-am/dm` (or keep `github.repository` and change compose to `ghcr.io/dm-am/dm3-api:latest`), then delete the `build:` block from the `dmapi` service in the production compose so a missing image fails loudly instead of silently building from source.

**Проверка оппонентом.** Every cited line says exactly what the finding claims, and I could find no guard that rescues it. Confirmed facts: .github/workflows/dotnet.yml:11 (`IMAGE_NAME: ${{ github.repository }}`) + :119 (`ghcr.io/${IMAGE_NAME}-${image_suffix}`) with `origin https://github.com/dm-am/dm3.git` yields `ghcr.io/dm-am/dm3-api`; docker/docker-compose.yml:527 pulls `${API_IMAGE:-ghcr.io/dm-am/dm-api:latest}`; docker/.env.example:16 repeats that wrong default; docs/guides/DEPLOYMENT.md:27 names a third variant. Watchtower (docker-compose.yml:561-573, 300s poll, WATCHTOWER_LABEL_ENABLE, label only on dmapi at :529) therefore polls a tag CI never pushes, so the auto-update flow documented at DEPLOYMENT.md:103-110 cannot work. No override sets API_IMAGE anywhere in the repo (docker-compose.preview.yml overrides only env vars), so the refutation ground "a config elsewhere fixes it" fails.

Three further brea

### [ВЫСОКАЯ] CD-02 — Publish matrix names three projects that do not exist — worker images are never built, and every push to dev/main leaves a red CI run

**Статус: Исправлено.** Матрица publish + guard на существование проекта (bb5d8d2d)

**Доказательство.** .github/workflows/dotnet.yml:96-101 declares `DM.Services.Mail.Sender.Consumer`, `DM.Services.Search.Consumer`, `DM.Services.Notifications.Consumer`. `ls src/` returns `DM.Workers.Mail`, `DM.Workers.SearchIndexer`, `DM.Workers.NotificationDispatcher`. The name is passed as `PROJECT_NAME` (line 134) into docker/app.Dockerfile:34, which runs `dotnet restore src/${PROJECT_NAME}/${PROJECT_NAME}.csproj` — MSB1009, project file does not exist. Confirmed broken: 3 of 4 matrix legs fail at the restore layer on every run.

**Последствие.** No consumer image has ever reached GHCR. Mail sending, search indexing and notification dispatch can only be deployed by building on the server (docker-compose.yml:441/468/496 pin `dm-worker-*:local`), so the three background services that own email delivery and search freshness have no artifact, no version, and no rollback. Secondary and equally corrosive: since these jobs fail on every push to dev, the workflow is permanently red, which destroys CI as a merge gate — a genuinely broken build is indistinguishable from the standing failure.

**Исправление.** Replace the three matrix `project:` values with `DM.Workers.Mail`, `DM.Workers.SearchIndexer`, `DM.Workers.NotificationDispatcher`, and add a guard so this class of typo fails fast: `- run: test -f src/${{ matrix.project }}/${{ matrix.project }}.csproj` as the first step of the publish job.

**Проверка оппонентом.** The core mechanism is real and I reproduced the chain myself. .github/workflows/dotnet.yml:96-101 declares matrix projects DM.Services.Mail.Sender.Consumer / DM.Services.Search.Consumer / DM.Services.Notifications.Consumer; `find src -name *.csproj` returns 17 projects and no DM.Services.* exists anywhere in the tree (the only textual hits are stale bin/ artifacts from a pre-rename era). That value is passed as PROJECT_NAME at dotnet.yml:134 into docker/app.Dockerfile:34 (`RUN dotnet restore src/${PROJECT_NAME}/${PROJECT_NAME}.csproj`) → MSB1009. Tellingly, the Dockerfile's own static COPY lines 29-31 already use the correct DM.Workers.* names, proving the rename landed in the Dockerfile and was never propagated to the workflow matrix. 3 of 4 legs fail; the finding's evidence holds exactly as written.

Severity is inflated, however, on two counts that the impact paragraph gets wrong. (1)

### [КРИТИЧНО] CD-04 — Every infrastructure port is published on 0.0.0.0, MongoDB runs with no authentication, and the install script's firewall rules do not apply to Docker-published ports

**Статус: Исправлено.** То же, что CR-02: порты, аутентификация Mongo и служебные интерфейсы закрыты. OpenSearch остается с выключенным security-плагином, но доступен только с loopback; при решении выкинуть OpenSearch (PG-10) вопрос снимается вместе с ним (6b5fa1de, f9a3e8e2, 5f5ede96)

**Доказательство.** docker-compose.yml publishes postgres `5432:5432` (line 92-93), mongo `27017:27017` (117-118), rabbitmq `5672`/`15672` (196-199), minio `9000`/`9001` (244-245), opensearch `9200` with `plugins.security.disabled: 'true'` (141-145), grafana `3000` (356), prometheus `9090` (335), jaeger `16686` (313). Mongo has no `MONGO_INITDB_ROOT_USERNAME` anywhere in the file (grep confirms none) and the connection string at line 22 is `mongodb://host:27017/dm3` — no credentials, i.e. auth is off. docker/setup-server.sh:21-24 only does `iptables -I INPUT -p tcp --dport 80/443 -j ACCEPT` with no default-DROP policy, and Docker's published ports traverse the nat/DOCKER + FORWARD chains, not INPUT, so INPUT rules would not gate them even if a DROP policy existed.

**Последствие.** Any host that runs this compose file — including the preview VPS created by the documented `curl | bash` installer — exposes an unauthenticated MongoDB containing all user messages and unread state, a Postgres reachable with the password from docker/.env, RabbitMQ management with guest/<pass>, MinIO, OpenSearch with security disabled, and Grafana to the public internet. The nginx basic-auth gate (docker/nginx/nginx.conf:50-51) protects port 80 only and is irrelevant to any of these. Unauthenticated internet-facing Mongo is the single most reliably exploited data-loss vector there is. MIRRORING.md:41-46 then formalizes this by instructing operators to open 5432/27017/5672/9000 to mirror servers.

**Исправление.** Bind every infra port to loopback in the production compose (`127.0.0.1:5432:5432`, and so on for mongo/rabbit/minio/opensearch/grafana/prometheus/jaeger) — the app containers reach them over the compose network and do not need published ports at all. Enable Mongo auth (`MONGO_INITDB_ROOT_USERNAME/PASSWORD` + credentials in the connection string). For mirrors, use a WireGuard tunnel rather than opening database ports; if ports must stay published, filter in DOCKER-USER, not INPUT.

**Проверка оппонентом.** Every citation verifies against the files, and the refutation attempts all fail.

VERIFIED VERBATIM (D:\Code Projects\dm3\docker\docker-compose.yml): postgres `'5432:5432'` (92-93); mongo `'27017:27017'` (117-118) with volumes/healthcheck only — no `MONGO_INITDB_ROOT_USERNAME`/`_PASSWORD` anywhere in the file, and line 22 is `DM_ConnectionStrings__Mongo: mongodb://${MONGO_HOST:-dm-mongo}:27017/dm3?maxPoolSize=1000${MONGO_TLS:-}` — zero credentials, i.e. mongod runs with auth off and `--bind_ip_all`; opensearch `'9200:9200'` + `plugins.security.disabled: 'true'` (140-145); rabbitmq `'15672:15672'`/`'5672:5672'` (197-199); minio `'9000:9000'`/`'9001:9001'` (243-245); jaeger 16686 (313-315); prometheus 9090 (335-336); grafana 3000 (355-356). All published on 0.0.0.0. setup-server.sh:21-24 is exactly `iptables -I INPUT -p tcp --dport 80/443 -j ACCEPT` + `netfilter-persistent save`, with no `

### [КРИТИЧНО] CD-05 — Backups cannot run: cron invokes non-executable scripts, and the MinIO backup uses an mc alias that is never configured

**Статус: Исправлено.** Режим 755, alias mc, verify в cron; проверено на живом MinIO (24a43d5e)

**Доказательство.** docker/scripts/install-cron.sh:22-25 writes crontab lines that execute the scripts directly (`0 2 * * * $SCRIPT_DIR/backup-postgres.sh >> $LOG_FILE 2>&1`). `git ls-files -s docker/scripts` shows all six scripts committed as mode **100644** — a fresh `git clone` (which is exactly what setup-server.sh:19 does) yields non-executable files, so every cron invocation dies with `Permission denied`. Separately, docker/scripts/backup-minio.sh:13-15 runs `minio/mc mirror "$MINIO_ALIAS/$BUCKET" /backup` in a throwaway container with no `mc alias set` and no credentials passed — alias `minio` does not exist in a fresh mc container, so this fails even if it were executable. docker/scripts/verify-backup.sh is never added to cron at all.

**Последствие.** There are no backups. Postgres, Mongo and all user-uploaded images are unprotected, and the failure is silent from the operator's perspective — install-cron.sh prints "Backup cron jobs installed" and DEPLOYMENT.md:165-171 documents a working backup and restore story, so the first time anyone discovers this is during a restore. Even after fixing the mode bits, the MinIO leg would still fail, meaning uploads have no backup path whatsoever. verify-backup.sh — the one control that would have caught this — is written but never scheduled.

**Исправление.** `git update-index --chmod=+x docker/scripts/*.sh` (or invoke them as `bash $SCRIPT_DIR/backup-*.sh` in the crontab). Add `mc alias set minio http://dm-minio:9000 minio $MINIO_ROOT_PASSWORD` inside backup-minio.sh with the credentials passed as env. Schedule verify-backup.sh daily at 05:00 with a non-zero exit routed to an alert, and do one documented restore drill into a scratch database before treating any of this as done.

**Проверка оппонентом.** Every cited fact verified. (1) `git ls-files -s docker/scripts` returns 100644 for all six scripts; docker/setup-server.sh:19 does a fresh `git clone`, so on Linux they land non-executable, and install-cron.sh:23-25 writes crontab lines that exec the paths directly. Because setup-server.sh:41 invokes `sudo bash install-cron.sh`, the jobs land in root's crontab — and root does NOT bypass the exec permission check on a regular file with zero x-bits (execve returns EACCES), so every nightly run dies with Permission denied. `grep -rn chmod` across the whole repo yields exactly one unrelated hit (.github/workflows/security.yml:43); nothing ever sets the exec bit. (2) The install itself succeeds because it is run through `bash`, so install-cron.sh:30-34 and setup-server.sh:58 both print a working-backup message — the failure is silent, confined to /var/log/dm3-backup.log which nothing monitors

### [ВЫСОКАЯ] CD-06 — The frontend has no deployable artifact: preview compose builds from a directory that does not exist, CI never builds the SPA image, and the API serves no static files

**Статус: Исправлено.** Контекст сборки SPA переведен с несуществующего каталога на src/DM.Web.Client, в CI добавлена отдельная задача publish-frontend (у фронтенда свой контекст и Dockerfile, в матрицу .NET он не влезает без условий в каждом шаге), оверлей переведен на образ из реестра с меткой watchtower - иначе публикация снова никого не кормит. Образ собран локально, в нем index.html и ассеты (5ed790e1)

**Доказательство.** docker/docker-compose.preview.yml:44-45 declares `build: context: ../frontend/DM.Web.Modern`. That path does not exist (`ls ../frontend` → No such file or directory); the SPA lives at src/DM.Web.Client and has its own working Dockerfile + nginx.conf. The publish matrix (.github/workflows/dotnet.yml:93-101) contains only .NET projects — no frontend image is ever built or pushed. grep for `UseStaticFiles|UseSpa|MapFallbackToFile|wwwroot` across src/DM.Web.API returns nothing, so the API does not serve the SPA either. Meanwhile docker/nginx/nginx.conf:37-39 and :106-113 route `/` to `upstream frontend { server dmfront:80; }`.

**Последствие.** `docker compose -f docker-compose.yml -f docker-compose.preview.yml up -d --build` — the exact command in setup-server.sh:45 and DEPLOYMENT.md:60 — aborts with "failed to read dockerfile / path not found" before anything starts. There is no combination of files in this repo that produces a running site: no frontend image in the registry, no frontend service in the base compose, and no SPA hosting in the API. DEPLOYMENT.md:36-37 confidently links `src/DM.Web.Client/Dockerfile` as the frontend build while the only compose service that would use it points somewhere else. Answering the review question directly: the frontend is served **separately** by design (its own nginx container behind the outer nginx), and that design is consistent in nginx.conf and the docs but broken in the only file that implements it.

**Исправление.** Point the preview service at the real path (`context: ../src/DM.Web.Client`), add a fifth publish matrix entry that builds `src/DM.Web.Client/Dockerfile` into `-front`, and give the base compose a `dmfront` service so `docker compose up` produces a complete site rather than an API with no UI in front of it.

**Проверка оппонентом.** Every cited fact holds under direct inspection, so the finding survives — but the "critical / architectural dead-end" framing is wrong and the severity should be high.\n\nVERIFIED:\n1. docker/docker-compose.preview.yml:40-47 — service `dmfront`, `image: 'dm-front:local'`, `build.context: ../frontend/DM.Web.Client`... actually `../frontend/DM.Web.Modern`. Relative to docker/ that resolves to D:\\Code Projects\\dm3\\frontend\\DM.Web.Modern; `ls D:/Code Projects/dm3/frontend` → "No such file or directory". Root cause found in history: commit 9fa0535c (2026-03-09, "refactor: restructure project to Clean Architecture naming") deleted frontend/DM.Web.Modern/Dockerfile and moved the SPA to src/DM.Web.Client, and that commit does not touch docker-compose.preview.yml (its last edit is c0804fd2, 2026-02-11). The reference has been dangling ~4.5 months.\n2. Base compose has no frontend service. Ser

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] CD-10 — Prometheus scrapes three container names that do not exist, so consumer metrics are absent and ConsumerDown fires forever

**Статус: Исправлено.** Реальные имена job + /metrics на воркерах + порт управления gRPC-хоста; 6 таргетов up (a0250f1f)

**Доказательство.** docker/prometheus.yml:12-28 targets `dm-notifications-consumer:5000`, `dm-search-engine-consumer:5000`, `dm-email-sender-consumer:5000`. The actual container names in docker-compose.yml are `dm-notification-worker` (496), `dm-search-worker` (468), `dm-mail-worker` (440). docker/prometheus/alerts.yml:36-45 keys ConsumerDown off exactly those non-existent job names: `up{job=~"dm-(notifications|search-engine|email-sender)-consumer"} == 0`.

**Последствие.** Half of the scrape config resolves to nothing: three targets are permanently DOWN and the critical-severity ConsumerDown alert has been firing continuously since the day it was written. That is worse than having no alert — it trains whoever is on call to ignore the one alert that would tell them mail delivery stopped. Simultaneously, zero metrics are collected from the three consumers, so the "dm-consumers" Grafana dashboard is empty and a real consumer outage is invisible. The same stale naming appears in docker-compose.yml:36 (`SEARCH_GRPC_HOST` default) — see CD-11 — which points to a rename that was done in compose and propagated nowhere else.

**Исправление.** Rename the three scrape jobs and targets to the real container names, and update the ConsumerDown regex to match. Then add a meta-alert (`up == 0 for 15m` on any job, or `absent(up{job="dm-mail-worker"})`) so a future rename produces a distinguishable signal instead of a permanent red.

**Проверка оппонентом.** Every cited fact checks out verbatim, so the finding cannot be refuted on evidence. D:\Code Projects\dm3\docker\prometheus.yml:14-27 defines jobs/targets `dm-notifications-consumer:5000`, `dm-search-engine-consumer:5000`, `dm-email-sender-consumer:5000`; docker-compose.yml uses service keys AND container_names `dm-mail-worker` (440), `dm-search-worker` (468), `dm-notification-worker` (496) with no `aliases:` block anywhere, so no Compose DNS name resolves the old strings; docker\prometheus\alerts.yml:36-45 keys ConsumerDown off `up{job=~"dm-(notifications|search-engine|email-sender)-consumer"} == 0`; docker-compose.yml:36 still defaults `SEARCH_GRPC_HOST` to `dm-search-engine-consumer`. Three targets are permanently DOWN and the dm-consumers dashboard (docker/grafana/dashboards/dm-consumers.json, queries filtered by those job names) is empty. ### [СРЕДНЯЯ] CD-11 — The API's gRPC search endpoint defaults to a hostname no container answers to

**Статус: Исправлено.** SEARCH_GRPC_HOST=dm-search-worker; канал подключается, поиск отдает 200 вместо 503 (a0250f1f)

**Доказательство.** docker-compose.yml:36 sets `DM_SearchServiceConfiguration__GrpcEndpoint: http://${SEARCH_GRPC_HOST:-dm-search-engine-consumer}:5000` for every workload. No service in the compose file is named or aliased `dm-search-engine-consumer`; the search worker's container_name is `dm-search-worker` (line 468). docker/.env.example:31 documents the same stale default as a commented override. The value is consumed by src/DM.Web.API/Shared/Configuration/SearchServiceConfiguration.cs:11.

**Последствие.** In any environment brought up from this compose file — local dev, the ZAP CI job, the preview VPS — the API's gRPC channel to the search service resolves to a nonexistent DNS name, so every search request fails at the transport layer. This is not a UI-not-finished issue; it is a wiring defect that makes a shipped feature unreachable in every containerized environment, and it is invisible in tests because integration tests do not exercise the compose network.

**Исправление.** Change the default to `dm-search-worker`. Better: add `SEARCH_GRPC_HOST` to a startup validation path so an unresolvable gRPC endpoint fails the readiness probe instead of failing per-request.

**Проверка оппонентом.** Every factual element verified. docker/docker-compose.yml:36 does set DM_SearchServiceConfiguration__GrpcEndpoint to http://${SEARCH_GRPC_HOST:-dm-search-engine-consumer}:5000 inside the shared x-workload-env anchor, and dmapi (docker/docker-compose.yml:525-536) consumes that anchor with no override. No service, container_name, hostname, network alias or extra_hosts entry in either compose file answers to dm-search-engine-consumer; the only search service is dm-search-worker (docker/docker-compose.yml:467-468). Grep for hostname/aliases/extra_hosts across docker/docker-compose.yml and docker/docker-compose.preview.yml yields only `hostname: 'dm-rmq'` at line 196. Root cause is provable rather than inferred: commit 9fa0535c renamed `dmsearchengineconsumer` / container_name 'dm-search-engine-consumer' to dm-search-worker and left the env default pointing at the dead name. docker/.env on di

### [ВЫСОКАЯ] CD-12 — Every security gate in CI is configured to be incapable of failing

**Статус: Исправлено.** Гейты умеют падать; 9 уязвимостей -> 1 в allowlist (bb5d8d2d, c0829574)

**Доказательство.** Four independent no-ops: (1) Directory.Build.props:7 sets `NoWarn: NU1902;NU1903` — NuGet's vulnerable-package warnings are suppressed at build time, and with TreatWarningsAsErrors on they would otherwise have been the strongest gate available. (2) .github/workflows/dotnet.yml:75 pipes `dotnet list package --vulnerable` through `tee`, so the job's exit code is tee's, always 0. (3) line 82 ends `npm audit --audit-level=high || true`. (4) .github/workflows/security.yml:45-51 runs zaproxy/action-baseline without `fail_action`, which defaults to false. The ZAP rules file (.zap/rules.tsv) contains 2 IGNORE lines.

**Последствие.** The repository has a "Security Scanning" workflow, a dependency-scan job, and NuGet audit — and not one of them can turn a PR red. A known-vulnerable transitive package or a new ZAP FAIL-level finding produces green checks and log output nobody reads. This is the most expensive kind of security control: full cost, zero signal, plus the false confidence of a green badge.

**Исправление.** Drop NU1902/NU1903 from NoWarn (with the codebase currently at zero warnings, this costs nothing today and catches the next vulnerable transitive). Remove `| tee` and `|| true`, and set `fail_action: true` on the ZAP step, using .zap/rules.tsv to whitelist the findings you have actually triaged.

**Проверка оппонентом.** Refutation attempted on four grounds; all failed, and the finding is understated rather than inflated.

(1) Cited code verified verbatim. D:\Code Projects\dm3\Directory.Build.props:4 `<TreatWarningsAsErrors>true</TreatWarningsAsErrors>` and :7 `<NoWarn>$(NoWarn);NU1902;NU1903</NoWarn>`. D:\Code Projects\dm3\.github\workflows\dotnet.yml:75 `dotnet list package --vulnerable --include-transitive 2>&1 | tee /tmp/dotnet-audit.txt` and :82 `... npm audit --audit-level=high || true`. D:\Code Projects\dm3\.github\workflows\security.yml:45-51 uses zaproxy/action-baseline@v0.14.0 with target/rules_file_name/allow_issue_writing/artifact_name and no `fail_action` (action default is false); D:\Code Projects\dm3\.zap\rules.tsv has 2 IGNORE lines (10038, 10098). Every quoted line is accurate.

(2) No compensating guard exists. D:\Code Projects\dm3\.github\workflows\ contains exactly two files (dotnet.y

### [ВЫСОКАЯ] CD-13 — Working credentials are committed to the repo and auto-installed into .env by the setup scripts

**Статус: Опровергнуто.** Проверено пофайлово: все закоммиченные секреты — документированные локальные заглушки (TestP@ss123!, minio/miniokey, guest), боевых учетных данных в репозитории нет. Ключ шифрования в appsettings — отдельная находка SEC-02

**Доказательство.** docker/.env.example ships `POSTGRES_PASSWORD=TestP@ss123!`, `RABBITMQ_DEFAULT_PASS`, `MINIO_ROOT_PASSWORD`, `GF_SECURITY_ADMIN_PASSWORD`, `OPENSEARCH_PASSWORD` with the same value (lines 3-7) plus real 64-hex `IMGPROXY_KEY` and `IMGPROXY_SALT` (12-13). docker/scripts/init-env.sh:19 and scripts/dm.sh:47 copy that file to .env unconditionally, with only a printed reminder. docker/nginx/.htpasswd is **tracked in git** despite being listed in .gitignore:15 (`git ls-files docker` shows it; ignore rules do not apply to already-tracked files) and contains `preview:$apr1$jxWKSGD4$...`. docs/guides/DEPLOYMENT.md:53 prints the plaintext preview password in the repo.

**Последствие.** The imgproxy HMAC key/salt are the anti-abuse control for on-the-fly image transforms (compose lines 52-55, 275-276); published in a public repo they are worthless — anyone can sign arbitrary transform URLs and burn CPU. Any operator who follows the documented recovery from CD-07 ends up running production Postgres/RabbitMQ/MinIO/Grafana on `TestP@ss123!`, which, combined with CD-04's 0.0.0.0 port bindings, is a directly exploitable path to the database. The committed htpasswd hash plus the plaintext password in DEPLOYMENT.md means the preview gate protects nothing.

**Исправление.** Replace the secret values in .env.example with empty placeholders and have compose fail on unset (`${POSTGRES_PASSWORD:?set in .env}`). `git rm --cached docker/nginx/.htpasswd`, rotate the preview password, delete it from DEPLOYMENT.md, and regenerate IMGPROXY_KEY/SALT — treat the committed pair as burned.

**Проверка оппонентом.** SURVIVES adversarial verification, with two minor evidence corrections and one correction that strengthens it. VERIFIED HARD: (1) The repo is genuinely public — unauthenticated `git ls-remote https://github.com/dm-am/dm3.git HEAD` succeeded, returning c9977c46. (2) `docker/nginx/.htpasswd` IS tracked (`git ls-files docker` lists it) despite `.gitignore:15`, added in c88772d3 (2026-01-17), and is present at the public HEAD (`git show c9977c46:docker/nginx/.htpasswd`). (3) The credential is cryptographically proven live: `openssl passwd -apr1 -salt jxWKSGD4 dm2026preview` reproduces `$apr1$jxWKSGD4$LJAWQQrzMOJh1ddmkJ2BS.` exactly — the plaintext printed at docs/guides/DEPLOYMENT.md:52 (`preview` / `dm2026preview`) unlocks the committed hash, and both files are public right now. (4) docker/.env.example:3-7 ships five services on the identical `TestP@ss123!`; docker/scripts/init-env.sh:19 an

- **CD-09** — The migration container is pinned to a locally-built tag Watchtower never updates, so auto-deploys ship new code against an un-migrated database.
