## Средние и низкие

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] CD-16 — No root .dockerignore: 236 MB of frontend node_modules and 17 bin/obj trees are uploaded and copied into every backend image build

**Статус: Исправлено.** Проверено сборкой: контекст .NET-образов был около 1.1 ГБ, стал 1.95 МБ. Причина ровно та, что в находке: паттерны без ** привязаны к корню контекста, поэтому bin/ и obj/ отсекали только ./bin и ./obj, а все 18 деревьев src/**/obj и src/DM.Web.Client/node_modules (246 МБ) ехали внутрь. Паттерны сделаны рекурсивными, и вдобавок каталог SPA исключен целиком - его собирает собственный образ из собственного контекста, .NET-проекта там нет вовсе. Образ dm-api собран после правки успешно

There is no `.dockerignore` at the repository root; the only one is docker/app.Dockerfile.dockerignore, whose patterns (`bin/`, `obj/`, `test/`, `node_modules/`) are root-anchored and therefore match only top-level directories — `src/DM.Web.Client/node_modules` and `src/DM.Domain.*/obj` are not matched. app.Dockerfile:37 then does `COPY src/ src/`, pulling all of it into the build layer. Measured: `du -sh src/DM.Web.Client/node_modules` = 236M; `find src -type d -name obj` = 17 directories.

Последствие: Every local `docker compose build` ships a quarter-gigabyte-plus context, and — more damaging — the `COPY src/ src/` layer's cache key includes node_modules and every obj/ directory, so a routine `npm install` or a Windows `dotnet build` invalidates the .NET source layer and forces a full recompile. Copying Windows-produced obj/project.assets.json into a Linux SDK image is also a live source of confusing restore failures.

Исправление: Add a root `.dockerignore` with `**/bin`, `**/obj`, `**/node_modules`, `src/DM.Web.Client`, `test/`, `.git`, `docs/`. Backend images need none of it.

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] CD-17 — The API project-references a worker project, and the Dockerfile carries an `rm -f appsettings` hack to paper over the resulting publish collision

**Статус: Исправлено.** ProjectReference из DM.Web.API на воркер снят вместе с воркером, а с ним и хак rm -f appsettings в app.Dockerfile, существовавший только чтобы обойти NETSDK1152 от этой ссылки. Из матрицы publish убрана четвертая запись, из compose - сервис dm-search-worker

src/DM.Web.API/DM.Web.API.csproj:51 declares `<ProjectReference Include="..\DM.Workers.SearchIndexer\DM.Workers.SearchIndexer.csproj" />` with the comment "Proto types are provided by DM.Workers.SearchIndexer reference". docker/app.Dockerfile:39-43 then works around the consequence: `RUN if [ "${PROJECT_NAME}" = "DM.Web.API" ]; then rm -f src/DM.Workers.SearchIndexer/appsettings*.json; fi` — commented as "prevents NETSDK1152 error with duplicate files".

Последствие: A deployable host (the API) takes a hard build- and run-time dependency on another deployable host purely to reuse generated gRPC contract types, so the API image carries the search worker's assembly and the two cannot version independently — the exact coupling the modular-monolith layout exists to avoid. The Dockerfile hack is load-bearing but conditional on a string comparison and on files that are gitignored (CD-18), so it silently no-ops in CI and only matters on developer machines: a fragile fix for a problem that should not exist.

Исправление: Extract the .proto contracts into a small `DM.Contracts.Search` project referenced by both the API and the worker, drop the worker ProjectReference, and delete the Dockerfile `rm -f` step.

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] CD-18 — Worker appsettings.json files are gitignored and untracked while the API's is tracked — a fresh clone builds workers with no configuration

**Статус: Исправлено.** Три воркерских appsettings добавлены в гит: свежий клон больше не собирает воркеры без конфигурации. Причина была в том же слепом правиле .gitignore (f9a3e8e2)

.gitignore:11-12 ignores `**/appsettings.json` and `appsettings.*.json`. On disk: src/DM.Web.API/appsettings.json, src/DM.Workers.Mail/appsettings.json, src/DM.Workers.NotificationDispatcher/appsettings.json, src/DM.Workers.SearchIndexer/appsettings.json. In git (`git ls-files "*appsettings*"`): only `src/DM.Web.API/appsettings.json` — it predates the ignore rule and is therefore still tracked, while the three worker files exist only on this machine.

Последствие: CI and Docker build the workers from a tree where those files are absent, so worker configuration is whatever the env vars supply plus framework defaults — while the developer's local build uses a file nobody else has. Config drift between local and deployed is undetectable, and the checked-in API appsettings.json makes the ignore rule's stated purpose ("NEVER commit these") false in the one case where it matters most. src/DM.Web.API/appsettings.template.json shows the intended pattern was templates.

Исправление: Pick one convention: track non-secret appsettings.json for every host (secrets already come from `DM_*` env vars per compose) and delete the blanket ignore, or untrack the API's file and ship `appsettings.template.json` for all four. The current half-state is the worst of both.

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] CD-19 — CI compiles and tests only Debug; the configuration that actually ships is first compiled inside the publish job

**Статус: Опровергнуто.** Устарела: .github/workflows/dotnet.yml уже собирает и тестирует с -c Release, причем с комментарием, почему именно так. Ветка Debug-only, описанная в находке, в текущем файле отсутствует

.github/workflows/dotnet.yml:26-28 runs `dotnet build --no-restore` and `dotnet test --no-build` with no `-c Release`. docker/app.Dockerfile:46 publishes with `-c Release`. The publish job's `needs: [build, frontend]` therefore gates on Debug results only.

Последствие: Release-configuration-only failures (different optimization-dependent analyzer output, `#if DEBUG` divergence, and — given TreatWarningsAsErrors — any warning that only appears in Release) surface for the first time inside a docker build layer, after the gate has already gone green, in a job whose log nobody reads because 3 of its 4 legs are already failing (CD-02). No test ever executes Release-compiled code.

Исправление: Add `-c Release` to the build and test steps so the gate exercises the shipped configuration.

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] CD-20 — The nullable warning-suppression list is dead weight that only permits future regressions, and LangVersion=preview with no global.json makes the compiler version machine-dependent

**Статус: Исправлено.** Обе половины подтверждены замером. Список WarningsNotAsErrors из десяти nullable-кодов удален: Rebuild всего решения в Release дает 0 предупреждений, то есть список ничего не покупал и оставлял дыру, через которую достигнутая nullable-чистота могла молча деградировать. LangVersion переведен с preview на 12.0 - и это не гипотетическая проблема: на машине разработчика стоит только SDK 9.0.308, а CI пинует 8.0.x и образы берут sdk:8.0, то есть принимаемый язык различался между локальной сборкой и CI. Добавлен global.json с порогом 8.0.100 и rollForward latestMajor: он не ломает ни машину с SDK 9, ни CI с 8.0.x, но фиксирует нижнюю границу. Release-Rebuild после правок чистый

Directory.Build.props:5-6 sets `TreatWarningsAsErrors=true` then exempts ten nullable codes (CS8600, 8601, 8602, 8603, 8604, 8618, 8619, 8620, 8625, 8765) as "gradual adoption". I ran `dotnet build DM.sln -c Release -t:Rebuild` on the current tree: **0 Warning(s), 0 Error(s)** — the exemption list buys nothing today. Line 8 sets `<LangVersion>preview</LangVersion>` and there is no global.json in the repo (verified).

Последствие: The exemption list is now purely a hole: the next `CS8602` a developer introduces compiles clean and warning-free, so a codebase that reached full nullable compliance can silently regress out of it. `LangVersion=preview` means the accepted language is whatever SDK happens to be installed — CI pins 8.0.x via setup-dotnet, Docker uses sdk:8.0, but a developer on an SDK 9/10 machine gets a different compiler and different preview features, and code that builds locally can fail in CI (or vice versa) for reasons no error message explains.

Исправление: Delete the WarningsNotAsErrors line — it costs nothing now and locks in the win. Set `<LangVersion>12.0</LangVersion>` and add a global.json pinning `"version": "8.0.x", "rollForward": "latestFeature"`.

### [НИЗКАЯ] CD-21 — Repo junk: docker/NUL, and the ignore rule that hides it only works on Windows

**Статус: Исправлено.** Файлы NUL удалены, правило игнора регистронезависимое; заодно untracked tsbuildinfo (a0250f1f, ef9847f3)

`docker/NUL` is a 0-byte Windows null-device artifact dated Mar 15 (`ls -la docker/`). .gitignore:30 ignores lowercase `nul`; it is hidden here only because `git config core.ignorecase` is `true` on this machine. On a case-sensitive filesystem (any Linux CI runner or contributor) the pattern does not match `NUL` and the file would show up as untracked noise.

Последствие: Cosmetic today, but it is exactly the kind of file that eventually gets `git add -A`-ed into the tree, and on Linux it becomes a permanent entry in every `git status` for anyone who touches this repo from a non-Windows machine.

Исправление: Delete docker/NUL and change the ignore pattern to `[Nn][Uu][Ll]` (or just `NUL`).

### [НИЗКАЯ] CD-22 — The migration-policy hook only inspects Write, so the standard way of creating a migration bypasses it entirely

**Статус: Опровергнуто.** Уже исправлено ранее: хук переписан на node, привязан и к Bash, и к Write, и блокирует dotnet ef migrations add. Остаточное требование про Edit/MultiEdit недостижимо - MultiEdit в харнессе нет, а Edit не создает файлов

.claude/hooks/block-new-migrations.sh:13-15 returns 0 immediately unless `tool_name == "Write"`. `dotnet ef migrations add Foo` executed through Bash creates the .cs files without any Write call; an Edit or MultiEdit on a new migration file is likewise unchecked.

Последствие: The single most consequential rule in this repo (CLAUDE.md's "only InitialCreate") is enforced by a guard that catches only the least likely path to violating it. The policy is really upheld by convention, and the hook provides false assurance that it is upheld mechanically.

Исправление: Extend the hook to Edit/MultiEdit on `Migrations/*.cs`, and add a Bash-command pattern for `dotnet ef migrations add`.

### [НИЗКАЯ] CD-23 — The preview compose passes a build arg the frontend Dockerfile does not declare and the app does not read

**Статус: Исправлено.** Оверлей передавал build-arg VITE_API_URL, которого Dockerfile не объявляет - аргумент молча не делал ничего. Объявлен ARG VITE_API_HOST, оверлей передает его же (5ed790e1)

docker/docker-compose.preview.yml:46-47 passes `args: VITE_API_URL: ""`. src/DM.Web.Client/Dockerfile declares no `ARG` at all and hardcodes `ENV VITE_API_HOST=""` at line 16. A grep of `import.meta.env.VITE_*` across src/DM.Web.Client/src finds exactly one variable in use: `VITE_API_HOST`.

Последствие: The build arg is silently discarded — Docker warns and continues. Today the hardcoded ENV happens to produce the intended empty value, so nothing is visibly broken; the moment someone tries to point a mirror's frontend at a different API origin by setting VITE_API_URL, it will have no effect and the misconfiguration will be invisible in both build output and runtime behavior.

Исправление: Delete the unused arg from the preview compose, or (better) declare `ARG VITE_API_HOST` in the Dockerfile, pass it from compose, and drop the hardcoded ENV.


---

# Процесс разработки (человек + агент) — C-

The bespoke process artifacts (the DI regression test, PROGRESS.md, the two custom skills) are genuinely senior-grade, but the mechanical layer underneath is unsound — the git guardrail is fully bypassed by the PowerShell tool, the migration guardrail watches the wrong tool, the entire .claude/ policy layer is gitignored, CI's publish stage has been red for 4.5 months, agent configs describe a frontend that no longer exists, and work lands in 300–1300-file commits with a month of uncommitted tree; the process works only because one person's personal rigor is compensating for every missing gate.

## Что сделано хорошо

- test/DM.Web.API.Tests/Features/General/DbContextScopeResolutionShould.cs is a textbook postmortem-to-test conversion: it rebuilds the exact production wiring order (ServiceCollection.AddDbContextPool → builder.Populate → RegisterModule<PersistenceModule>) rather than mocking it, and asserts four independent properties — one instance per scope, repositories share the scope's instance (via reflection on the private field), distinct instances across scopes, and ContextId.Lease > 0 to prove the pool was actually engaged. The XML doc block (lines 16-26) explains the failure mode in terms of consequence (transactions never spanned service and repository) rather than mechanism. Most teams would have written one assertion and moved on.
- docs/PROGRESS.md is a real verification ledger, not a status doc: exact counts (1697+54 backend tests, 798/798 vitest, seed inventory of 22 users / 69 games / 9326 posts), an explicit 53/53 live-pass result, a regression it found and fixed during that pass (/game/{id}/details OOM from a cartesian projection → AsSplitQuery, ~0.4s), and an honest open flag it did NOT close (SignalR /whatsup negotiate-404). Objective numbers and admitted gaps — this is what proof-of-correctness should look like.
- .claude/skills/requirement-ledger/SKILL.md encodes a discipline most teams never formalize: a status vocabulary that distinguishes 🔨 done-but-unverified from 👁 verified-live, an absolute rule that a visual requirement is never marked verified without owner approval or objective browser measurement, a hard split between the autonomous track (functional/invisible) and the approval-gated track (visible), and a recovery procedure that reconstructs lost requirements from .jsonl transcripts rather than guessing.
- .claude/skills/skeleton-parity/SKILL.md correctly rejects screenshots as the verification method and prescribes objective measurement instead — compare getBoundingClientRect().height plus computed margin/padding/gap of the same slot in both skeleton and loaded phase, with a >4px threshold. Naming the tolerance is what makes it a check rather than a vibe.
- Commit message bodies are excellent for a solo project — 3cc08d03 and f217a249 group changes by subsystem, state the before-state of each change ("was div-based equal-width cells", "was duplicated as PulsePostSkeleton"), and record deletions and renames explicitly. The information density is far above the industry norm; the problem is the commits they are attached to, not the writing.
- CI breadth is real for a one-person project: .github/workflows/dotnet.yml runs build, full dotnet test, frontend type-check, build and coverage, plus a dependency-scan job covering both dotnet list package --vulnerable --include-transitive and npm audit; security.yml stands up the actual docker-compose stack (postgres, mongo, rabbitmq, minio, mailhog, opensearch, jaeger, migration, dmapi), waits on /_health, and runs an OWASP ZAP baseline on push and weekly cron. Integration tests use Testcontainers against real PostgreSQL rather than InMemory.

## Подтвержденные находки

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] P1 — The git guardrail does not apply to the PowerShell tool — CLAUDE.md's critical_rules are unenforced on the harness's Windows-native path

**Статус: Исправлено.** matcher хуков расширен на PowerShell, block-new-migrations принимает его tool_name; проверено: git reset --hard и создание второй миграции через PowerShell блокируются, штатные команды проходят. Локально: .claude не в гите

**Доказательство.** .claude/settings.json:5 registers the PreToolUse hook with matcher "Bash" only. This harness also exposes a first-class PowerShell tool. I verified the hole empirically during this audit: `git checkout -h` submitted through the PowerShell tool executed and returned git's usage text with no hook interception, while the identical string submitted through Bash was blocked by node .claude/hooks/block-dangerous-git.js. There is no matcher for PowerShell in settings.json, and .claude/settings.local.json additionally pre-approves Bash(powershell:*) and Bash(pwsh:*), giving a second unguarded route from inside Bash itself.

**Последствие.** The entire <critical_rules> block of .claude/CLAUDE.md (lines 7-21) is advisory on Windows, where PowerShell is the natural tool choice. `git checkout .` or `git reset --hard` through that path destroys the working tree — which currently holds 816 modified files and 198 untracked files representing 25 days of uncommitted work. The owner believes this is mechanically prevented; it is not.

**Исправление.** Add the PowerShell tool name to the PreToolUse matcher in .claude/settings.json (matcher "Bash|PowerShell"), and remove Bash(powershell:*) / Bash(pwsh:*) from the allowlist or route them through the same hook. Then re-test both paths — a guardrail nobody has attempted to defeat is an untested guardrail.

**Проверка оппонентом.** Core mechanical claim verified: .claude/settings.json:5 sets PreToolUse matcher "Bash" only, which cannot match the PowerShell tool name, and PowerShell is a live path (122 PowerShell tool_use blocks across the owner's transcripts in C:\Users\Ivan\.claude\projects\D--Code-Projects-dm3\*.jsonl, 15 of them running git: add -A, commit, stash push/apply, show). Dirty-tree exposure is accurate (last commit 0ed49eff 2026-07-02, 1030 porcelain lines: 709 M / 198 untracked / 55 D). So a `git checkout .` or `git reset --hard` issued through PowerShell is not intercepted by .claude/hooks/block-dangerous-git.js, and the one-line fix (matcher "Bash|PowerShell") is correct. BUT severity "critical" is inflated on three grounds. (1) The finding's second evidence pillar is factually WRONG: it claims Bash(powershell:*) / Bash(pwsh:*) in the allowlist give "a second unguarded route from inside Bash itself

### [НИЗКАЯ] P4 — The migration guardrail watches the Write tool, but migrations are created by the EF CLI — the only real path is unguarded and pre-approved

**Статус: Исправлено.** matcher хуков расширен на PowerShell, regex ловит форму dotnet-ef. По решению владельца хук уточнен: запрещена вторая миграция (add с любым именем кроме InitialCreate), штатный цикл пересоздания разрешен - иначе единственным путем остается ручная правка трех файлов, из которой и пришло расхождение снапшота. Совпадение ищется от начала команды: подстрочная проверка блокировала даже упоминание фразы в тексте. 6 случаев проверены полезной нагрузкой

**Доказательство.** .claude/settings.json:14 binds block-new-migrations.sh to matcher "Write", and the script itself early-exits unless tool_name == "Write" (block-new-migrations.sh:11-13). The canonical way to add a migration is `dotnet ef migrations add <Name>`, which writes the .cs files via the EF tool, not the Write tool. I confirmed that command matches none of the block patterns, and .claude/settings.local.json pre-approves Bash(dotnet:*), Bash(PGPASSWORD=postgres dotnet ef database update:*) and Bash(MigrateOnStart=true dotnet run:*).

**Последствие.** The one-migration policy — which the project's own CLAUDE.md marks КРИТИЧНО and which the codebase depends on structurally (commit 0ed49eff's body says it "regenerate[d] the InitialCreate migration") — is enforced only against an agent that hand-authors migration .cs files, a path no agent takes. The guardrail creates false confidence in exactly the area where a mistake is hardest to unwind, because a stray second migration diverges the local DB from the single-migration assumption silently.

**Исправление.** Move the check to the Bash/PowerShell PreToolUse hook and match on `dotnet ef migrations add` and `Add-Migration`. Keep the Write matcher as a secondary net. Also worth stating: a >1700-test project past InitialCreate is at the point where the single-migration rule costs more than it saves — reconsider it before first production deploy, not after.

**Проверка оппонентом.** MECHANISM CONFIRMED, SEVERITY WRONG BY THREE STEPS. Every citation verified: .claude/settings.json:13-21 binds block-new-migrations.sh to matcher "Write"; block-new-migrations.sh:11-13 early-exits unless tool_name=="Write"; block-dangerous-git.js:17-22 matches only four git patterns and nothing migration-related; settings.local.json:6,161,178 pre-approve Bash(dotnet:*), Bash(MigrateOnStart=true dotnet run:*), Bash(PGPASSWORD=postgres dotnet ef database update:*). dotnet-ef 8.0.8 is installed globally, .git/hooks/ contains only samples, and no CI job or test asserts a migration count. So the EF CLI path genuinely bypasses the guard — the finding is not factually wrong and I will not call it refuted.

But the impact analysis is self-refuting. It cites commit 0ed49eff ("...and regenerate the InitialCreate migration") as proof the codebase "depends on [the rule] structurally"; wholesale rege

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] P5 — CI's publish stage has been failing on every push to dev for ~4.5 months and nobody noticed

**Статус: Исправлено.** Publish зеленый впервые, проверено прогоном CI (bb5d8d2d)

**Доказательство.** .github/workflows/dotnet.yml publish matrix builds projects DM.Services.Mail.Sender.Consumer, DM.Services.Search.Consumer and DM.Services.Notifications.Consumer. Those directories do not exist — `ls src` shows DM.Workers.Mail, DM.Workers.SearchIndexer and DM.Workers.NotificationDispatcher, renamed in commit 9fa0535c (2026-03-09, "refactor: restructure project to Clean Architecture naming"). docker/app.Dockerfile:34 runs `dotnet restore src/${PROJECT_NAME}/${PROJECT_NAME}.csproj`, which cannot resolve, so three of the four matrix legs fail deterministically on every push to main or dev.

**Последствие.** Three of four container images have not been published since March. More corrosive than the broken images: the team has been living with a permanently red pipeline for 4.5 months, which means CI status carries zero signal — a genuine build or test failure now looks exactly like the ambient red. This is the mechanism by which every other CI gate in the repo quietly stops mattering.

**Исправление.** Fix the three matrix project names, then make red CI actionable: branch protection on dev requiring the build and frontend jobs to pass. If the publish leg is not actually wanted right now, delete it rather than leaving it red.

**Проверка оппонентом.** The mechanical claim holds and I verified every cited fact. .github/workflows/dotnet.yml:96-101 does reference DM.Services.Mail.Sender.Consumer, DM.Services.Search.Consumer and DM.Services.Notifications.Consumer; those projects exist nowhere (ls src shows only DM.Workers.{Mail,NotificationDispatcher,SearchIndexer}; `grep "DM.Services" DM.sln` returns nothing; the only live references repo-wide are the workflow itself and docs/guides/DEPLOYMENT.md:24,27). docker/app.Dockerfile:34 is indeed `RUN dotnet restore src/${PROJECT_NAME}/${PROJECT_NAME}.csproj`, which fails with MSB1009 for a nonexistent path, so those three matrix legs cannot succeed. Commit 9fa0535c is real (Mon Mar 9 2026) and it DID update docker/app.Dockerfile:29-31 to the new worker names while leaving the workflow matrix stale — and the workflow was edited again afterwards in c9977c46 (Apr 8) without anyone catching it. So 

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] P6 — Work lands in 300–1300-file commits with no PR and no branch — nothing is reviewable, bisectable, or revertible

**Статус: Открыто.** 

**Доказательство.** Measured with git show --stat over the last 25 commits: c9977c46 = 1341 files / +82655 / −25313; 9fa0535c = 3352 files / +109334 / −106695; 0ed49eff = 551 files / +28778 / −10722; 91b5e571 = 328 files; f217a249 = 297 files. All 25 commits authored in 2026 are by a single author (git shortlog --since=2026-01-01) landing directly on dev; `git log --merges` shows the newest merge commit predates the fork. Branch dev is the working branch.

**Последствие.** 0ed49eff bundles UI polish, a profile restructure, deletion of the entire legacy upload subsystem and a migration regeneration into one commit — reverting any one of those means reverting all four. git bisect over a 28k-line commit tells you nothing. The excellent commit bodies are release notes on a release-sized blob, not a review unit. This is also why the DI-shadowing bug in P-strengths took a manual hunt to find: no diff in this history was ever small enough to read.

**Исправление.** One behavioural change per commit, feature branch plus self-merged PR so CI is a merge gate rather than a post-hoc notification. Ship the current 816-file tree in themed slices, not as commit #26.

**Проверка оппонентом.** CORE SURVIVES, SEVERITY INFLATED, THREE SUPPORTING PILLARS REFUTED.

What I verified and could not refute:
- Commit sizes are exact. `git show --numstat` per commit: 0ed49eff=551 files (+28778/−10722), c9977c46=1341 (+82655/−25313), f217a249=297 (+9983/−8866), 91b5e571=328, 9fa0535c=3352 (+109334/−106695). All five cited numbers reproduce.
- Single author: all 25 commits are AllicGator <allic.gator@gmail.com>, dated 2026-01-17..2026-07-02, all reachable from dev.
- Newest merge commit is e6169418 (2024-09-01), predating every 2026 commit. No merge-based flow in the current work.
- 0ed49eff genuinely bundles four unrelated concerns (guest UX, legacy upload subsystem deletion, profile restructure, migration regen) into one revert unit. That specific harm is live and real.
- Additionally worse than reported: origin/dev = c9977c46 while local dev = 0ed49eff, so the last three commits (551+41

### [ВЫСОКАЯ] P7 — 25 days of uncommitted work: 816 modified files, 198 untracked, sitting in the tree the guardrails fail to protect

**Статус: Исправлено.** Дерево закоммичено и запушено

**Доказательство.** git status --porcelain returns 1030 entries; git diff --shortstat = 816 files changed, +26635 / −16650; 198 untracked; 71 files partially staged (git diff --cached --shortstat). Last commit 0ed49eff is dated 2026-07-02, today is 2026-07-27. Distribution: 547 entries under src/DM.Web.Client, 129 under src/DM.Web.API, 71 under src/DM.Infrastructure.Persistence.

**Последствие.** A month of work exists in exactly one place, on one disk, with no history. This is the single largest concentration of risk in the project, and it is the direct reason the git hooks exist — yet the hooks are bypassable (P1, P2) and the untracked files are outside even stash's protection. Secondary cost: PROGRESS.md's verification numbers (798/798 vitest) already disagree with the current suite size, because the doc and the code drifted apart inside this uncommitted window.

**Исправление.** Commit today, in slices. Then set a rule that the tree is empty at the end of each session — with commit granularity from P6, that is cheap rather than heroic.

**Проверка оппонентом.** I tried to break this one and could not. Every number in the evidence reproduces exactly: `git status --porcelain` = 1030 entries; `git diff --shortstat` = 816 files, +26635/-16650; 198 `??` entries; `git diff --cached --shortstat` = 71 files; distribution 547 / 129 / 71 under src/DM.Web.Client, src/DM.Web.API, src/DM.Infrastructure.Persistence; HEAD 0ed49eff dated 2026-07-02 vs today 2026-07-27 = 25 days. No refutation ground held. I checked whether the delta was CRLF/whitespace noise inflating the count -- `git diff --numstat -w` still returns 816 files with 0 whitespace-only files, so the churn is real content. I checked whether the stashes constitute a durable snapshot: stash@{0} (178 files) and stash@{1} (183 files) are pre-0ed49eff work, covering ~22% of the current delta and living in the same .git on the same disk, so they are not mitigation.

The finding is in fact UNDERSTATED. 

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] P8 — Agent definitions describe a frontend architecture that no longer exists and cite four documentation files that were deleted

**Статус: Исправлено.** Все три определения агентов проверены и приведены к реальности. frontend-developer описывал структуру api/components/composables/pages/stores/styles/utils, которой нет (в проекте FSD), требовал SCSS при 292 файлах на lang="sass" и перечислял три несуществующих ключевых файла - переписан на реальные слои и, что важнее, перестал содержать инвентарь файлов: он устаревает при первом же переносе и подсовывает свежему контексту ложные факты. Все четыре битые ссылки на удаленные документы перенацелены на существующие (проверено обходом ссылок: 0 битых). У debugger исправлены имена контейнеров dm-postgres и dm-rabbitmq на реальные dm-pg и dm-rmq и добавлен запрет создавать миграции

**Доказательство.** .claude/agents/frontend-developer.md:20-29 documents the structure as api/ components/ composables/ pages/ stores/ styles/ utils/ — actual `ls src/DM.Web.Client/src` is app assets entities features pages shared widgets (FSD). Lines 59-62 list src/api/client.ts, src/stores/auth.ts, src/composables/useApi.ts as "Key Files"; I checked each — all three MISSING. Line 15 and the output template at line 103 specify SCSS and `<style scoped lang="scss">`; grep across all .vue files returns 292 occurrences of lang="sass" and zero of lang="scss". code-reviewer.md:24, :30, :40 and :49 reference docs/reference/standards.md, docs/architecture/patterns.md and docs/architecture/security.md — all MISSING since the doc reorg in 97e7d328/944e1dfc. debugger.md:16 cites docs/architecture/overview.md (MISSING) and lines 31-33 use container names dm-postgres / dm-rabbitmq, where docker/docker-compose.yml:90 an

**Последствие.** An agent invoked with these definitions will write SCSS into a directory tree that does not exist, import from files that were deleted, and run docker log commands against container names that never match. The code-reviewer agent's whole rule set points at four missing documents, so its "Reference:" lines resolve to nothing and its judgments silently fall back to generic priors. These are worse than absent config — they actively inject wrong facts into a fresh context.

**Исправление.** Delete all three agent files. Their content is either stale, or duplicated in docs/conventions/, or generic enough that the base model already has it. If agent specialisation is wanted later, generate the structure section from the tree at invocation time rather than transcribing it.

**Проверка оппонентом.** All 8 citations verified true, no refutation ground survives. VERIFIED: frontend-developer.md:21-29 lists api/ components/ composables/ pages/ stores/ styles/ utils/ — all six absent (actual src/DM.Web.Client/src = app assets entities features pages shared widgets); :59-62 cites src/api/client.ts, src/stores/auth.ts, src/composables/useApi.ts — all three MISSING; :16/:103 specify SCSS while grep gives 292 lang="sass", 0 lang="scss", 0 .scss files, 23 .sass files — and vite.config.ts configures ONLY css.preprocessorOptions.sass with additionalData injecting Variables/Breakpoints/Layout/Themes in indented syntax, so a lang="scss" block per the agent's own template gets zero globals and fails on the first $variable (impact is stronger than the finding claimed); code-reviewer.md:23,30 → docs/reference/standards.md MISSING (real dir is docs/references/, API standards live in docs/conventions/

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] P9 — No automated gate exists for any of the bug classes the owner keeps finding by hand

**Статус: Открыто.** 

**Доказательство.** Visual regressions: grep for toHaveScreenshot|toMatchSnapshot|percy|chromatic across src/DM.Web.Client returns zero hits, despite playwright.config.ts and 20+ specs under e2e/tests/. The e2e suite is not in CI — grep for 'lint|e2e|playwright' across .github/workflows/*.yml returns nothing, and the frontend job runs only type-check, build and test:coverage. ESLint is absent from CI, and package.json's lint script hardcodes --fix, so it mutates rather than reports and cannot serve as a gate. Frontend coverage thresholds in vite.config.ts:38-45 are lines 7 / functions 5 / branches 5, with a stale comment at line 40 ("Current coverage is ~7.65%") against a suite that now has 844 tests. .claude/settings.json declares only PreToolUse — there is no PostToolUse or Stop hook, so nothing runs after an edit locally either.

**Последствие.** Every class of defect the owner reports catching manually — skeleton layout shift, CSS transition freezes, theme flashes, DI lifetimes, EF query shape — has zero machinery behind it. The skeleton-parity skill prescribes exactly the right measurement (getBoundingClientRect, >4px threshold) and then leaves it to be executed by hand, per component, on demand. The 7% coverage threshold is a gate calibrated to pass, which is worse than no threshold because it reads as one on the dashboard.

**Исправление.** Three concrete additions, in order of value per hour: (1) Playwright toHaveScreenshot on ~10 representative pages in both themes and at 375px, run in CI — this is the one that pays for the skeleton and theme-transition bug classes automatically; (2) add `eslint --max-warnings=0` (no --fix) and the e2e suite to the frontend CI job; (3) raise coverage thresholds to just under actual measured coverage and make it a ratchet.

**Проверка оппонентом.** Every cited fact verified by reading the files, and one is understated: I ran `npm run test:coverage` and measured lines 12.61 / functions 29.48 / branches 66.32 / statements 12.61 against vite.config.ts:38-45 thresholds of 7/5/5/7 — branch coverage could collapse from 66% to 5% with CI still green, and the comment at vite.config.ts:40 ("Current coverage is ~7.65%") is stale. Confirmed too: .github/workflows/dotnet.yml frontend job runs only `npm ci`, `type-check`, `build`, `test:coverage` (grep for e2e|playwright|lint across .github/*.yml returns nothing); package.json:15 lint hardcodes `--fix`; zero hits for toHaveScreenshot|toMatchSnapshot|percy|chromatic|argos anywhere in src/ or e2e/; .claude/settings.json declares PreToolUse only. HOWEVER the title and impact paragraph are materially overstated, which is why severity drops to medium. "No automated gate exists for ANY of the bug cla

## Опровергнуто оппонентом (в работу не берем)

- **P2** — Within Bash, the block list is a regex denylist that misses every equivalent destructive command, while pre-approving worse ones. Причина отклонения: The regex-bypass mechanics reproduce exactly (I ran all 13 commands against the four patterns from .claude/hooks/block-dangerous-git.js:17-22: git restore --source=HEAD ., git -C . checkout ., git switch -f main, git clean -f, git stash clear, git push --force, git reflog expire, git update-ref -d, and rm -rf src/ all pass; only the four literal forms are caught). But the finding fails on three in
- **P3** — The whole .claude/ policy layer is gitignored — guardrails, agents, skills and CLAUDE.md have no version control, no history, no review, no backup. Причина отклонения: The mechanical facts hold (`.gitignore:22` = `.claude/`; `git ls-files .claude/` returns 0; the 12 listed files exist), but the load-bearing impact claim is factually false and the severity is off by three levels.

(1) FALSE PREMISE — "erases the only written statement of the git and migration policy". The migration rule is stated verbatim in tracked, version-controlled docs: `docs/conventions/DAT
- **P10** — The modular monolith's central invariant — no Domain.X → Domain.Y references — is enforced by an LLM reading diffs on request, not by a test. Причина отклонения: The finding's premise — "no mechanical enforcement, holds only while an LLM reviews diffs" — is false. The boundary is enforced by the C# compiler across physical assembly boundaries. All nine Domain modules are separate assemblies each declaring exactly ONE ProjectReference (src/DM.Domain.{Account,Blog,Community,Forum,Game,Messaging,Moderation,Personal}/*.csproj -> ..\DM.Domain.Core\DM.Domain.Cor

## Средние и низкие

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] P11 — CLAUDE.md is at the wrong altitude — it carries settled-argument trivia while the rules that actually govern daily work live outside the repo

**Статус: Исправлено.** Дублирование снято по принципу самого файла: чек-лист ревью и три разобранных ложных срабатывания переехали в code-reviewer, где им место, CLAUDE.md ссылается. Ничего не потеряно - два пункта чек-листа, которых у ревьюера не было (пустые XML-теги, admin-эндпоинты без RequireRole), добавлены туда явно. Обратное движение тоже сделано: правила русского текста (буква е, прямые кавычки) были только в машинно-локальной памяти, теперь записаны в CODE_STYLE.md, то есть видны в репозитории и доступны свежему агенту на чистом клоне. Правило единственной миграции снабжено ссылкой на условие своего окончания

.claude/CLAUDE.md:48-52 ("Что НЕ проблема") lists three rebuttals to specific past review claims — route ordering, MinimumLength=1 on login, GeneralError for invalid tokens. Lines 54-60 list five review checks that duplicate .claude/agents/code-reviewer.md:19-26. Meanwhile the rules that shape every session — FSD layering, lang="sass", the no-ё rule, the numbered-catalog approval protocol for visual changes, the requirement ledger, the design-language separator rules — appear nowhere in the repo; they live in C:/Users/Ivan/.claude/projects/D--Code-Projects-dm3/memory/*.md (28 files), machine-local and untracked.

Последствие: Roughly a third of the project's instruction file is residue from arguments that were resolved once, which is memory-of-a-conversation, not policy. The genuinely load-bearing conventions are in a location git cannot see, no teammate can read, and no CI job can consult — so a fresh agent on a clean clone gets the trivia and none of the rules.

Исправление: Cut lines 39-60 from CLAUDE.md. Promote the durable conventions out of the private memory directory into docs/conventions/ (the sass/FSD/ё rules) and into a tracked .claude/CLAUDE.md (the approval protocol and ledger pointer). Keep CLAUDE.md to the rules an agent must obey and could not infer from the code.

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] P12 — The requirement ledger — the stated SSOT for every owner instruction and approval — is not under version control, and its own skill file claims it is

**Статус: Открыто.** 

.claude/skills/requirement-ledger/SKILL.md asserts of C:/Users/Ivan/.claude/projects/D--Code-Projects-dm3/memory/REQUIREMENTS.md: "под гитом, виден владельцу, переживает всё". I checked: `git rev-parse --show-toplevel` in that directory returns "fatal: not a git repository (or any of the parent directories): .git". The file is 61046 bytes / 286 lines and was last written 2026-07-27.

Последствие: The single artifact designed to survive context compaction — the record of what was asked, approved, rejected and verified — has no history, no backup and no diff. Its stated recovery path (reconstruct from .jsonl transcripts) sits in the same untracked directory. Worse than the exposure itself: the skill file states the opposite, so the gap will never be noticed, and the ledger's own status column (🔨 vs 👁) becomes unauditable — you cannot tell when a requirement was marked verified or by what evidence.

Исправление: Either git init the memory directory (a private local repo is enough — this is owner-only data by explicit decision) or move REQUIREMENTS.md into the tracked repo. Then correct the SKILL.md claim either way; a false assurance in a process doc is worse than a missing one.

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] P13 — A dev-only mockup route ships to production with a TODO comment as its removal plan

**Статус: Исправлено.** То же, что HYG-17. Уточнение проверки: артефакт DigestVariantsMockup, на который ссылалась находка, не существует и не существовал никогда - вывод про привычку при этом верен (c0d9d638)

src/DM.Web.Client/src/app/providers/router.ts:831-843 registers name "dev-style-variants", path "/dev/style-variants", loading @/pages/dev/StyleVariantsPage.vue, with the comment "Dev-only mockup catalog (style variants) — removed once the owner picks the variants." There is no import.meta.env.DEV guard — grep for DEV guards across src/app/router returns nothing. The same pattern has already shipped once: src/DM.Web.Client/dist/assets/ contains built DigestVariantsMockup-*.js and .css chunks.

Последствие: A publicly reachable internal design catalog in the production bundle, plus a lazy chunk in the build output. The build artifact proves this is a recurring habit, not a one-off. The approach itself is right — reviewing variants as real components in the real app beats screenshots — the defect is purely that nothing removes them and a comment is not a mechanism.

Исправление: Wrap dev route registration in `if (import.meta.env.DEV)` so the chunk is tree-shaken from production builds; keep the pages. That converts a manual cleanup obligation into a build-time property and lets the catalog pattern be used freely.

### [НИЗКАЯ] P14 — The permission allowlist has decayed into transcript sludge, hiding the handful of grants that actually matter

**Статус: Открыто.** 

.claude/settings.local.json permissions.allow holds 222 entries. Roughly 40 are one-shot curl benchmark loops pasted verbatim from past sessions, several including box-drawing characters inside the format string — e.g. `Bash(do curl -s -o /dev/null -w "│   Call $i: %{time_total}s | %{size_download} bytes                           │\n" "http://localhost:5000/v1/games/tags")`. Another entry is a 16-path `for f in docs/guides/SETUP.md …` loop over files that no longer exist. Interleaved with these are the grants that carry real authority: Bash(rm:*), Bash(powershell:*), Bash(git:*), Bash(chmod:*), Bash(taskkill:*), Bash(winget install:*).

Последствие: Nobody will ever audit a 222-entry list in which a fifth of the entries are dead literal strings, which means Bash(rm:*) and Bash(winget install:*) — arbitrary deletion and arbitrary software installation, both pre-approved with no prompt — are effectively invisible. The dead entries never match again, so the list grows monotonically and the signal-to-noise ratio only worsens.

Исправление: Prune to the ~30 broad-pattern entries that actually match, and review that short list deliberately — in particular whether Bash(rm:*) and Bash(winget install:*) should be prompt-free given P2. Then stop letting one-shot commands accrete: prefer approving the tool pattern once over accepting per-invocation entries.


---

# Гигиена кода и техдолг — C

Surface hygiene is exemplary — 0 TODO/FIXME in 167k lines of C#, zero commented-out code, zero console.log, TreatWarningsAsErrors on — but that clean surface hides a tech-debt inventory containing committed encoding corruption that permanently hard-fails the notification worker and mangles ~118 user-facing Russian strings, a duplicate route registration on an endpoint the SPA actively calls, a 5,422-line seed god-file shipped inside the production API assembly, and four byte-identical duplicated background services; the absence of debt *markers* is not the absence of debt.

## Что сделано хорошо

- Comment/logging discipline is genuinely exceptional and rare at this scale: `grep -rn 'TODO|FIXME|HACK|XXX'` over 1740 .cs files returns **zero** hits, and the frontend has exactly **two** (src/DM.Web.Client/src/pages/warnings/WarningsPage.vue:4, src/DM.Web.Client/src/shared/ui/ErrorPage/errorConfig.ts:28). Zero commented-out code blocks in either stack. Zero `console.log` in 325 .vue + 380 .ts files (only 12 deliberate console.error/warn).
- Exception handling is disciplined rather than defensive. Of 65 catch sites, the domain ones use exception *filters* not bare catch-alls — src/DM.Domain.Account/Features/Authentication/AuthenticationService.cs:153 filters `ex is JsonException or KeyNotFoundException or FormatException or CryptographicException`; src/DM.Domain.Game/Features/GameReviews/GameReviewService.cs:100 filters `ex.InnerException is PostgresException { SqlState: "23505" }` to convert a unique-violation into a 409. Every catch-all in the HostedServices tree logs with the exception object and documents *why* it swallows ("Don't throw - we want the service to continue running"). No empty catch blocks anywhere.
- Async hygiene is clean: **zero** `async void`, zero `.Result`/`.Wait()`/`GetAwaiter().GetResult()` blocking calls in 167k lines (all 25 `.Result` grep hits are the `PagingData.Result` property, a false positive). No fire-and-forget except one deliberately-documented case (src/DM.Web.API/HostedServices/WarmupService.cs:65, wrapped in try/catch and logged as non-critical).
- Authorization is properly centralized rather than scattered. 81 role comparisons exist, but 56 of them live inside dedicated `*IntentionResolver` classes (src/DM.Domain.Blog/Authorization/BlogIntentionResolver.cs, src/DM.Domain.Game/Authorization/GameIntentionResolver.cs, etc.). Only ~8 leak into service code, and all use the ordinal `Role >= UserRole.X` idiom rather than enumerated equality — one consistent convention.
- Directory.Build.props sets `TreatWarningsAsErrors=true` globally with an explicit, narrow, *documented* nullable escape hatch (`WarningsNotAsErrors` listing exactly CS8600–CS8765 "for gradual adoption"). This is why the codebase has no unused-using/unreachable-code rot — the compiler is the enforcement mechanism, not a linting ritual.
- FSD layer discipline on the frontend is real, not aspirational. PATTERNS.md:139 forbids same-layer imports outside `@x`; a sweep of all entities/features/widgets found exactly **1** cross-slice deep import (src/DM.Web.Client/src/entities/user/model/useAvatarUpload.ts:4, importing its own slice barrel) and **3** barrel-bypassing deep imports total. The `@x` mechanism is actually used (src/DM.Web.Client/src/entities/user/@x/).
- The "fat service interface" choice is deliberate and documented, not accidental sprawl. PATTERNS.md:24 explicitly argues for one interface per aggregate ("Один интерфейс вместо трех-четырех"), and src/DM.Domain.Blog/Features/Blogs/BlogService.cs (978 lines, covering Blogs + Publications + Rubrics + Subscriptions) is the honest execution of that decision rather than a God-file. Same for GameService.cs. I would not split these.
- Paging has a single implementation with no duplicates: all 28 construction sites go through the one `PagingData(PagingQuery, int defaultPageSize, int totalCount)` ctor at src/DM.Domain.Core/Dto/PagingData.cs:17, with per-entity page sizes pulled from user settings rather than hardcoded.
- Build output is correctly ignored — `src/DM.Web.Client/dist` (9.8 MB) is untracked and listed in src/DM.Web.Client/.gitignore:12. No committed build artifacts, no committed node_modules, no committed secrets (`.env.preview` is tracked but contains only `VITE_API_HOST=`).

## Подтвержденные находки

### [КРИТИЧНО] HYG-01 — Committed encoding corruption makes ReadableGuidHelper throw TypeInitializationException, permanently killing the notification dispatcher

**Статус: Исправлено.** Ключи транслитерации восстановлены из истории (304e618a)

**Доказательство.** src/DM.Domain.Core/Extensions/ReadableGuidHelper.cs:78-89 declares `private static readonly Dictionary<string,string> TransliterationReplacements = new() { {" ", "-"}, {"?", "a"}, {"?", "b"}, {"?", "v"}, ... }`. `file` reports the source as pure **ASCII text** and `od -c` on line 80 confirms the bytes are literal `0x3F` question marks — the Cyrillic keys were destroyed. The key `"?"` appears **33 times**. A `{k, v}` collection initializer compiles to `.Add(k, v)`, and `Dictionary.Add` throws `ArgumentException` on a duplicate key. `git show HEAD:...` proves the corruption is committed (introduced by 9fa0535c "refactor: restructure project to Clean Architecture naming"), not a working-tree artifact. The exploding path is live: `Transliterate` is reached from `EncodeToReadable(guid, readableText)` (line 20-27), which is called with a non-null title from ~30 sites, e.g. src/DM.Workers.Notif

**Последствие.** The first blog/game/forum notification the dispatcher generates throws ArgumentException wrapped in TypeInitializationException. The CLR caches failed type initializers for the process lifetime, so every subsequent notification rethrows the identical exception — the worker never recovers without a restart, and a restart reproduces it immediately. Effectively 100% of title-bearing notifications are dead in production, and the failure mode (TypeInitializationException on a GUID helper) gives no hint about the actual cause.

**Исправление.** Restore the Cyrillic keys in the dictionary (а→a, б→b, в→v, … я→ya) and re-save the file as UTF-8 with BOM. Then add a guard so this class of corruption cannot recur silently: replace the collection initializer with an explicit `.ToDictionary()` over a source array, or at minimum add a unit test asserting `TransliterationReplacements.Count == 66`. Audit commit 9fa0535c for the same damage in every file it touched.

**Проверка оппонентом.** Could not refute — verified at byte level and at runtime. (1) `git cat-file blob HEAD:src/DM.Domain.Core/Extensions/ReadableGuidHelper.cs` piped through `od -tx1` and filtered for bytes >= 0x80 returns ZERO non-ASCII bytes: the Cyrillic keys are destroyed in the committed blob, not a checkout artifact. Lines 78-89 contain 33 literal `{"?", ...}` entries. (2) Decisive runtime repro: I extracted the committed blob verbatim into a net8.0 console project and executed it. `Guid.NewGuid().EncodeToReadable("Blog Title")` throws `System.TypeInitializationException` with inner `System.ArgumentException: An item with the same key has already been added. Key: ?`, and a second call rethrows identically (failed cctor cached), exactly as the finding predicts. (3) The path is live: 31 call sites pass a non-null title (grep for `EncodeToReadable(` minus the no-arg overload), all in src/DM.Workers.Notifi

### [ВЫСОКАЯ] HYG-02 — Two controllers register the identical route GET/PATCH v1/moderation/users/{x}/profile — an endpoint the SPA calls on every moderator profile view

**Статус: Исправлено.** PATCH-дубль тоже удален вместе с реализацией сервиса; было 500 AmbiguousMatchException без аутентификации, стало 401 (c91d8d4d)

**Доказательство.** src/DM.Web.API/Features/Moderation/Profiles/ProfileController.cs:25 `[Route("v1/moderation/users")]` + :53 `[HttpGet("{username}/profile")]`. src/DM.Web.API/Features/Moderation/Profiles/ModerationProfileController.cs:24 `[Route("v1/moderation/users")]` + :52 `[HttpGet("{login}/profile")]`. Both are `[ApiController]`, both `[RequireRole(UserRole.Moderator)]`, neither is excluded in DM.Web.API.csproj. The PATCH verb collides three ways: ProfileController.cs:75 `[HttpPatch("{username}/profile")]` vs ModerationController.cs:28+124 `[Route("v1/moderation")]` + `[HttpPatch("users/{login}/profile")]`. The route is live from the UI: src/DM.Web.Client/src/shared/api/moderationApi.ts:199 `Api.get(\`moderation/users/${username}/profile\`)`, reached via src/DM.Web.Client/src/entities/user/lib/useModeratedProfile.ts:33 which src/DM.Web.Client/src/pages/profile/ProfilePage.vue:399 calls on mount. `gre

**Последствие.** `{username}/profile` and `{login}/profile` are template segments of identical precedence, so ASP.NET Core endpoint selection has two equally-good candidates and throws AmbiguousMatchException → HTTP 500. Note this is NOT the case CLAUDE.md waves off ("literal > template") — there is no literal here to break the tie. Every moderator opening any user profile gets a 500 on the moderation panel fetch, and the duplicate PATCH means moderating a profile is equally broken.

**Исправление.** Delete ModerationProfileController + ModerationProfileApiService + IModerationProfileApiService + ModerationProfileDto entirely (see HYG-09 — it is the redundant twin), and delete the duplicated `ModerateUserProfileLegacy` PATCH at ModerationController.cs:124-131. Then add a startup smoke test that enumerates `EndpointDataSource.Endpoints` and asserts no two route patterns are equal — this class of bug is cheap to prevent and invisible to the compiler.

**Проверка оппонентом.** Could not refute — I reproduced it empirically. Every cited line is accurate: ProfileController.cs:25 `[Route("v1/moderation/users")]` + :53 `[HttpGet("{username}/profile")]` + :75 `[HttpPatch("{username}/profile")]`; ModerationProfileController.cs:24 same `[Route]` + :52 `[HttpGet("{login}/profile")]`; ModerationController.cs:28 `[Route("v1/moderation")]` + :124 `[HttpPatch("users/{login}/profile")]`. All three classes are `public`, none is excluded (DM.Web.API.csproj has no Compile Remove), no application-model convention exists (Startup.cs:350 is a bare `AddMvc` with only a model-binder tweak), and `RequireRoleAttribute` is a TypeFilter/IAuthorizationFilter (RequireRoleAttribute.cs:13,25), not an IActionConstraint, so it cannot break the tie.

I built a probe host that does `AddControllers().AddApplicationPart(typeof(ProfileController).Assembly)` + `MapControllers()` against the real 

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] HYG-03 — ~118 user-facing Russian string literals destroyed by the same encoding loss — every email subject and bot notification label is question marks

**Статус: Исправлено.** ~80 строк писем и бота написаны заново (304e618a)

**Доказательство.** src/DM.Workers.NotificationDispatcher/Implementation/Email/NotificationEmailSender.cs:27-33 — the `EventTypeSubjects` dictionary that supplies outbound **email subject lines**: `[EventType.StatusGameActive] = "???? ????????????"`, `[EventType.GameClosureWarning] = "?????????????? ? ???????? ????"`. 55 lines mangled. src/DM.Workers.NotificationDispatcher/Implementation/Bot/NotificationBotSender.cs:33-36+ — 54 lines, same pattern. src/DM.Infrastructure.Core/Parsing/BbParserProvider.cs:30 emits `data-swaptext="?????? ?????????? ???????">???????? ?????????? ???????` into the rendered NSFW spoiler, and :75/:83 render the private-block header as `??????????: {value}` — this is server-rendered BBCode shown to every reader. src/DM.Workers.NotificationDispatcher/Implementation/Notifiers/Blog/BlogInvitationNotificationGenerator.cs:49 is worse than mangled — the ternary `invitationData.Type == Toke

**Последствие.** Every notification email that reaches a user has a subject line of question marks. Every Telegram/bot notification label likewise. Every NSFW spoiler and privileged `[mod]`/private block on the site renders its header as question marks. The blog-invitation role label lost its distinction between assistant and reader entirely. None of this is caught by the compiler or by any test, and it has been committed since the restructure.

**Исправление.** Recover the original strings from the pre-9fa0535c history (`git show 9fa0535c^:<path>`) for all five files, re-save as UTF-8 with BOM, and add a CI grep gate that fails the build on `\?\?\?` inside a double-quoted literal in .cs — the project already treats warnings as errors, so it clearly accepts mechanical enforcement.

**Проверка оппонентом.** The encoding damage itself is real and byte-verified, so the finding survives — but its impact narrative is roughly half wrong, which drops it from high to medium.

WHAT IS REAL. `od -c` on src/DM.Workers.NotificationDispatcher/Implementation/Email/NotificationEmailSender.cs line 30 shows literal 0x3F bytes ("????"), while line 65 in the same file shows valid UTF-8 ("Блог открыт") — so this is genuine mixed-encoding damage in the file, not a reader artifact. Scope is 113 lines across exactly 4 files (the finding's "~118" is close): NotificationEmailSender.cs (55), NotificationBotSender.cs (54), BbParserProvider.cs (3), BlogInvitationNotificationGenerator.cs (1); the rest of src is clean, confirming file-scoped damage. It is a true regression, not placeholder text: `git show 9fa0535c^:src/DM.Services.Core/Parsing/BbParserProvider.cs` held `data-swaptext="Скрыть шокирующий контент"` and `П

### [КРИТИЧНО] HYG-04 — A 5,422-line test-data seeder is the single largest file in the backend and ships inside the production API assembly behind anonymous endpoints

**Статус: Исправлено.** Сид вынесен в отдельный проект DM.Tools.Seeder, четыре dev-эндпоинта (самоназначение роли, дамп всех юзеров, два сида) удалены из API вместе с реализацией. Самый большой файл API теперь Startup.cs на 591 строку вместо 5406 (7887c089)

**Доказательство.** src/DM.Web.API/Features/Moderation/Profiles/ModerationApiService.cs is 5,422 lines / 338 KB — 4.5x the next largest non-migration file (GameModels.cs, 1,470) and ~3.2% of the entire 167k-line backend. Of its 31 methods, 28 are seeders: `SeedComprehensiveData` (:548), `CreateGames` (:2420, ~1,050 lines), `SeedCharactersAndPostsForGames` (:3470, ~450 lines), `CreateReviews` (:4154, ~680 lines), `CreatePolls` (:4927). It contains inline Russian prose fixtures (:3324 NPC dialogue, :4191 testimonial text, :4801 review text, :5268 poll questions) and writes directly to DmDbContext + DmMongoClient + MinIO (`PutSeedObjectAsync` :298), bypassing every repository and domain service in the architecture. The exposing endpoints have **no** `[AuthenticationRequired]` and **no** `[RequireRole]` — ModerationController.cs:152 `POST /v1/moderation/seed` and :189 `POST /v1/moderation/seed/comprehensive` ar

**Последствие.** Two problems. (a) Architectural: the modular monolith's entire layering — repositories, domain services, intention resolvers, validators — is bypassed by the biggest file in the API project, so the seeder silently drifts from real write paths and can produce states the app cannot produce. It also inflates the production image and forces DM.Web.API to reference persistence entities and Mongo/MinIO clients it otherwise would not. (b) Operational: a single misconfigured `ASPNETCORE_ENVIRONMENT` in any deployment turns an unauthenticated POST into a full database mutation and an unauthenticated GET into a user dump. Environment string equality is the only thing standing between the internet and that.

**Исправление.** Move the whole seeder out of DM.Web.API into a separate `DM.Tools.Seeder` console project referenced only by docker-compose/local tooling, and delete IModerationApiService's seed members. If an HTTP trigger is genuinely wanted for the dev loop, keep it in a `#if DEBUG`-guarded controller AND add `[RequireRole(UserRole.Admin)]` so environment misconfiguration is not the sole control. Split the remaining seed logic by aggregate (games / forum / blogs / community) so no single file exceeds ~500 lines.

**Проверка оппонентом.** Every cited fact checks out and no mitigating control exists. ModerationApiService.cs is 5,422 lines / 338,057 bytes (largest non-migration .cs; next is GameRepository.cs at 45,794), and the cited offsets are exact: PutSeedObjectAsync :298 calls _s3Client.PutObjectAsync directly, SeedComprehensiveData :548, CreateGames :2420, SeedCharactersAndPostsForGames :3470, CreateReviews :4154, CreatePolls :4927; only SetRole :317, GetAllUsers :345 and ModerateUserProfile :364 are non-seed. ModerationController.cs :96/:152/:189 genuinely carry no [AuthenticationRequired] or [RequireRole] while :124 in the same file does carry [RequireRole(SeniorModerator)], so the omission is not a project-wide convention. I tried and failed to find a compensating gate: Startup.cs has no AddAuthorization/fallback policy and AddMvc (:350) registers no auth filter; AuthenticationMiddleware assigns Identity.Guest() to

### [СРЕДНЯЯ] HYG-05 — Four background services exist as duplicate files; the dead WarmupService copy is the *richer* one and the copies have already drifted

**Статус: Исправлено.** То же, что CR-04 (c91d8d4d)

The WarmupService pair is the dangerous case: the version that got the popularity-score warm-up improvement is the one that never runs, so game/blog sidebar popularity is cold on every cold start until PopularityScoreService's first tick. This is the canonical copy-drift failure and it has already fired once — the next person who "fixes" a cleanup service has a 50% chance of editing the dead copy.

**Исправление.** Delete src/DM.Web.API/Shared/BackgroundServices/ (3 files) and src/DM.Web.API/Warmup/ (1 file) after porting `WarmupPopularityScores` into src/DM.Web.API/HostedServices/WarmupService.cs. Adopt one home for hosted services and enforce it — a duplicate-basename check over src/**/*.cs in CI would have caught this the day it landed.

**Проверка оппонентом.** I tried to refute this and could not — every factual claim in the evidence reproduces exactly.

VERIFIED FACTS:
1. 2. `diff` confirms SessionCleanupService and TokenCleanupService differ by exactly one line (the `namespace` at line 11); PendingRegistrationCleanupService differs by the namespace plus "username" vs "login" at line 19.
